HarmoWAM: Harmonizing Generalizable and Precise Manipulation via Adaptive World Action Models
Authors: Qiuxuan Feng, Jiale Yu, Jiaming Liu, Yueru Jia, Zhuangzhe Wu, Hao Chen, Zezhong Qian, Shuo Gu, Peng Jia, Siwei Ma, Shanghang Zhang
First: 2026-05-11T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:59:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot control by modeling physical dynamics. Current WAMs generally follow two paradigms: the "Imagine-then-Execute" approach, which uses video prediction to infer actions via inverse dynamics, and the "Joint Modeling" approach, which jointly models actions and video representations. Based on systematic experiments, we observe a fundamental trade-off between these paradigms: the former explicitly leverages world models for generalizable transit but lacks interaction precision, whereas the latter enables fine-grained, temporally coherent action generation but is constrained by the exploration space of the training distribution. Motivated by these findings, we propose HarmoWAM, an end-to-end WAM that fully leverages a world model to unify predictive and reactive control, enabling both generalizable transit and precise manipulation. Specifically, the world model provides spatio-temporal physical priors that condition two complementary action experts: a predictive expert that leverages latent dynamics for iterative action generation, and a reactive expert that directly infers actions from predicted visual evolution. To enable adaptive coordination, a Process-Adaptive Gating Mechanism is proposed to automatically determine the timing and location of switching between them. This allows the world model to drive the reactive expert to expand the exploration space and the predictive expert to perform precise interactions across different stages of a task. For evaluation, we construct three training-unseen test environments across six real-world robotic tasks, covering variations in background, position, and object semantics. Notably, HarmoWAM achieves strong zero-shot generalization across these scenarios, significantly outperforming prior state-of-the-art VLA models and WAMs by margins of 33% and 29%, respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HarmoWAM：通过自适应世界动作模型协调通用与精准操作
世界动作模型（WAMs）已成为通过建模物理动态进行机器人控制的有前景范式。当前WAMs主要遵循两种范式："想象后执行"方法，利用视频预测通过逆动力学推断动作；以及"联合建模"方法，联合建模动作与视频表示。基于系统性实验，我们观察到这两种范式存在根本性权衡：前者明确利用世界模型实现可泛化的过渡但缺乏交互精度，后者则支持细粒度、时间一致的动作生成，但受限于训练分布的探索空间。受此启发，我们提出HarmoWAM，一种端到端WAM，充分利用世界模型统一预测与反应控制，实现通用过渡与精准操作。具体而言，世界模型提供时空物理先验，条件化两个互补的动作专家：一个预测专家利用潜在动态进行迭代动作生成，一个反应专家直接从预测的视觉演化中推断动作。为实现自适应协调，提出过程自适应门控机制，自动确定二者切换的时机与位置。这使世界模型驱动反应专家扩展探索空间，同时使预测专家在任务不同阶段执行精准交互。为评估，我们在六个真实机器人任务中构建三个训练未见的测试环境，涵盖背景、位置和物体语义的变异。值得注意的是，HarmoWAM在这些场景中实现强零样本泛化，分别显著优于现有最先进VLA模型和WAMs 33%和29%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observed trade-off between generalizable and precise manipulation in existing World Action Models, this paper proposes HarmoWAM, an end-to-end framework that unifies predictive and reactive control via a world model. It employs two complementary action experts—a predictive expert using latent dynamics for iterative action generation and a reactive expert inferring actions from predicted visual evolution—conditioned by spatio-temporal physical priors. A Process-Adaptive Gating Mechanism dynamically switches between these experts based on task stage, enabling both broad generalization and fine-grained interaction. Evaluated across six real-world robotic tasks with unseen environments, HarmoWAM demonstrates strong zero-shot generalization, outperforming prior VLA models and WAMs by 33% and 29% respectively.
针对现有世界动作模型在通用性与精确操作间存在固有权衡的问题，本文提出HarmoWAM框架，通过自适应协调机制统一二者能力。该框架利用世界模型为两个互补的动作专家提供时空物理先验——一个基于潜动态迭代生成动作，另一个直接从预测视觉演化推断动作，并通过过程自适应门控机制动态切换专家。在六个真实机器人任务的未见环境测试中，HarmoWAM展现出强大的零样本泛化能力，相较现有最先进VLA模型和WAM分别提升33%和29%。
ELF: Embedded Language Flows
Authors: Keya Hu, Linlu Qiu, Yiyang Lu, Hanhong Zhao, Tianhong Li, Yoon Kim, Jacob Andreas, Kaiming He
First: 2026-05-11T17:59:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:59:29+00:00
Comments: Tech Report. Project webpage: https://github.com/lillian039/ELF
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Diffusion and flow-based models have become the de facto approaches for generating continuous data, e.g., in domains such as images and videos. Their success has attracted growing interest in applying them to language modeling. Unlike their image-domain counterparts, today's leading diffusion language models (DLMs) primarily operate over discrete tokens. In this paper, we show that continuous DLMs can be made effective with minimal adaptation to the discrete domain. We propose Embedded Language Flows (ELF), a class of diffusion models in continuous embedding space based on continuous-time Flow Matching. Unlike existing DLMs, ELF predominantly stays within the continuous embedding space until the final time step, where it maps to discrete tokens using a shared-weight network. This formulation makes it straightforward to adapt established techniques from image-domain diffusion models, e.g., classifier-free guidance (CFG). Experiments show that ELF substantially outperforms leading discrete and continuous DLMs, achieving better generation quality with fewer sampling steps. These results suggest that ELF offers a promising path toward effective continuous DLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ELF：嵌入式语言流
扩散模型和基于流的模型已成为生成连续数据（如图像和视频领域）的主流方法。它们的成功吸引了人们将其应用于语言建模的兴趣。与图像领域的对应模型不同，当前主流的扩散语言模型（DLMs）主要在离散token上操作。本文表明，通过最小化对离散域的调整，连续DLMs可以变得有效。我们提出了嵌入式语言流（ELF），这是一种基于连续时间流匹配的连续嵌入空间中的扩散模型。与现有DLMs不同，ELF在最终时间步之前主要停留在连续嵌入空间中，最终通过共享权重网络映射到离散token。这种设计使得可以轻松适配图像领域扩散模型中已有的技术，例如无分类器引导（CFG）。实验表明，ELF显著优于当前主流的离散和连续DLMs，在更少采样步数下实现更优的生成质量。这些结果表明，ELF为构建有效的连续DLMs提供了一条有前景的路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the success of diffusion and flow-based models in image and video generation, this paper explores their application to language modeling, where current leading models operate on discrete tokens. The authors propose Embedded Language Flows (ELF), a diffusion model that operates in continuous embedding space using continuous-time Flow Matching, only mapping to discrete tokens at the final step via a shared-weight network. This design enables straightforward adaptation of image-domain techniques like classifier-free guidance. Experiments demonstrate that ELF significantly outperforms both discrete and continuous language models, producing higher-quality outputs with fewer sampling steps.
鉴于扩散和流式模型在图像与视频生成中的成功，本文探索其在语言建模中的应用，解决当前主流扩散语言模型依赖离散token的问题。作者提出Embedded Language Flows（ELF），一种基于连续时间流匹配的扩散模型，主要在连续嵌入空间中运行，仅在最后一步通过共享权重网络映射到离散token。该设计便于迁移图像领域技术如无分类器引导。实验表明，ELF显著优于现有离散与连续DLM基线，在更少采样步数下生成更高质量结果。
Power Reinforcement Post-Training of Text-to-Image Models with Super-Linear Advantage Shaping
Authors: Haoyuan Sun, Jing Wang, Yuxin Song, Yu Lu, Bo Fang, Yifu Luo, Jun Yin, Pengyu Zeng, Miao Zhang, Tiantian Zhang, Xueqian Wang, Shijian Lu
First: 2026-05-11T17:59:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:59:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recently, post-training methods based on reinforcement learning, with a particular focus on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), have emerged as the robust paradigm for further advancement of text-to-image (T2I) models. However, these methods are often prone to reward hacking, wherein models exploit biases in imperfect reward functions rather than yielding genuine performance gains. In this work, we identify that normalization could lead to miscalibration and directly removing the prompt-level standard deviation term yields an optimal policy ascent direction that is linear in the advantage but still limits the separation of genuine signals from noise. To mitigate the above issues, we propose Super-Linear Advantage Shaping (SLAS) by revisiting the functional update from an information geometry perspective. By extending the Fisher-Rao information metric with advantage-dependent weighting, SLAS introduces a non-linear geometric structure that reshapes the local policy space. This design relaxes constraints along high-advantage directions to amplify informative updates, while tightening those in low-advantage regions to suppress illusory gradients. In addition, batch-level normalization is applied to stabilize training under varying reward scales. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that SLAS consistently surpasses the DanceGRPO baseline across multiple backbones and benchmarks. In particular, it yields faster training dynamics, improved out-of-domain performance on GenEval and UniGenBench++, and enhanced robustness to model scaling, while mitigating reward hacking and preserving semantic and compositional fidelity in generations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于超线性优势塑造的文本到图像模型训练后强化学习
近期，以组相对策略优化（GRPO）为代表的基于强化学习的训练后方法已成为文本到图像（T2I）模型进一步发展的稳健范式。然而，这些方法常易出现奖励黑客问题，即模型利用不完美奖励函数中的偏差而非真正提升性能。本文发现，标准化可能导致校准错误，直接移除提示级标准差项虽可获得线性于优势的最优策略上升方向，但仍难以区分真实信号与噪声。为缓解上述问题，我们从信息几何视角重新审视函数更新，提出超线性优势塑造（SLAS）方法。通过引入依赖优势的Fisher-Rao信息度量加权，SLAS构建非线性几何结构，重塑局部策略空间。该设计在高优势方向放松约束以放大信息更新，在低优势区域收紧约束以抑制虚假梯度。此外，采用批级标准化以稳定不同奖励尺度下的训练。大量评估表明，SLAS在多个骨干网络和基准测试中持续超越DanceGRPO基线。尤其在训练动态加速、GenEval和UniGenBench++的跨域性能提升、模型扩展鲁棒性增强方面表现突出，同时缓解奖励黑客问题并保持生成结果的语义与组合保真度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the reward hacking issue in reinforcement learning-based post-training of text-to-image models, this work introduces Super-Linear Advantage Shaping (SLAS) to improve policy optimization by reshaping the local policy space using a non-linear geometric structure derived from information geometry. SLAS extends the Fisher-Rao metric with advantage-dependent weighting to amplify informative updates in high-advantage directions while suppressing noise in low-advantage regions, and employs batch-level normalization to stabilize training under varying reward scales. Experimental results show SLAS consistently outperforms the DanceGRPO baseline across multiple backbones and benchmarks, achieving faster convergence, better out-of-domain performance on GenEval and UniGenBench++, and improved robustness to model scaling without compromising semantic or compositional fidelity.
为解决文本到图像模型强化学习后训练中常见的奖励欺骗问题，本文提出超线性优势塑造（SLAS）方法，通过信息几何视角重构局部策略空间，引入基于优势的非线性几何结构，强化高优势方向的有益更新并抑制低优势区域的虚假梯度，同时采用批级归一化稳定不同奖励尺度下的训练。实验表明，SLAS在多个模型架构和基准测试中持续优于DanceGRPO基线，训练收敛更快，GenEval和UniGenBench++等离域任务表现更优，对模型规模扩展更具鲁棒性，同时有效缓解奖励欺骗并保持生成内容的语义与组合一致性。
Personal Visual Context Learning in Large Multimodal Models
Authors: Zihui Xue, Ami Baid, Sangho Kim, Mi Luo, Kristen Grauman
First: 2026-05-11T17:59:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:59:17+00:00
Comments: Project website: https://vision.cs.utexas.edu/projects/PersonalVCL/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
As wearable devices like smart glasses integrate Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) into the continuous first-person visual streams of individual users, the evolution of these models into true personal assistants hinges on visual personalization: the ability to reason over visual information unique to the wearer. We formalize this capability as Personal Visual Context Learning (Personal VCL), the prompt-time capability of using user-specific visual context to resolve personalized queries. To systematically evaluate this, we present Personal-VCL-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark capturing the personal visual world across persons, objects, and behaviors. Our analysis of frontier LMMs identifies a profound context utilization gap, revealing that the mechanisms for leveraging visual evidence, as well as aggregating multiple visual observations, remain critically understudied. Motivated by these findings, we propose the Agentic Context Bank, a strong inference-time baseline that structures a user's visual context into a self-refining memory bank and employs query-adaptive evidence selection. Our baseline approach consistently improves over standard context prompting regimes across tasks and evaluated backbones, demonstrating a practical path towards future personalized LMMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型多模态模型中的个人视觉上下文学习
随着智能眼镜等可穿戴设备将大型多模态模型（LMMs）集成到用户持续的第一人称视觉流中，这些模型演变为真正个人助手的关键在于视觉个性化：即能够推理与佩戴者相关的独特视觉信息。我们将此能力形式化为个人视觉上下文学习（Personal VCL），即在提示时利用用户特定的视觉上下文以解决个性化查询的能力。为系统性评估该能力，我们提出了Personal-VCL-Bench，一个全面的基准测试，涵盖不同人物、物体和行为的个人视觉世界。我们对前沿LMMs的分析揭示了显著的上下文利用差距，表明利用视觉证据以及聚合多个视觉观察的机制仍严重缺乏研究。受此启发，我们提出了代理上下文库（Agentic Context Bank），一种强大的推理时基线方法，将用户的视觉上下文结构化为自优化记忆库，并采用查询自适应证据选择。我们的基线方法在各类任务和评估骨干模型上均优于标准上下文提示范式，展示了未来个性化LMMs的可行路径。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for large multimodal models to adapt to individual users' visual environments, this work introduces Personal Visual Context Learning (Personal VCL) as a framework for leveraging user-specific visual context at prompt time. To evaluate this, the authors construct Personal-VCL-Bench, a benchmark covering diverse personal visual scenarios. Their analysis reveals that current frontier models poorly utilize visual evidence and aggregate multiple visual cues. In response, they propose the Agentic Context Bank, a memory-based approach that refines user context dynamically and selects relevant visual evidence adaptively during inference. Experiments show this method consistently outperforms standard prompting strategies across multiple models and tasks, demonstrating a viable path toward personalized LMMs.
为实现LMMs对个体用户视觉环境的适应以提供真正个性化服务，本文提出个人视觉上下文学习（Personal VCL）作为提示时利用用户专属视觉上下文进行推理的能力。为评估该能力，作者构建了涵盖多个人、物体与行为的Personal-VCL-Bench基准。分析发现当前前沿LMMs在利用视觉证据和聚合多视觉观察方面存在严重不足。为此，作者提出Agentic Context Bank基线方法，将用户视觉上下文结构化为自优化记忆库，并根据查询动态选择证据。该方法在多个任务和模型架构上均显著优于标准提示方式，为未来个性化LMMs提供了可行路径。
LiLAW: Lightweight Learnable Adaptive Weighting to Learn Sample Difficulty & Improve Noisy Training
Authors: Abhishek Moturu, Muhammad Muzammil, Anna Goldenberg, Babak Taati
First: 2025-09-25T06:13:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:58:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training deep neural networks with noise and data heterogeneity is a major challenge. We introduce Lightweight Learnable Adaptive Weighting (LiLAW), a method that dynamically adjusts the loss weight of each training sample based on its evolving difficulty, categorized as easy, moderate, and hard, using only three global learnable scalar parameters. LiLAW learns to adaptively prioritize samples by updating these parameters with a single gradient descent step on a validation mini-batch after each training mini-batch, without requiring a clean, unbiased validation set. Experiments across general and medical imaging datasets, several noise types and levels, loss functions, and architectures with and without pretraining, including linear probing and full fine-tuning, show that LiLAW consistently improves accuracy and AUROC, especially in higher-noise settings, without requiring excessive tuning. We also obtain state-of-the-art results incorporating synthetic and augmented data from SynPAIN, GAITGen, ECG5000, and improved fairness on the Adult dataset. LiLAW is lightweight, practical, and computationally efficient, making it an effective, scalable approach to boost generalization and robustness across diverse deep learning training setups, especially in resource-constrained settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LiLAW：轻量级可学习自适应加权以学习样本难度并提升噪声训练效果
在噪声和数据异质性条件下训练深度神经网络是一个重大挑战。我们提出了轻量级可学习自适应加权（LiLAW）方法，该方法仅使用三个全局可学习标量参数，根据样本随训练动态变化的难度（易、中、难）动态调整每个训练样本的损失权重。LiLAW通过在每个训练小批量后，仅对验证小批量执行一次梯度下降更新参数，实现自适应样本优先级调整，无需干净无偏的验证集。在通用和医学影像数据集、多种噪声类型与强度、不同损失函数及预训练与无预训练架构（包括线性探针和全微调）的实验中，LiLAW持续提升准确率和AUROC，尤其在高噪声环境下表现更优，且无需过度调参。我们还通过结合SynPAIN、GAITGen、ECG5000等合成与增强数据，在Adult数据集上获得最先进结果并提升公平性。LiLAW轻量、实用且计算高效，是提升各类深度学习训练设置下泛化能力与鲁棒性的有效可扩展方法，尤其适用于资源受限环境。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of training deep neural networks under noisy and heterogeneous data conditions, LiLAW introduces a lightweight, learnable adaptive weighting mechanism that dynamically adjusts per-sample loss weights based on evolving difficulty levels—easy, moderate, and hard—using only three global scalar parameters. These parameters are updated via a single gradient step on a validation mini-batch after each training mini-batch, without needing a clean validation set. Experiments across diverse datasets, noise types, loss functions, and architectures demonstrate consistent gains in accuracy and AUROC, particularly under high noise, with no excessive tuning required. LiLAW also achieves state-of-the-art performance when combined with synthetic data from SynPAIN, GAITGen, and ECG5000, and improves fairness on the Adult dataset, while remaining computationally efficient and practical for resource-constrained environments.
为应对在噪声和数据异质性环境下训练深度神经网络的挑战，LiLAW提出一种轻量级可学习机制，通过仅三个全局标量参数动态调整样本损失权重，依据样本难度（易、中、难）进行自适应优先级排序。该方法在每个训练小批量后，仅用一个梯度步骤更新参数，无需干净无偏的验证集。实验表明，该方法在多种数据集、噪声类型、损失函数和架构下均稳定提升准确率与AUROC，尤其在高噪声环境下效果显著，并在结合SynPAIN、GAITGen、ECG5000合成数据时达到最先进水平，同时在Adult数据集上提升公平性。
Variational Inference for Lévy Process-Driven SDEs via Neural Tilting
Authors: Yaman Kindap, Manfred Opper, Benjamin Dupuis, Umut Simsekli, Tolga Birdal
First: 2026-05-11T17:58:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:58:45+00:00
Comments: The associated project page which contains the official implementation can be found in https://circle-group.github.io/research/NeuralTilting/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Modelling extreme events and heavy-tailed phenomena is central to building reliable predictive systems in domains such as finance, climate science, and safety-critical AI. While Lévy processes provide a natural mathematical framework for capturing jumps and heavy tails, Bayesian inference for Lévy-driven stochastic differential equations (SDEs) remains intractable with existing methods: Monte Carlo approaches are rigorous but lack scalability, whereas neural variational inference methods are efficient but rely on Gaussian assumptions that fail to capture discontinuities. We address this tension by introducing a neural exponential tilting framework for variational inference in Lévy-driven SDEs. Our approach constructs a flexible variational family by exponentially reweighting the Lévy measure using neural networks. This parametrization preserves the jump structure of the underlying process while remaining computationally tractable. To enable efficient inference, we develop a quadratic neural parametrization that yields closed-form normalization of the tilted measure, a conditional Gaussian representation for stable processes that facilitates simulation, and symmetry-aware Monte Carlo estimators for scalable optimization. Empirically, we demonstrate that the method accurately captures jump dynamics and yields reliable posterior inference in regimes where Gaussian-based variational approaches fail, on both synthetic and real-world datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过神经倾斜进行Lévy过程驱动SDE的变分推断
建模极端事件和重尾现象是构建金融、气候科学和安全关键型AI等领域可靠预测系统的核心。虽然Lévy过程为捕捉跳跃和重尾提供了自然的数学框架，但现有方法对Lévy驱动随机微分方程（SDE）的贝叶斯推断仍不可行：蒙特卡洛方法严谨但缺乏可扩展性，而神经变分推断方法高效但依赖高斯假设，无法捕捉不连续性。我们通过引入神经指数倾斜框架解决这一矛盾，用于Lévy驱动SDE的变分推断。该方法通过神经网络指数重加权Lévy测度，构建灵活的变分族，既保留了底层过程的跳跃结构，又保持计算可行性。为实现高效推断，我们开发了二次神经参数化，可获得倾斜测度的闭式归一化，为稳定过程提供条件高斯表示以支持模拟，并设计对称感知的蒙特卡洛估计器以实现可扩展优化。实证表明，该方法在合成和真实数据集上均能准确捕捉跳跃动态，并在高斯变分方法失效的场景中提供可靠的后验推断。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of modeling extreme events and heavy-tailed phenomena in domains like finance and climate science, this work introduces a neural exponential tilting framework for variational inference in Lévy-driven stochastic differential equations. The method constructs a flexible variational family by reweighting the Lévy measure via neural networks, preserving jump structure while enabling tractable computation. It further employs a quadratic neural parametrization to achieve closed-form normalization, conditional Gaussian representations for stable processes, and symmetry-aware Monte Carlo estimators for scalable optimization. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that the approach accurately captures jump dynamics and produces reliable posterior inference where Gaussian-based methods fail.
为建模金融与气候科学等领域中的极端事件和重尾现象，本文提出一种基于神经指数倾斜的变分推断框架，用于Lévy驱动随机微分方程。该方法通过神经网络重加权Lévy测度，构建灵活的变分族，保留跳跃结构同时保证计算可行性。引入二次神经参数化实现测度归一化的闭式解，为稳定过程提供条件高斯表示，并设计对称蒙特卡洛估计器以支持高效优化。实验表明，该方法在合成与真实数据上均能准确捕捉跳跃动态，且在高斯变分方法失效的场景下仍能提供可靠的后验推断。
DECO: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts with Dense-Comparable Performance on End-Side Devices
Authors: Chenyang Song, Weilin Zhao, Xu Han, Chaojun Xiao, Yingfa Chen, Zhiyuan Liu
First: 2026-05-11T17:58:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:58:28+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 11 figures, 11 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) scales model capacity without proportionally increasing computation, its massive total parameter footprint creates significant storage and memory-access bottlenecks, which hinder efficient end-side deployment that simultaneously requires high performance, low computational cost, and small storage overhead. To achieve these properties, we present DECO, a sparse MoE architecture designed to match the performance of dense Transformers under identical total parameter budgets and training tokens. DECO utilizes the differentiable and flexible ReLU-based routing enhanced by learnable expert-wise scaling, which adaptively balances the contributions of routed and shared experts. Furthermore, we introduce NormSiLU, an activation function that normalizes inputs prior to SiLU operators, producing a more stable trend of routed-expert activation ratio and a higher intrinsic sparsity level. We also identify an empirical advantage in using non-gated MLP experts with ReLU-based routing, indicating the possibility of MoE architecture simplification. Experiments demonstrate that DECO, activating only 20% of experts, matches dense performance and outperforms established MoE baselines. Our specialized acceleration kernel delivers a 3.00$\times$ speedup on real hardware compared with dense inference. Codes and checkpoints will be released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DECO：在端侧设备上实现稀疏专家混合且性能媲美稠密模型
尽管专家混合（MoE）能在不按比例增加计算量的情况下扩展模型容量，但其庞大的总参数量导致显著的存储和内存访问瓶颈，阻碍了端侧高效部署——该部署需同时满足高性能、低计算成本和小存储开销。为实现这些特性，我们提出DECO，一种稀疏MoE架构，在相同总参数预算和训练token数下，其性能可媲美稠密Transformer。DECO采用可微且灵活的基于ReLU的路由机制，并结合可学习的专家级缩放，自适应平衡路由与共享专家的贡献。此外，我们引入NormSiLU激活函数，其在SiLU操作前对输入进行归一化，使路由专家激活比例更稳定，且内在稀疏性更高。我们还发现，使用非门控MLP专家配合基于ReLU的路由具有实证优势，表明MoE架构可简化。实验表明，仅激活20%专家的DECO即可匹配稠密模型性能，并优于现有MoE基线。我们的专用加速内核在真实硬件上相比稠密推理实现3.00倍加速。代码与检查点将公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the storage and memory bottlenecks of dense MoE models despite their scalability, DECO introduces a sparse MoE architecture that matches dense Transformer performance under identical parameter budgets. It employs differentiable ReLU-based routing with learnable expert-wise scaling to adaptively balance expert contributions, and introduces NormSiLU to stabilize activation ratios and enhance sparsity. Experiments show that DECO, activating only 20% of experts, achieves performance comparable to dense models while outperforming existing MoE baselines and delivering 3.00x faster inference on real hardware.

Quantifying Concentration Phenomena of Mean-Field Transformers in the Low-Temperature Regime
Authors: Albert Alcalde, Leon Bungert, Konstantin Riedl, Tim Roith
First: 2026-05-11T17:58:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:58:14+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformers with self-attention modules as their core components have become an integral architecture in modern large language and foundation models. In this paper, we study the evolution of tokens in deep encoder-only transformers at inference time which is described in the large-token limit by a mean-field continuity equation. Leveraging ideas from the convergence analysis of interacting multi-particle systems, with particles corresponding to tokens, we prove that the token distribution rapidly concentrates onto the push-forward of the initial distribution under a projection map induced by the key, query, and value matrices, and remains metastable for moderate times. Specifically, we show that the Wasserstein distance of the two distributions scales like $\sqrt{{\log(β+1)}/β}\exp(Ct)+\exp(-ct)$ in terms of the temperature parameter $β^{-1}\to 0$ and inference time $t\geq 0$. For the proof, we establish Lyapunov-type estimates for the zero-temperature equation, identify its limit as $t\to\infty$, and employ a stability estimate in Wasserstein space together with a quantitative Laplace principle to couple the two equations. Our result implies that for time scales of order $\logβ$ the token distribution concentrates at the identified limiting distribution. Numerical experiments confirm this and, beyond that, complement our theory by showing that for finite $β$ and large $t$ the dynamics enter a different terminal phase, dominated by the spectrum of the value matrix.
中文标题/摘要
标题：低温条件下均场变换器中集中现象的量化研究
以自注意力模块为核心组件的变换器已成为现代大型语言和基础模型的重要架构。本文研究了在推理过程中深度仅编码器变换器中令牌的演化，其在大令牌极限下由均场连续方程描述。借鉴相互作用多粒子系统的收敛性分析思想（粒子对应令牌），我们证明令牌分布迅速集中于由键、查询和值矩阵诱导的投影映射下初始分布的推前分布，并在中等时间内保持亚稳态。具体而言，我们表明两分布的Wasserstein距离随温度参数β⁻¹→0和推理时间t≥0的尺度为√[log(β+1)/β]·exp(Ct)+exp(-ct)。证明中，我们为零温方程建立了Lyapunov型估计，确定其t→∞极限，并结合Wasserstein空间中的稳定性估计与定量Laplace原理，将两个方程耦合。结果表明，在时间尺度为logβ时，令牌分布集中于所识别的极限分布。数值实验验证了该结论，并进一步表明，对于有限β和大时间t，动力学进入由值矩阵谱主导的不同终态。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding token concentration behavior in deep transformers under low-temperature inference, this paper analyzes the mean-field dynamics of token distributions using a continuity equation derived from self-attention mechanisms. By modeling tokens as interacting particles and applying tools from multi-particle system convergence theory, the authors prove that token distributions rapidly concentrate onto a projection of the initial distribution induced by attention matrices, remaining metastable for moderate times. Their analysis shows that the Wasserstein distance between the evolving distribution and the limiting one scales as sqrt(log(β+1)/β) * exp(Ct) + exp(-ct) as temperature β^{-1} → 0 and time t ≥ 0, with concentration occurring on time scales of order log β. Numerical experiments validate this theoretical prediction and reveal that for finite β and large t, the system enters a terminal phase governed by the value matrix’s spectrum.
为理解深度Transformer在推理过程中令牌分布的集中现象，本文在大令牌极限下，利用平均场连续方程分析仅编码器结构的模型中令牌分布的演化。通过将令牌建模为相互作用粒子，并借鉴多粒子系统收敛分析方法，作者证明令牌分布会快速集中于由注意力矩阵诱导的投影映射下初始分布的推前分布，并在中等时间内保持亚稳态。理论结果表明，当温度β趋于0且时间t增加时，Wasserstein距离的尺度为sqrt(log(β+1)/β) * exp(Ct) + exp(-ct)，意味着在log β量级的时间尺度上分布集中。数值实验验证了该集中现象，并进一步表明，对于有限β和较大t，系统进入由值矩阵谱主导的终端相态。
Evaluating the False Trust engendered by LLM Explanations
Authors: Vardhan Palod, Upasana Biswas, Subbarao Kambhampati
First: 2026-05-11T17:58:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:58:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) and Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) are increasingly used for critical tasks, yet they provide no guarantees about the correctness of their solutions. Users must decide whether to trust the model's answer, aided by reasoning traces, their summaries, or post-hoc generated explanations. These reasoning traces, despite evidence that they are neither faithful representations of the model's computations nor necessarily semantically meaningful, are often interpreted as provenance explanations. It is unclear whether explanations or reasoning traces help users identify when the AI is incorrect, or whether they simply persuade users to trust the AI regardless. In this paper, we take a user-centered approach and develop an evaluation protocol to study how different explanation types affect users' ability to judge the correctness of AI-generated answers and engender false trust in the users. We conduct a between-subject user study, simulating a setting where users do not have the means to verify the solution and analyze the false trust engendered by commonly used LLM explanations - reasoning traces, their summaries and post-hoc explanations. We also test a contrastive dual explanation setting where we present arguments for and against the AI's answer. We find that reasoning traces and post-hoc explanations are persuasive but not informative: they increase user acceptance of LLM predictions regardless of their correctness. In contrast, dual explanation is the only condition that genuinely improves users' ability to distinguish correct from incorrect AI outputs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估大型语言模型解释所引发的虚假信任
大型语言模型（LLMs）和大型推理模型（LRMs）日益用于关键任务，但它们无法保证其解决方案的正确性。用户需决定是否信任模型答案，常借助推理轨迹、其摘要或事后生成的解释。尽管有证据表明这些推理轨迹并非模型计算的忠实反映，也未必语义上有意义，却常被解读为溯源解释。尚不清楚这些解释或推理轨迹是否帮助用户识别AI错误，或仅使用户盲目信任AI。本文采用以用户为中心的方法，开发评估协议，研究不同解释类型如何影响用户判断AI答案正确性及引发虚假信任的能力。我们进行组间用户研究，模拟用户无法验证答案的情形，分析常用LLM解释——推理轨迹、其摘要及事后解释所引发的虚假信任。同时测试对比式双解释设置（呈现支持与反对AI答案的论据）。结果发现，推理轨迹和事后解释具有说服力但无信息性：无论答案正确与否，均提高用户对LLM预测的接受度。相比之下，双解释是唯一真正提升用户区分AI正确与错误输出能力的条件。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of users trusting incorrect LLM outputs due to misleading explanations, this study evaluates how different explanation types—reasoning traces, summaries, post-hoc explanations, and dual explanations—affect users' ability to judge AI answer correctness. Through a between-subject user study simulating unverifiable scenarios, the researchers found that reasoning traces and post-hoc explanations increase user trust without improving accuracy judgment, while dual explanations (presenting both supporting and opposing arguments) uniquely enhanced users' capacity to correctly identify correct and incorrect AI outputs.

PriorVLA: Prior-Preserving Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Xinyu Guo, Bin Xie, Wei Chai, Xianchi Deng, Tiancai Wang, Zhengxing Wu, Xingyu Chen
First: 2026-05-11T17:56:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:56:02+00:00
Comments: 32 pages. Project page: https://priorvla.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large-scale pretraining has made Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models promising foundations for generalist robot manipulation, yet adapting them to downstream tasks remains necessary. However, the common practice of full fine-tuning treats pretraining as initialization and can shift broad priors toward narrow training-distribution patterns. We propose PriorVLA, a novel framework that preserves pretrained priors and learns to leverage them for effective adaptation. PriorVLA keeps a frozen Prior Expert as a read-only prior source and trains an Adaptation Expert for downstream specialization. Expert Queries capture scene priors from the pretrained VLM and motor priors from the Prior Expert, integrating both into the Adaptation Expert to guide adaptation. Together, PriorVLA updates only 25% of the parameters updated by full fine-tuning. Across RoboTwin 2.0, LIBERO, and real-world tasks, PriorVLA achieves stronger overall performance than full fine-tuning and state-of-the-art VLA baselines, with the largest gains under out-of-distribution (OOD) and few-shot settings. PriorVLA improves over pi0.5 by 11 points on RoboTwin 2.0-Hard and achieves 99.1% average success on LIBERO. Across eight real-world tasks and two embodiments, PriorVLA reaches 81% in-distribution (ID) and 57% OOD success with standard data. With only 10 demonstrations per task, PriorVLA reaches 48% ID and 32% OOD success, surpassing pi0.5 by 24 and 22 points, respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PriorVLA：面向视觉-语言-动作模型的先验保持适配方法
大规模预训练使视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型成为通用机器人操作的有前景基础，但将其适配至下游任务仍有必要。然而，常规的全量微调将预训练视为初始化，可能使广泛先验向狭窄训练分布模式偏移。我们提出PriorVLA，一种新型框架，旨在保持预训练先验并学习有效利用它们进行适配。PriorVLA保留一个冻结的先验专家作为只读先验源，并训练一个适配专家以实现下游专业化。专家查询从预训练VLM中捕获场景先验，从先验专家中捕获运动先验，将二者整合至适配专家以引导适配。整体而言，PriorVLA仅更新全量微调所需参数的25%。在RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO及真实世界任务中，PriorVLA整体性能优于全量微调和现有SOTA VLA基线，尤其在分布外（OOD）和少样本设置下收益最大。在RoboTwin 2.0-Hard上，PriorVLA相较pi0.5提升11分，LIBERO平均成功率达99.1%。在八个真实世界任务和两种具身形式中，PriorVLA在标准数据下实现81%分布内（ID）成功率和57% OOD成功率；仅用每任务10个演示时，ID和OOD成功率分别达48%和32%，相较pi0.5分别高出24和22分。

It also demonstrates robustness in out-of-distribution and few-shot settings, reaching 57% OOD success with standard data and 32% OOD success with only 10 demonstrations per task, surpassing pi0.5 by 22 points.
为在下游任务中适应大规模视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的同时保留其广泛的预训练先验，PriorVLA提出了一种框架：冻结一个先验专家，训练一个适应专家进行专业化，同时通过专家查询整合场景与运动先验。该方法仅更新全微调的25%参数，在RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO及真实世界任务中表现优于全微调和现有基线，尤其在分布外和少样本场景下优势显著，如在RoboTwin 2.0-Hard上比pi0.5高11分，在LIBERO上平均成功率达99.1%。
Dynamic Skill Lifecycle Management for Agentic Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Junhao Shen, Teng Zhang, Xiaoyan Zhao, Hong Cheng
First: 2026-05-11T17:55:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:55:13+00:00
Comments: Implementation code is available at https://github.com/ejhshen/SLIM
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language model agents increasingly rely on external skills to solve complex tasks, where skills act as modular units that extend their capabilities beyond what parametric memory alone supports. Existing methods assume external skills either accumulate as persistent guidance or internalized into the policy, eventually leading to zero-skill inference. We argue this assumption is overly restrictive, since with limited parametric capacity and uneven marginal contribution across skills, the optimal active skill set is non-monotonic, task- and stage-dependent. In this work, we propose SLIM, a framework of dynamic Skill LIfecycle Management for agentic reinforcement learning (RL), which treats the active external skill set as a dynamic optimization variable jointly updated with policy learning. Specifically, SLIM estimates each active skill's marginal external contribution through leave-one-skill-out validation, then applies three lifecycle operations: retaining high-value skills, retiring skills whose contribution becomes negligible after sufficient exposure, and expanding the skill bank when persistent failures reveal missing capability coverage. Experiments show that SLIM outperforms the best baselines by an average of 7.1% points across ALFWorld and SearchQA. Results further indicate that policy learning and external skill retention are not mutually exclusive: some skills are absorbed into the policy, while others continue to provide external value, supporting SLIM as a more general paradigm for skill-based agentic RL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向代理强化学习的动态技能生命周期管理
大型语言模型代理日益依赖外部技能解决复杂任务，技能作为模块化单元扩展了其能力，超越了参数记忆的局限。现有方法假设外部技能要么累积为持久引导，要么内化为策略，最终导致零技能推理。我们认为这一假设过于严格，因为参数容量有限且技能边际贡献不均，最优活跃技能集具有非单调性，且依赖于任务和阶段。本文提出SLIM框架，即面向代理强化学习的动态技能生命周期管理，将活跃外部技能集视为与策略学习共同优化的动态变量。具体而言，SLIM通过留一技能验证估计每个活跃技能的边际外部贡献，然后执行三种生命周期操作：保留高价值技能、在充分暴露后淘汰贡献可忽略的技能、以及在持续失败揭示能力覆盖缺失时扩展技能库。实验表明，SLIM在ALFWorld和SearchQA上平均优于最佳基线7.1%。结果进一步表明，策略学习与外部技能保留并非互斥：部分技能被内化为策略，而其他技能仍提供外部价值，支持SLIM作为更通用的基于技能的代理强化学习范式。

Motivated by the inefficiency of static skill management in agentic reinforcement learning, this work introduces SLIM, a dynamic framework that treats the active skill set as an optimization variable co-trained with the policy. SLIM evaluates each skill’s marginal contribution via leave-one-skill-out validation and applies lifecycle operations—retaining high-value skills, retiring underperforming ones, and expanding the skill bank when capability gaps are detected. Experiments on ALFWorld and SearchQA show SLIM achieves a 7.1% average performance gain over the best baselines, demonstrating that policy learning and external skill retention can coexist, enabling a more flexible and effective skill management paradigm.
为解决现有方法中技能静态管理导致的效率低下问题，本文提出SLIM框架，将活跃技能集作为与策略共同优化的动态变量。SLIM通过留一技能验证评估各技能的边际贡献，并执行三项操作：保留高价值技能、淘汰贡献微弱的技能、在能力缺失时扩充技能库。在ALFWorld和SearchQA上的实验表明，SLIM平均优于基线7.1%，证明策略学习与外部技能保留可共存，为基于技能的智能体强化学习提供了更通用的范式。
Pixal3D: Pixel-Aligned 3D Generation from Images
Authors: Dong-Yang Li, Wang Zhao, Yuxin Chen, Wenbo Hu, Meng-Hao Guo, Fang-Lue Zhang, Ying Shan, Shi-Min Hu
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026
First: 2026-05-11T17:55:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:55:04+00:00
Comments: SIGGRAPH 2026. Project page: https://ldyang694.github.io/projects/pixal3d/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advances in 3D generative models have rapidly improved image-to-3D synthesis quality, enabling higher-resolution geometry and more realistic appearance. Yet fidelity, which measures pixel-level faithfulness of the generated 3D asset to the input image, still remains a central bottleneck. We argue this stems from an implicit 2D-3D correspondence issue: most 3D-native generators synthesize shape in canonical space and inject image cues via attention, leaving pixel-to-3D associations ambiguous. To tackle this issue, we draw inspiration from 3D reconstruction and propose Pixal3D, a pixel-aligned 3D generation paradigm for high-fidelity 3D asset creation from images. Instead of generating in a canonical pose, Pixal3D directly generates 3D in a pixel-aligned way, consistent with the input view. To enable this, we introduce a pixel back-projection conditioning scheme that explicitly lifts multi-scale image features into a 3D feature volume, establishing direct pixel-to-3D correspondence without ambiguity. We show that Pixal3D is not only scalable and capable of producing high-quality 3D assets, but also substantially improves fidelity, approaching the fidelity level of reconstruction. Furthermore, Pixal3D naturally extends to multi-view generation by aggregating back-projected feature volumes across views. Finally, we show pixel-aligned generation benefits scene synthesis, and present a modular pipeline that produces high-fidelity, object-separated 3D scenes from images. Pixal3D for the first time demonstrates 3D-native pixel-aligned generation at scale, and provides a new inspiring way towards high-fidelity 3D generation of object or scene from single or multi-view images. Project page: https://ldyang694.github.io/projects/pixal3d/
中文标题/摘要
标题：Pixal3D：从图像生成像素对齐的3D模型
近期3D生成模型的进展显著提升了图像到3D合成的质量，实现了更高分辨率的几何结构和更逼真的外观。然而，衡量生成3D资产与输入图像像素级忠实度的保真度，仍是核心瓶颈。我们认为，这源于隐式的2D-3D对应问题：大多数原生3D生成器在规范空间中合成形状，并通过注意力机制注入图像线索，导致像素到3D的关联模糊。为解决此问题，我们借鉴3D重建方法，提出Pixal3D，一种面向高保真3D资产生成的像素对齐3D生成范式。与传统方法不同，Pixal3D直接在像素对齐方式下生成3D，与输入视角保持一致。为此，我们引入像素反投影条件化方案，将多尺度图像特征显式提升至3D特征体，建立无歧义的像素到3D对应关系。我们证明Pixal3D不仅具备可扩展性并能生成高质量3D资产，还能显著提升保真度，接近重建水平。此外，Pixal3D自然扩展至多视角生成，通过聚合多视角反投影特征体实现。最后，我们展示像素对齐生成对场景合成的益处，并提出一个模块化流程，可从图像生成高保真、物体分离的3D场景。Pixal3D首次在大规模场景下实现了原生3D像素对齐生成，为从单或多视角图像生成高保真物体或场景提供了新思路。项目页面：https://ldyang694.github.io/projects/pixal3d/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent fidelity gap in 3D generative models—where generated 3D assets fail to preserve pixel-level detail from input images—this work introduces Pixal3D, a pixel-aligned 3D generation method that directly synthesizes 3D geometry consistent with the input view, rather than relying on canonical space or ambiguous attention-based image cues. It achieves this through a pixel back-projection conditioning scheme that lifts multi-scale image features into a 3D feature volume, establishing explicit pixel-to-3D correspondences. Experiments demonstrate that Pixal3D significantly improves fidelity, approaching reconstruction-level accuracy, while remaining scalable and extendable to multi-view generation. It also enables high-fidelity, object-separated 3D scene synthesis, marking the first large-scale 3D-native pixel-aligned generation approach.
为解决图像到3D生成中像素级保真度不足的问题，作者提出Pixal3D方法，通过像素对齐的方式直接生成与输入视角一致的3D几何。该方法采用像素反投影条件化机制，将多尺度图像特征提升至3D特征体中，建立明确的像素到3D对应关系。实验表明，Pixal3D显著提升生成保真度，接近重建水平，同时支持多视角生成与模块化场景合成，实现从单图或多图生成高保真、物体分离的3D场景。
RoboMemArena: A Comprehensive and Challenging Robotic Memory Benchmark
Authors: Huashuo Lei, Wenxuan Song, Huarui Zhang, Jieyuan Pei, Jiayi Chen, Haodong Yan, Han Zhao, Pengxiang Ding, Zhipeng Zhang, Lida Huang, Donglin Wang, Yan Wang, Haoang Li
First: 2026-05-11T17:54:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:54:49+00:00
Comments: Project website: https://robomemarena.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Memory is a critical component of robotic intelligence, as robots must rely on past observations and actions to accomplish long-horizon tasks in partially observable environments. However, existing robotic memory benchmarks still lack multimodal annotations for memory formation, provide limited task coverage and structural complexity, and remain restricted to simulation without real-world evaluation. We address this gap with RoboMemArena, a large-scale benchmark of 26 tasks, with average trajectory lengths exceeding 1,000 steps per task and 68.9% of subtasks being memory-dependent. The generation pipeline leverages a vision-language model (VLM) to design and compose subtasks, generates full trajectories through atomic functions, and provides memory-related annotations, including subtask instructions and native keyframe annotations, while paired real-world memory tasks support physical evaluation. We further design PrediMem, a dual-system VLA in which a high-level VLM planner manages a memory bank with recent and keyframe buffers and uses a predictive coding head to improve sensitivity to task dynamics. Extensive experiments on RoboMemArena show that PrediMem outperforms all baselines and provides insights into memory management, model architecture, and scaling laws for complex memory systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoboMemArena：全面且具有挑战性的机器人记忆基准
记忆是机器人智能的关键组成部分，因为机器人必须依赖过往的观测和行动以在部分可观测环境中完成长期任务。然而，现有的机器人记忆基准仍缺乏多模态的记忆形成标注，任务覆盖范围和结构复杂性有限，且仅限于仿真而无现实世界评估。我们通过RoboMemArena填补这一空白，该基准包含26个任务，每个任务平均轨迹长度超过1000步，68.9%的子任务依赖记忆。生成流程利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）设计和组合子任务，通过原子函数生成完整轨迹，并提供记忆相关标注，包括子任务指令和原生关键帧标注，同时配对现实世界记忆任务支持物理评估。我们进一步设计了PrediMem，一种双系统视觉-语言-动作（VLA）架构，其中高层VLM规划器管理包含近期和关键帧缓冲的记忆库，并使用预测编码头提升对任务动态的敏感性。在RoboMemArena上的大量实验表明，PrediMem优于所有基线方法，并为记忆管理、模型架构和复杂记忆系统的缩放规律提供了洞见。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for comprehensive robotic memory evaluation in real-world, partially observable environments, this work introduces RoboMemArena, a large-scale benchmark with 26 tasks, over 1,000 steps per task on average, and 68.9% memory-dependent subtasks. The benchmark is generated via a vision-language model that composes subtasks and full trajectories using atomic functions, and includes multimodal memory annotations such as keyframes and instructions. Evaluated on both simulation and real robots, the proposed PrediMem system—a dual-system VLA with a VLM planner managing memory buffers and a predictive coding head—achieves superior performance across all baselines, revealing insights into memory architecture, planning, and scaling for complex robotic memory systems.
为应对机器人在部分可观测环境中执行长时任务时对记忆能力的迫切需求，本文提出RoboMemArena，一个包含26个任务、平均轨迹长度超1000步、68.9%子任务依赖记忆的大规模基准。该基准通过视觉-语言模型生成子任务与完整轨迹，辅以关键帧与指令等记忆标注，并支持真实世界评估。所提出的PrediMem系统采用双系统架构，由视觉-语言模型规划器管理记忆缓冲区并结合预测编码头，显著优于所有基线模型，为复杂机器人记忆系统的架构设计、管理策略与扩展规律提供了关键洞见。
Optimal and Scalable MAPF via Multi-Marginal Optimal Transport and Schrödinger Bridges
Authors: Usman A. Khan, Joseph W. Durham
Venue: ICML 2026 spotlight
First: 2026-05-11T17:52:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:52:15+00:00
Comments: Accepted in ICML 2026 as a spotlight paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We consider anonymous multi-agent path finding (MAPF) where a set of robots is tasked to travel to a set of targets on a finite, connected graph. We show that MAPF can be cast as a special class of multi-marginal optimal transport (MMOT) problems with an underlying Markovian structure, under which the exponentially large MMOT collapses to a linear program (LP) polynomial in size. Focusing on the anonymous setting, we establish conditions under which the corresponding LP is feasible, totally unimodular, and consequently, yields min-cost, integral $(\{0,1\})$ transports that do not overlap in both space and time. To adapt the approach to large-scale problems, we cast the MAPF-MMOT in a probabilistic framework via Schrödinger bridges. Under standard assumptions, we show that the Schrödinger bridge formulation reduces to an entropic regularization of the corresponding MMOT that admits an iterative Sinkhorn-type solution. The Schrödinger bridge, being a probabilistic framework, provides a shadow (fractional) transport that we use as a template to solve a reduced LP and demonstrate that it results in near-optimal, integral transports at a significant reduction in complexity. Extensive experiments highlight the optimality and scalability of the proposed approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过多边际最优传输与薛定谔桥实现最优且可扩展的多智能体路径规划
我们研究匿名多智能体路径规划（MAPF）问题，其中一组机器人需在有限连通图上前往一组目标。我们证明MAPF可视为具有马尔可夫结构的特殊多边际最优传输（MMOT）问题，此时指数级大的MMOT问题可坍缩为多项式规模的线性规划（LP）。聚焦匿名设置，我们建立相应LP可行、全单模的条件，从而得到空间与时间均无重叠的最小成本整数（{0,1}）传输方案。为适应大规模问题，我们通过薛定谔桥将MAPF-MMOT转化为概率框架。在标准假设下，薛定谔桥形式化等价于对应MMOT的熵正则化，可迭代求解类似Sinkhorn的解。薛定谔桥作为概率框架，提供一个“影子”（分数）传输方案，我们将其作为模板求解简化LP，证明其可获得近最优整数传输，且复杂度显著降低。大量实验验证了所提方法的最优性与可扩展性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and scalable solutions to anonymous multi-agent path finding (MAPF) on graphs, this work formulates MAPF as a multi-marginal optimal transport (MMOT) problem with a Markovian structure, which reduces the problem to a tractable linear program. Under anonymity constraints, the LP is shown to be feasible and totally unimodular, guaranteeing optimal, integral, non-overlapping paths. To handle large-scale scenarios, the authors introduce a probabilistic Schrödinger bridge formulation that regularizes the MMOT with entropy, enabling an efficient Sinkhorn-style iterative solution. The resulting fractional transport is used to guide a reduced LP, yielding near-optimal integral paths with significantly lower computational complexity, as demonstrated by extensive experiments.
为解决图结构上匿名多智能体路径规划（MAPF）的效率与可扩展性问题，本文将其建模为具有马尔可夫结构的多边际最优传输（MMOT）问题，从而将其压缩为多项式规模的线性规划。在匿名约束下，该线性规划被证明可行且全幺模，可保证最优、整数解、无时空冲突的路径。为应对大规模场景，作者引入概率框架下的施罗丁格桥，通过熵正则化将MMOT转化为可迭代求解的Sinkhorn型问题。所得的分数传输用于指导简化线性规划，最终生成近最优整数路径，且计算复杂度显著降低，实验验证了该方法的最优性与可扩展性。
Confidence-Guided Diffusion Augmentation for Enhanced Bangla Compound Character Recognition
Authors: Md. Sultan Al Rayhan, Maheen Islam
First: 2026-05-11T17:51:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:51:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recognition of handwritten Bangla compound characters remains a challenging problem due to complex character structures, large intra-class variation, and limited availability of high-quality annotated data. Existing Bangla handwritten character recognition systems often struggle to generalize across diverse writing styles, particularly for compound characters containing intricate ligatures and diacritical variations. In this work, we propose a confidence-guided diffusion augmentation framework for low-resolution Bangla compound character recognition. Our framework combines class-conditional diffusion modeling with classifier guidance to synthesize high-quality handwritten compound character samples. To further improve generation quality, we introduce Squeeze-and-Excitation enhanced residual blocks within the diffusion model's U-Net backbone. We additionally propose a confidence-based filtering mechanism where pre-trained classifiers act as quality gates to retain only highly class-consistent synthetic samples. The filtered synthetic images are fused with the original training data and used to retrain multiple classification architectures. Experiments conducted on the AIBangla compound character dataset demonstrate consistent performance improvements across ResNet50, DenseNet121, VGG16, and Vision Transformer architectures. Our best-performing model achieves 89.2\% classification accuracy, surpassing the previously published AIBangla benchmark by a substantial margin. The results demonstrate that quality-aware diffusion augmentation can effectively enhance handwritten character recognition performance in low-resource script domains.

标题：基于置信度引导的扩散增强方法提升孟加拉语复合字符识别

Motivated by the challenges in recognizing handwritten Bangla compound characters due to complex structures and limited annotated data, this work introduces a confidence-guided diffusion augmentation framework. 为解决手写孟加拉语复合字符识别中存在的结构复杂、类内差异大、标注数据稀缺等问题，作者提出一种基于置信度引导的扩散增强框架。该方法结合类条件扩散建模与分类器引导，生成高质量合成复合字符样本，并在U-Net主干网络中引入Squeeze-and-Excitation增强残差块以提升生成质量。通过置信度过滤机制，利用预训练分类器筛选出高度类一致的合成图像，再与原始训练数据融合用于重训练多个架构。在AIBangla数据集上的实验表明，该方法在ResNet50、DenseNet121、VGG16和Vision Transformer等模型上均取得稳定提升，最佳模型准确率达89.2%，显著超越先前基准。
Shepherd: A Runtime Substrate Empowering Meta-Agents with a Formalized Execution Trace
Authors: Simon Yu, Derek Chong, Ananjan Nandi, Dilara Soylu, Jiuding Sun, Christopher D Manning, Weiyan Shi
First: 2026-05-11T17:50:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:50:51+00:00
Comments: 56 pages, 21 figures, 14 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Shepherd, a functional programming model that formalizes meta-agent operations on target agents as functions, with core operations mechanized in Lean. Shepherd records every agent-environment interaction as a typed event in a Git-like execution trace, enabling any past state to be forked and replayed. The system forks the agent process and its filesystem $5\times$ faster than Docker, achieving $>95\%$ prompt-cache reuse on replay. We demonstrate the model through three applications. First, in runtime intervention, a live supervisor increases pair coding pass rates from 28.8% to 54.7% on CooperBench. Second, in counterfactual meta-optimization, branching exploration outperforms baselines across four benchmarks by up to 11 points while reducing wall-clock time by up to 58%. Third, in Tree-RL training, forking rollouts at selected turns improves TerminalBench-2 performance from 34.2% to 39.4%. These results establish Shepherd as an efficient infrastructure for programming meta-agents. We open-source the system to support future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Shepherd：一种支持元代理的运行时基底，其形式化执行轨迹
我们引入Shepherd，一种形式化元代理在目标代理上操作的函数式编程模型，其核心操作在Lean中实现。Shepherd将每个代理-环境交互记录为Git式执行轨迹中的带类型事件，从而支持任意历史状态的分叉与重放。该系统分叉代理进程及其文件系统速度比Docker快5倍，重放时提示缓存复用率超过95%。我们通过三个应用展示了该模型：首先，在运行时干预中，一个实时监督者将CooperBench上的配对编码通过率从28.8%提升至54.7%；其次，在反事实元优化中，分支探索在四个基准测试中表现优于基线最多11点，同时将实际运行时间减少最多58%；第三，在Tree-RL训练中，于选定回合分叉 rollout 将TerminalBench-2性能从34.2%提升至39.4%。这些结果确立了Shepherd作为编程元代理的高效基础设施。我们开源该系统以支持未来研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, reproducible, and traceable meta-agent execution, Shepherd introduces a functional programming model that formalizes meta-agent operations as typed functions mechanized in Lean, recording agent-environment interactions as events in a Git-like trace. The system enables state replay by forking agent processes and filesystems 5x faster than Docker, achieving over 95% prompt-cache reuse. Experimental results show a live supervisor boosting pair coding pass rates from 28.8% to 54.7% on CooperBench, branching exploration improving performance by up to 11 points while cutting wall-clock time by 58% across four benchmarks, and Tree-RL training achieving a 39.4% TerminalBench-2 score via selective rollout forking.
为实现对元智能体的高效、形式化运行时控制，Shepherd提出一种将元智能体操作建模为类型化函数的函数式编程模型，并在Lean中实现核心操作，将智能体与环境交互记录为类型化事件，形成类似Git的执行轨迹以支持状态回放。该系统比Docker快5倍完成进程与文件系统分叉，回放时缓存重用率超过95%。实验表明，实时监督可将CooperBench配对编码通过率从28.8%提升至54.7%，分支探索在四项基准测试中最高提升11点，同时节省58%运行时间，Tree-RL训练中选择性回放使TerminalBench-2性能从34.2%提升至39.4%。
WildClawBench: A Benchmark for Real-World, Long-Horizon Agent Evaluation
Authors: Shuangrui Ding, Xuanlang Dai, Long Xing, Shengyuan Ding, Ziyu Liu, Yang JingYi, Penghui Yang, Zhixiong Zhang, Xilin Wei, Xinyu Fang, Yubo Ma, Haodong Duan, Jing Shao, Jiaqi Wang, Dahua Lin, Kai Chen, Yuhang Zang
First: 2026-05-11T17:49:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:49:43+00:00
Comments: Github link: https://github.com/internlm/WildClawBench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language and vision-language models increasingly power agents that act on a user's behalf through command-line interface (CLI) harnesses. However, most agent benchmarks still rely on synthetic sandboxes, short-horizon tasks, mock-service APIs, and final-answer checks, leaving open whether agents can complete realistic long-horizon work in the runtimes where they are deployed. This work presents WildClawBench, a native-runtime benchmark of 60 human-authored, bilingual, multimodal tasks spanning six thematic categories. Each task averages roughly 8 minutes of wall-clock time and over 20 tool calls, and runs inside a reproducible Docker container hosting an actual CLI agent harness (OpenClaw, Claude Code, Codex, or Hermes Agent) with access to real tools rather than mock services. Grading is hybrid, combining deterministic rule-based checks, environment-state auditing of side effects, and an LLM/VLM judge for semantic verification. Across 19 frontier models, the best, Claude Opus 4.7, reaches only 62.2% overall under OpenClaw, while every other model stays below 60%, and switching harness alone shifts a single model by up to 18 points. These results show that long-horizon, native-runtime agent evaluation remains a far-from-resolved task for current frontier models. We release the tasks, code, and containerized tooling to support reproducible evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WildClawBench：面向真实长周期代理评估的基准测试
大型语言模型和视觉-语言模型正越来越多地通过命令行界面（CLI）代理执行用户任务。然而，大多数代理基准仍依赖合成沙箱、短周期任务、模拟服务API和最终答案检查，未能验证代理在实际部署运行时能否完成真实长周期工作。本工作提出WildClawBench，包含60个由人类编写的、双语、多模态任务，覆盖六大主题类别。每个任务平均耗时约8分钟，工具调用超过20次，运行于可复现的Docker容器内，容器中部署真实CLI代理框架（OpenClaw、Claude Code、Codex或Hermes Agent），并接入真实工具而非模拟服务。评分采用混合方式，结合确定性规则检查、环境状态审计（副作用检测）及LLM/VLM语义验证。在19个前沿模型中，表现最佳的Claude Opus 4.7在OpenClaw框架下仅达62.2%准确率，其余模型均低于60%，且仅更换代理框架即可使单个模型得分变动高达18分。结果表明，当前前沿模型在真实运行时的长周期代理评估仍远未解决。我们开源任务、代码及容器化工具，以支持可复现评估。

Motivated by the gap between synthetic benchmarks and real-world agent deployment, this work introduces WildClawBench, a native-runtime benchmark featuring 60 human-authored, bilingual, multimodal tasks requiring long-horizon execution. 为填补合成基准与真实部署环境之间的差距，本研究提出WildClawBench，包含60个由人类编写的、双语、多模态的长时任务，每个任务平均耗时8分钟，需调用20多次工具，运行于真实CLI代理框架（OpenClaw、Claude Code、Codex、Hermes Agent）的Docker容器中，并使用真实工具。评估结合规则检查、状态审计与大模型语义判断。在19个前沿模型中，Claude Opus 4.7在OpenClaw框架下得分62.2%，其余均低于60%，且仅更换代理框架即可使单模型性能波动达18分，表明当前前沿模型在长时原生运行环境中的评估仍面临重大挑战。
Equivariant Reinforcement Learning for Clifford Quantum Circuit Synthesis
Authors: Richie Yeung, Aleks Kissinger, Rob Cornish
First: 2026-05-11T17:49:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:49:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We consider the problem of synthesizing Clifford quantum circuits for devices with all-to-all qubit connectivity. We approach this task as a reinforcement learning problem in which an agent learns to discover a sequence of elementary Clifford gates that reduces a given symplectic matrix representation of a Clifford circuit to the identity. This formulation permits a simple learning curriculum based on random walks from the identity. We introduce a novel neural network architecture that is equivariant to qubit relabelings of the symplectic matrix representation, and which is size-agnostic, allowing a single learned policy to be applied across different qubit counts without circuit splicing or network reparameterization. On six-qubit Clifford circuits, the largest regime for which optimal references are available, our agent finds circuits within one two-qubit gate of optimality in milliseconds per instance, and finds optimal circuits in 99.2% of instances within seconds per instance. After continued training on ten-qubit instances, the agent scales to unseen Clifford tableaus with up to thirty qubits, including targets generated from circuits with over a thousand Clifford gates, where it achieves lower average two-qubit gate counts than Qiskit's Aaronson-Gottesman and greedy Clifford synthesizers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于克利福德量子电路合成的等变强化学习
我们考虑在所有量子比特全连接设备上合成克利福德量子电路的问题。我们将此任务建模为强化学习问题，其中智能体学习发现一系列基本克利福德门，以将给定的克利福德电路的辛矩阵表示化简为单位矩阵。该建模允许基于从单位矩阵出发的随机游走构建简单的学习课程。我们引入了一种新颖的神经网络架构，该架构对辛矩阵表示中的量子比特重标记具有等变性，且与电路规模无关，使得单一学习策略可应用于不同量子比特数量的电路，无需电路拼接或网络重新参数化。在六量子比特克利福德电路（目前最优参考方案覆盖的最大规模）上，我们的智能体在毫秒级内即可找到与最优解相差一个两量子比特门的电路，并在99.2%的实例中于秒级内找到最优解。经过在十量子比特实例上的持续训练，智能体可扩展至未见过的克利福德表征，最多支持三十量子比特，包括由超过一千个克利福德门组成的电路生成的目标，其平均两量子比特门数量优于Qiskit的Aaronson-Gottesman和贪心克利福德合成器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of synthesizing efficient Clifford quantum circuits for all-to-all connected devices, the authors frame the problem as a reinforcement learning task where an agent learns to apply elementary Clifford gates to reduce a symplectic matrix to identity. Revisiting Policy Gradients for Restricted Policy Classes: Escaping Myopic Local Optima with $k$-step Policy Gradients
Authors: Alex DeWeese, Guannan Qu
First: 2026-05-11T17:49:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:49:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work revisits standard policy gradient methods used on restricted policy classes, which are known to get stuck in suboptimal critical points. We identify an important cause for this phenomenon to be that the policy gradient is itself fundamentally myopic, i.e. it only improves the policy based on the one-step $Q$-function. In this work, we propose a generalized $k$-step policy gradient method that couples the randomness within a $k$-step time window and can escape the myopic local optima in MDPs with restricted policy classes. We show this new method is theoretically guaranteed to converge to a solution that is exponentially close in performance to the optimal deterministic policy with respect to $k$. Further, we show projected gradient descent and mirror descent with this $k$-step policy gradient can achieve this exponential guarantee in $O(\frac{1}{T})$ iterations, despite only assuming smoothness and differentiability of the value function. This will provide near optimal solutions to previously elusive applications like state aggregation and partially observable cooperative multi-agent settings. Moreover, our bounds avoid the ubiquitous distribution mismatch factors $||d_μ^{π^*} / d_μ^π||_\infty$ and $||d_μ^{π^*} / μ||_\infty$ enabling the $k$-step policy gradient method to escape suboptimal critical points that emerge from poor exploration in fully observable settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新审视受限策略类中的策略梯度方法：利用k步策略梯度摆脱近视局部最优
本工作重新审视了在受限策略类上使用的标准策略梯度方法，该方法已知会陷入次优临界点。我们识别出这一现象的重要原因是策略梯度本身具有根本性的近视性，即仅基于一步Q函数改进策略。本文提出了一种广义的k步策略梯度方法，该方法耦合了k步时间窗口内的随机性，可在具有受限策略类的MDP中摆脱近视局部最优。我们证明该新方法在理论上能收敛至相对于k而言性能接近最优确定性策略的解，且指数级接近。进一步，我们证明使用该k步策略梯度的投影梯度下降和镜像下降可在仅假设价值函数光滑性和可微性的情况下，在O(1/T)次迭代内实现这一指数级保证。这将为此前难以处理的应用（如状态聚合和部分可观测的协作多智能体设置）提供近最优解。此外，我们的界避免了普遍存在的分布失配因子||d_μ^{π^*} / d_μ^π||_∞和||d_μ^{π^*} / μ||_∞，使k步策略梯度方法能够摆脱在完全可观测设置中因探索不足而产生的次优临界点。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the limitation of standard policy gradient methods on restricted policy classes, which often converge to suboptimal critical points due to their myopic nature—relying solely on one-step Q-functions. The authors propose a k-step policy gradient method that incorporates randomness over a k-step horizon to escape these local optima. Theoretically, this method guarantees convergence to a policy whose performance is exponentially close to the optimal deterministic policy, with convergence rates of O(1/T) for projected gradient and mirror descent variants, under mild smoothness and differentiability assumptions. Crucially, the method avoids distribution mismatch terms, enabling it to overcome suboptimal points even in fully observable settings, and is applicable to challenging domains like state aggregation and partially observable multi-agent systems.
该论文针对受限策略类上标准策略梯度方法易陷入次优临界点的问题，指出其根源在于一阶Q函数梯度的短视性。作者提出k步策略梯度方法，通过在k步时间窗口内引入随机性以跳出局部最优。理论上，该方法可收敛至性能接近最优确定性策略的解，且在仅假设价值函数光滑可微的前提下，投影梯度下降与镜像下降可在O(1/T)步内实现指数级性能逼近。该方法避免了常见的分布失配项，使其在完全可观测环境下也能克服因探索不足导致的次优临界点，适用于状态聚合与部分可观测多智能体等此前难以解决的应用场景。
FedQueue: Queue-Aware Federated Learning for Cross-Facility HPC Training
Authors: Yijiang Li, Emon Dey, Zilinghan Li, Krishnan Raghavan, Ravi Madduri, Kibaek Kim
First: 2026-05-04T01:11:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:48:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated learning (FL) across multiple HPC facilities faces stochastic admission delays from batch schedulers that dominate wall-clock time. Synchronous FL suffers from severe stragglers, while asynchronous FL accumulates stale updates when queues spike. We propose FedQueue, a queue-aware FL protocol that incorporates scheduler delays directly into training and aggregation, which (i) predicts per-facility queue delays online to budget local work, (ii) applies cutoff-based admission that buffers late arrivals to bound staleness, and (iii) performs staleness-aware aggregation to stabilize heterogeneous local workloads. We prove the convergence for non-convex objectives at rate $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{R})$ under bounded staleness, and show that the admission controls yield bounded staleness with high probability under queue-prediction error. Real-world cross-facility deployment of FedQueue shows 20.5% improvement over baseline algorithms. Controlled queue simulations demonstrate robust improvement over the baselines; in particular, about 34% reduction in time to reach a target accuracy level under high queue variance and non-IID partitions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FedQueue：面向跨设施HPC训练的队列感知联邦学习
多HPC设施间的联邦学习（FL）面临批处理调度器导致的随机准入延迟，该延迟主导实际运行时间。同步FL易受慢节点影响，而异步FL在队列激增时累积过时更新。我们提出FedQueue，一种将调度延迟直接纳入训练与聚合的队列感知FL协议，其（i）在线预测各设施队列延迟以规划本地工作量，（ii）采用截止准入机制缓冲迟到更新以控制过时度，（iii）执行过时感知聚合以稳定异构本地负载。我们在有界过时条件下证明了非凸目标函数的收敛率$\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{R})$，并表明准入控制在队列预测误差下以高概率保证有界过时。FedQueue在真实跨设施部署中较基线算法提升20.5%；受控队列模拟显示其优于基线，尤其在高队列方差与非IID划分下，达到目标精度所需时间减少约34%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the stochastic scheduling delays that dominate training time in cross-facility federated learning, FedQueue introduces a queue-aware protocol that directly models these delays into training and aggregation. It predicts per-facility queue delays to allocate local computation, uses cutoff-based admission to buffer late updates and bound staleness, and employs staleness-aware aggregation to stabilize heterogeneous workloads. 为解决HPC设施间联邦学习因批处理调度器导致的随机准入延迟问题，FedQueue提出一种队列感知协议，将调度延迟直接融入训练与聚合过程。该方法在线预测各设施队列延迟以合理分配本地计算量，采用截止准入机制缓冲迟到更新以控制数据陈旧度，并执行陈旧度感知聚合以稳定异构负载。理论证明其在有界陈旧度下对非凸目标函数收敛率为O(1/√R)，且在队列预测误差下准入控制仍可高概率保证陈旧度有界。实际部署中性能较基线提升20.5%，在高队列波动与非独立同分布数据下，达到目标精度所需时间减少34%。
Engineering Robustness into Personal Agents with the AI Workflow Store
Authors: Roxana Geambasu, Mariana Raykova, Pierre Tholoniat, Trishita Tiwari, Lillian Tsai, Wen Zhang
First: 2026-05-11T17:46:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:46:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The dominant paradigm for AI agents is an "on-the-fly" loop in which agents synthesize plans and execute actions within seconds or minutes in response to user prompts. We argue that this paradigm short-circuits disciplined software engineering (SE) processes -- iterative design, rigorous testing, adversarial evaluation, staged deployment, and more -- that have delivered the (relatively) reliable and secure systems we use today. By focusing on rapid, real-time synthesis, are AI agents effectively delivering users improvised prototypes rather than systems fit for high-stakes scenarios in which users may unwittingly apply them? This paper argues for the need to integrate rigorous SE processes into the agentic loop to produce production-grade, hardened, and deterministically-constrained agent *workflows* that substantially outperform the potentially brittle and vulnerable results of on-the-fly synthesis. Doing so may require extra compute and time, and if so, we must amortize the cost of rigor through reuse across a broad user community. We envision an *AI Workflow Store* that consists of hardened and reusable workflows that agents can invoke with far greater reliability and security than improvised tool chains. We outline the research challenges of this vision, which stem from a broader flexibility-robustness tension that we argue requires moving beyond the ``on-the-fly'' paradigm to navigate effectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过AI工作流存储将鲁棒性融入个人代理
当前AI代理的主流范式是“即时循环”：代理在数秒或数分钟内根据用户提示合成计划并执行操作。我们认为，这种范式绕过了现代软件工程（SE）中严谨的流程——如迭代设计、严格测试、对抗性评估、分阶段部署等——这些流程造就了我们今天所使用的（相对）可靠和安全的系统。若AI代理仅聚焦于快速实时合成，是否实质上只是向用户提供即兴原型，而非适用于高风险场景、用户可能无意中使用的系统？ 本文主张，必须将严谨的SE流程整合进代理循环，以生成符合生产标准、经过加固且确定性约束的代理*工作流*，其性能远超即时合成可能带来的脆弱和易受攻击的结果。实现这一目标可能需要额外的计算和时间，因此必须通过在广泛用户群体中复用，来摊销严谨性的成本。我们构想一个*AI工作流存储*，其中包含经过加固和可复用的工作流，代理可调用这些工作流，实现远高于即兴工具链的可靠性和安全性。我们概述了这一愿景所面临的科研挑战，这些挑战源于更广泛的灵活性-鲁棒性张力，我们认为，要有效应对这一张力，必须超越“即时”范式。

本文指出，当前AI代理在用户提示下即时合成并执行动作，跳过了迭代设计、对抗性测试和分阶段部署等关键软件工程流程，导致系统脆弱且不安全，难以应对高风险场景。为解决此问题，作者主张将严谨的软件工程流程融入代理循环，以构建稳健、可重用且确定性约束的代理工作流。他们构想了一个“AI工作流商店”，其中存储经过加固的工作流，使代理能调用比临时工具链更可靠、更安全的流程，尽管该方法可能增加计算与时间开销，但其效益可通过大规模用户复用摊薄。
DataMaster: Towards Autonomous Data Engineering for Machine Learning
Authors: Yaxin Du, Xiyuan Yang, Zhifan Zhou, Wanxu Liu, Zixing Lei, Zimeng Chen, Fenyi Liu, Haotian Wu, Yuzhu Cai, Zexi Liu, Xinyu Zhu, WenHao Wang, Linfeng Zhang, Chen Qian, Siheng Chen
First: 2026-05-11T17:46:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:46:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As model families, training recipes, and compute budgets become increasingly standardized, further gains in machine learning systems depend increasingly on data. Yet data engineering remains largely manual and ad hoc: practitioners repeatedly search for external datasets, adapt them to existing pipelines, validate candidate data through downstream training, and carry forward lessons from prior attempts. We study task-conditioned autonomous data engineering, where an autonomous agent improves a fixed learning algorithm by optimizing only the data side, including external data discovery, data selection and composition, cleaning and transformation. The goal is to obtain a stronger downstream solution while leaving the learning algorithm unchanged. To address the open-ended search space, branch-dependent refinement, and delayed validation inherent in autonomous data engineering, we propose DataMaster, a data-agent framework that integrates tree-structured search, shared candidate data, and cumulative memory. DataMaster consists of three key components: a DataTree that organizes alternative data-engineering branches, a shared Data Pool that stores discovered external data sources for reuse, and a Global Memory that records node outcomes, artifacts, and reusable findings. Together, these components allow the agent to discover candidate data, construct executable training inputs, evaluate them through downstream feedback, and carry useful evidence across branches. We evaluate DataMaster on two types of benchmarks, MLE-Bench Lite and PostTrainBench. On MLE-Bench Lite, it improves medal rate by 32.27% over the initial score; on PostTrainBench, it surpasses the instruct model on GPQA (31.02% vs 30.35%).

标题：DataMaster：迈向机器学习的自主数据工程
随着模型家族、训练方案和计算预算日益标准化，机器学习系统进一步提升的关键正日益依赖于数据。然而，数据工程仍主要依赖人工和临时性操作：从业者需反复搜索外部数据集，将其适配至现有流水线，通过下游训练验证候选数据，并从过往尝试中汲取经验。我们研究任务驱动的自主数据工程，即通过仅优化数据层面（包括外部数据发现、数据选择与组合、清洗与转换）来提升固定学习算法的性能，目标是在不改变学习算法的前提下获得更强的下游解决方案。为应对自主数据工程中开放式的搜索空间、分支依赖的细化及延迟验证等挑战，我们提出DataMaster——一个数据代理框架，整合树状搜索、共享候选数据与累积记忆机制。DataMaster包含三个核心组件：DataTree用于组织不同的数据工程分支；共享Data Pool用于存储可复用的外部数据源；Global Memory用于记录节点结果、产物及可复用发现。这些组件协同作用，使代理能够发现候选数据、构建可执行训练输入、通过下游反馈评估，并在分支间传递有用证据。我们在两类基准（MLE-Bench Lite和PostTrainBench）上评估DataMaster：在MLE-Bench Lite上，其奖牌率较初始成绩提升32.27%；在PostTrainBench上，其在GPQA任务中超越指令模型（31.02% vs 30.35%）。
