The framework uses a tree-structured search (DataTree), a shared data pool for reuse, and a global memory to accumulate findings across branches. Evaluated on MLE-Bench Lite and PostTrainBench, DataMaster improved the medal rate by 32.27% and outperformed the instruct model on GPQA (31.02% vs 30.35%).
鉴于机器学习系统中数据的重要性日益提升，而模型与算力趋于标准化，本文提出DataMaster框架，旨在通过自主优化数据工程（如数据发现、选择、清洗）而不改变学习算法，解决传统手动数据工程效率低下的问题。该框架包含树状搜索结构、共享数据池和全局记忆模块，以支持跨分支的候选数据发现、可执行训练输入构建、下游反馈评估及经验复用。在MLE-Bench Lite和PostTrainBench两个基准上，DataMaster将初始成绩的奖牌率提升了32.27%，并在GPQA任务上超越了指令模型（31.02% vs 30.35%）。
MDrive: Benchmarking Closed-Loop Cooperative Driving for End-to-End Multi-agent Systems
Authors: Marco Coscoy, Zewei Zhou, Seth Z. Zhao, Henry Wei, Angela Magtoto, Johnson Liu, Rui Song, Walter Zimmer, Zhiyu Huang, Chen Tang, Bolei Zhou, Jiaqi Ma
First: 2026-05-11T17:44:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:44:34+00:00
Comments: website:https://mdrive-challenge.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communication has emerged as a promising paradigm for autonomous driving, enabling connected agents to share complementary perception information and negotiate with each other to benefit the final planning. Existing V2X benchmarks, however, fall short in two ways: (i) open-loop evaluations fail to capture the inherently closed-loop nature of driving, leading to evaluation gaps, and (ii) current closed-loop evaluations lack behavioral and interactive diversity to reflect real-world driving. Thus, it is still unclear the extent of benefits of multi-agent systems for closed-loop driving. In this paper, we introduce MDrive, a closed-loop cooperative driving benchmark comprising 225 scenarios grounded in both NHTSA pre-crash typologies and real-world V2X datasets. Our benchmark results demonstrate that multi-agent systems are generally better than single-agent counterparts. However, current multi-agent systems still face two important challenges: (i) perception sharing enhances perceptions, but doesn't always translate to better planning; (ii) negotiation improves planning performance but harms it in complex and dense traffic scenarios. MDrive further provides an open-source toolbox for scenario generation, Real2Sim conversion, and human-in-the-loop simulation. Together, MDrive establishes a reproducible foundation for evaluating and improving the generalization and robustness of cooperative driving systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MDrive：面向端到端多智能体系统的闭环协同驾驶基准
车对万物（V2X）通信已成为自动驾驶的有前景范式，使互联智能体能够共享互补的感知信息并相互协商以优化最终规划。然而现有V2X基准存在两大不足：（i）开环评估无法捕捉驾驶固有的闭环特性，导致评估偏差；（ii）当前闭环评估缺乏行为与交互多样性，难以反映真实驾驶场景。因此，多智能体系统在闭环驾驶中的优势尚不明确。本文提出MDrive，一个包含225个场景的闭环协同驾驶基准，其场景基于NHTSA预碰撞类型和真实世界V2X数据集构建。实验结果表明，多智能体系统通常优于单智能体系统。但当前多智能体系统仍面临两大挑战：（i）感知共享可增强感知，但未必提升规划效果；（ii）协商可提升规划性能，但在复杂密集交通场景中反而损害性能。MDrive还提供开源工具箱，支持场景生成、真实到仿真转换及人机协同仿真。整体而言，MDrive为评估和提升协同驾驶系统的泛化能力与鲁棒性提供了可复现基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate multi-agent cooperative driving in realistic, closed-loop scenarios, MDrive introduces a benchmark with 225 scenarios derived from NHTSA pre-crash typologies and real-world V2X data. It employs a closed-loop simulation framework that enables agents to share perception and negotiate plans dynamically, addressing gaps in prior open-loop benchmarks. Experimental results show that multi-agent systems generally outperform single-agent systems, but face two key limitations: perception sharing improves perception without always enhancing planning, and negotiation boosts performance in simple scenarios but degrades it in dense traffic. The benchmark also includes tools for scenario generation and human-in-the-loop simulation to support reproducible research.
为评估多智能体协同驾驶在真实闭环环境中的表现，MDrive构建了包含225个场景的基准测试，这些场景基于NHTSA预碰撞类型和真实V2X数据。该基准采用闭环仿真框架，支持智能体动态共享感知信息并协商规划，以反映现实交互多样性。实验表明，多智能体系统总体优于单智能体系统，但效果不一：感知共享可提升感知能力，但未必改善规划；协商在简单场景中提升规划性能，但在密集交通中反而降低性能。该基准还提供场景生成、真实到仿真转换及人机协同仿真工具，支持可复现研究。
Distributionally Robust Token Optimization in RLHF
Authors: Yeping Jin, Jiaming Hu, Ioannis Ch. Paschalidis
First: 2026-03-27T21:36:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:42:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) tend to respond correctly to prompts that align well with the data they were trained and fine-tuned on. Yet, small shifts in wording, format, or language can trigger surprisingly large failures, especially on multi-step reasoning problems. To address this problem, we propose a Distributionally Robust Token Optimization (DRTO) approach, which combines token-level Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) with Distributionally Robust Optimization (DRO). DRTO constructs f-divergence ambiguity sets over span-level actor losses, providing a principled way to emphasize difficult response segments during policy optimization. Empirically, DRTO enhances consistency under distribution shifts in multiple reasoning benchmarks among different tasks, achieving $+4.4$ percentage points on MATH-500 and $+2.7$ percentage points on LiveCodeBench over standard RTO.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RLHF中的分布鲁棒令牌优化
大语言模型（LLMs）倾向于正确响应与其训练和微调数据高度一致的提示，但措辞、格式或语言的微小变化可能引发显著失败，尤其是在多步推理问题上。为解决此问题，我们提出分布鲁棒令牌优化（DRTO）方法，将令牌级基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）与分布鲁棒优化（DRO）相结合。DRTO在跨度级演员损失上构建f-散度模糊集，为策略优化提供了一种强调困难响应段落的原理性方法。实证表明，DRTO在多个任务的推理基准测试中提升了分布偏移下的稳定性，在MATH-500上提升4.4个百分点，在LiveCodeBench上提升2.7个百分点，优于标准RTO。
Summary / 总结
To improve the robustness of large language models against distributional shifts in prompts, especially in multi-step reasoning, this paper introduces Distributionally Robust Token Optimization (DRTO), which integrates token-level RLHF with DRO. DRTO builds f-divergence ambiguity sets around span-level actor losses to prioritize hard response segments during policy updates. Experiments show DRTO improves consistency under distributional shifts, boosting performance by +4.4 percentage points on MATH-500 and +2.7 percentage points on LiveCodeBench compared to standard RTO.
为提升大语言模型在提示词微小变化下的鲁棒性，特别是在多步推理场景中，本文提出分布鲁棒令牌优化（DRTO），将令牌级RLHF与分布鲁棒优化结合，构建基于f-散度的跨度级演员损失模糊集，引导模型聚焦困难响应片段。实验表明，DRTO在分布偏移下显著提升一致性，在MATH-500和LiveCodeBench上分别比标准RTO提升4.4和2.7个百分点。
CapVector: Learning Transferable Capability Vectors in Parametric Space for Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Wenxuan Song, Han Zhao, Fuhao Li, Ziyang Zhou, Xi Wang, Jing Lyu, Pengxiang Ding, Yan Wang, Donglin Wang, Haoang Li
First: 2026-05-11T17:41:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:41:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper proposes a novel approach to address the challenge that pretrained VLA models often fail to effectively improve performance and reduce adaptation costs during standard supervised finetuning (SFT). Some advanced finetuning methods with auxiliary training objectives can improve performance and reduce the number of convergence steps. However, they typically incur significant computational overhead due to the additional losses from auxiliary objectives. To simultaneously achieve the enhanced capabilities of auxiliary training with the simplicity of standard SFT, we decouple the two objectives of auxiliary-objective SFT within the parameter space, namely, enhancing general capabilities and fitting task-specific action distributions. To deliver the goal, we only need to train the model to converge on a small-scale task set using two distinct training strategies, resulting in two finetuned models. The parameters' difference between the two models can then be interpreted as capability vectors provided by auxiliary objectives. These vectors are then merged with pretrained parameters to form a capability-enhanced meta model. Moreover, when standard SFT is augmented with a lightweight orthogonal regularization loss, the merged model attains performance comparable to auxiliary finetuned baselines with reduced computational overhead. Internal and external experiments demonstrate that our capability vectors (1) are effective and versatile across diverse models, (2) can generalize to novel environments and embodiments out of the box.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CapVector：在参数空间中学习可迁移的能力向量以提升视觉-语言-动作模型
本文提出了一种新方法，以解决预训练VLA模型在标准监督微调（SFT）中常难以有效提升性能并降低适配成本的问题。部分带有辅助训练目标的高级微调方法可提升性能并减少收敛步数，但通常因额外损失导致显著计算开销。为同时实现辅助训练的增强能力与标准SFT的简洁性，我们在参数空间中解耦辅助目标SFT的两个目标：增强通用能力与拟合任务特定动作分布。为达成目标，仅需用两种不同训练策略在小规模任务集上训练模型，得到两个微调模型。两模型参数差异可被解释为辅助目标提供的能力向量，再与预训练参数融合形成能力增强的元模型。此外，当标准SFT加入轻量级正交正则化损失时，融合模型性能可媲美辅助微调基线，且计算开销更低。内部与外部实验表明，我们的能力向量（1）在多种模型中均有效且通用，（2）可直接泛化至新环境与具身形态。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of auxiliary training in vision-language-action models during supervised fine-tuning, this work introduces CapVector, a method that decouples general capability enhancement from task-specific adaptation in parameter space. By training two distinct models on a small task set using different strategies, the difference in their parameters is interpreted as capability vectors, which are then merged with pretrained weights to form a meta-model. When this meta-model is fine-tuned with a lightweight orthogonal regularization, it achieves performance comparable to full auxiliary training but with significantly reduced computational cost. Experiments confirm that these capability vectors are transferable across models and generalize to unseen environments and embodiments.
为解决预训练视觉-语言-动作模型在标准监督微调中性能提升有限、适应成本高的问题，本文提出CapVector方法，通过在参数空间中解耦通用能力增强与任务特定适应。在小规模任务集上用两种不同策略训练两个模型，其参数差异即为由辅助目标生成的能力向量，再与预训练权重融合形成增强型元模型。实验表明，该元模型在标准微调中加入轻量正则化后，性能可媲美传统辅助微调基线，且能力向量具备跨模型与新环境的泛化能力。
Beyond Red-Teaming: Formal Guarantees of LLM Guardrail Classifiers
Authors: Nikita Kezins, Urbas Ekka, Pascal Berrang, Luca Arnaboldi
First: 2026-05-11T17:41:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:41:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Guardrail Classifiers defend production language models against harmful behavior, but although results seem promising in testing, they provide no formal guarantees. Providing formal guarantees for such models is hard because "harmful behavior" has no natural specification in a discrete input space: and the standard epsilon-ball properties used in other domains do not carry semantic meaning. We close this gap by shifting verification from the discrete input space to the classifier's pre-activation space, where we define a harmful region as a convex shape enclosing the representations of known harmful prompts. Because the sigmoid classification head is monotonic, certifying the worst-case point is sufficient to certify the entire region, yielding a closed-form soundness proof without approximation in O(d) time. To formally evaluate these classifiers, we propose two constructions of such regions: SVD-aligned hyper-rectangles, which yield exact SAT/UNSAT certificates, and Gaussian Mixture Models, which yield probabilistic certificates over semantically coherent clusters. Applying this framework to three author-trained Guardrail Classifiers on the toxicity domain, every hyper-rectangle configuration returns SAT, exposing verifiable safety holes across all classifiers, despite seemingly high empirical metrics. Probabilistic GMM certificates also expose a divergent structural stability in how these models represent harm. While GPT-2 and Llama-3.1-8B maintain robust coverage of 90% and 80% across varying boundaries, BERT's safety guarantees prove uniquely volatile. This 'coverage collapse' to 55% at the optimal threshold reveals a sparsely populated safety margin in BERT, which only achieves full coverage by adopting an extremely conservative pessimistic threshold. These approaches combined, provide new insights on how effective Guardrail Classifiers really are, beyond traditional red-teaming.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越红队攻击：大型语言模型护栏分类器的形式化保障
护栏分类器用于防御生产级语言模型中的有害行为，尽管测试结果看似乐观，但它们并未提供形式化保障。为这类模型提供形式化保障困难，因为“有害行为”在离散输入空间中没有自然的规范定义，且其他领域常用的ε球性质在此不具语义意义。我们通过将验证从离散输入空间转移到分类器的预激活空间来弥合这一差距，在该空间中，我们将有害区域定义为包含已知有害提示表示的凸形区域。由于Sigmoid分类头是单调的，验证最坏情况点足以验证整个区域，从而在O(d)时间内无需近似即可获得闭式形式的正确性证明。为正式评估此类分类器，我们提出两种区域构造方法：SVD对齐的超矩形，可提供精确的SAT/UNSAT证书；高斯混合模型，可提供语义连贯聚类上的概率证书。将该框架应用于三个作者训练的毒性领域护栏分类器，每个超矩形配置均返回SAT，揭示了所有分类器中可验证的安全漏洞，尽管其经验指标看似较高。概率性GMM证书还揭示了这些模型在表示危害时结构稳定性存在差异。尽管GPT-2和Llama-3.1-8B在不同边界下保持90%和80%的稳健覆盖，BERT的安全保障却表现出独特波动性——在最优阈值下“覆盖坍缩”至55%，揭示BERT安全边际稀疏，仅在采用极端保守悲观阈值时才能实现完全覆盖。这些方法结合，为超越传统红队攻击的护栏分类器实际有效性提供了新见解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of formal safety guarantees for language model guardrail classifiers despite promising empirical performance, the paper introduces a verification method that shifts analysis from discrete input space to the classifier’s pre-activation space, defining harmful regions as convex shapes enclosing representations of known harmful prompts. Leveraging the monotonicity of the sigmoid head, it certifies worst-case points to guarantee entire regions, enabling closed-form soundness proofs in linear time. Two region constructions—SVD-aligned hyper-rectangles yielding exact SAT/UNSAT certificates and Gaussian Mixture Models providing probabilistic coverage—are applied to three author-trained classifiers on toxicity. Results show all hyper-rectangle configurations return SAT, revealing verifiable safety holes; GMMs further expose structural instability, with BERT exhibiting a coverage collapse to 55% at optimal thresholds, indicating a sparsely populated safety margin requiring extreme conservatism, while GPT-2 and Llama-3.1-8B maintain robust coverage of 90% and 80% respectively.
鉴于现有语言模型守卫分类器虽在实证测试中表现良好，但缺乏形式化安全保证，该研究将验证从离散输入空间迁移到分类器的预激活空间，将有害区域定义为包含已知有害提示表征的凸形区域。利用sigmoid分类头的单调性，通过验证最坏点即可保证整个区域，从而在O(d)时间内实现闭式形式化证明。通过两种区域构造方法——SVD对齐的超矩形（提供精确SAT/UNSAT证书）和高斯混合模型（提供语义聚类上的概率覆盖证书）——发现所有三个测试守卫分类器均存在可验证的安全漏洞。GMM分析进一步揭示结构稳定性差异：GPT-2和Llama-3.1-8B在不同边界下保持90%和80%的稳健覆盖，而BERT的安全覆盖在最优阈值下骤降至55%，表明其安全边际稀疏，需采用极端保守阈值才能实现全覆盖。
RubricEM: Meta-RL with Rubric-guided Policy Decomposition beyond Verifiable Rewards
Authors: Gaotang Li, Bhavana Dalvi Mishra, Zifeng Wang, Jun Yan, Yanfei Chen, Chun-Liang Li, Long T. Le, Rujun Han, George Lee, Hanghang Tong, Chen-Yu Lee, Tomas Pfister
First: 2026-05-11T17:40:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:40:38+00:00
Comments: 63 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training deep research agents, namely systems that plan, search, evaluate evidence, and synthesize long-form reports, pushes reinforcement learning beyond the regime of verifiable rewards. Their outputs lack ground-truth answers, their trajectories span many tool-augmented decisions, and standard post-training offers little mechanism for turning past attempts into reusable experience. In this work, we argue that rubrics should serve not merely as final-answer evaluators, but as the shared interface that structures policy execution, judge feedback, and agent memory. Based on this view, we introduce RubricEM, a rubric-guided reinforcement learning framework that combines stagewise policy decomposition with reflection-based meta-policy evolution. RubricEM first makes research trajectories stage-aware by conditioning planning, evidence gathering, review, and synthesis on self-generated rubrics. It then assigns credit with Stage-Structured GRPO, which uses stagewise rubric judgments to provide denser semantic feedback for long-horizon optimization. In parallel, RubricEM trains a shared-backbone reflection meta-policy that distills judged trajectories into reusable rubric-grounded guidance for future attempts. The resulting RubricEM-8B achieves strong performance across four long-form research benchmarks, outperforming comparable open models and approaching proprietary deep-research systems. Beyond final performance, we perform thorough analyses to understand the key ingredients of RubricEM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RubricEM：超越可验证奖励的基于评分标准引导的元强化学习策略分解
训练深度研究代理（即能够规划、搜索、评估证据并合成长篇报告的系统）将强化学习推向了可验证奖励之外的领域。这些代理的输出缺乏真实答案，其轨迹涵盖大量工具增强决策，且标准的后训练过程难以将过往尝试转化为可复用的经验。本文认为，评分标准不应仅作为最终答案的评估工具，而应作为结构化策略执行、判断反馈和代理记忆的共享接口。基于此观点，我们提出了RubricEM，一种基于评分标准引导的强化学习框架，结合分阶段策略分解与基于反思的元策略演化。RubricEM首先通过将规划、证据收集、审查和合成过程与自动生成的评分标准相结合，使研究轨迹具备阶段感知能力；随后采用阶段结构化GRPO分配信用，利用分阶段评分判断提供更密集的语义反馈以支持长时域优化。同时，RubricEM训练一个共享主干的反思元策略，将已评估轨迹提炼为可复用的评分标准引导性指导，用于未来尝试。最终，RubricEM-8B在四个长篇研究基准测试中表现强劲，超越同类开源模型，接近专有深度研究系统。除最终性能外，我们还进行了全面分析，以理解RubricEM的关键要素。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of training deep research agents that operate beyond verifiable rewards, this work proposes RubricEM, a meta-reinforcement learning framework that uses rubrics not just for final evaluation but as a structural interface for policy execution, feedback, and memory. The method decomposes research trajectories into stages and conditions each phase on self-generated rubrics, then employs Stage-Structured GRPO to assign credit based on stagewise rubric judgments, enabling denser semantic feedback. Concurrently, a reflection meta-policy distills judged trajectories into reusable guidance. Experiments show RubricEM-8B achieves strong performance across four long-form research benchmarks, outperforming comparable open models and approaching proprietary systems.
为解决深度研究代理在缺乏可验证奖励情况下训练的难题，本文提出RubricEM框架，通过评分标准引导策略分解与元策略反思。该方法将研究轨迹分解为规划、证据收集、评审与合成等阶段，每个阶段依据自动生成的评分标准进行条件化，并采用分阶段GRPO分配信用以提供密集语义反馈；同时，共享元策略将被评判的轨迹提炼为可复用的评分标准引导指导。实验表明，RubricEM-8B在四个长文本研究基准上表现强劲，超越同类开源模型并接近专有深度研究系统，分析验证了评分引导分解与元反思机制的有效性。
How Creatives Approach GenAI Image Generation: Tensions Between Structured Guidance, Self-Experimentation, and Creative Autonomy
Authors: Haidan Liu, Isabelle Kwan, Taiga Okuma, Jeffrey Loverock, Nicholas Vincent, Parmit K Chilana
First: 2026-05-11T17:40:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:40:36+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ACM Creativity & Cognition 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As generative AI tools increasingly influence creative practice, they raise longstanding HCI questions about how creatives learn complex software and how they can be better supported. We conducted an interview study with artists and hobbyists (n=8) and a follow-up survey (n=159) to understand how this population approaches and seeks guidance for GenAI image tools. We found that creatives commonly use either self-experimentation or tutorials to explore GenAI tools, yet many struggle with confusing AI terminology. To gain further insight into creatives' learning experiences, we developed a research probe to elicit creatives' perceptions of structured guidance. Our user study with 17 creatives revealed that, even when creatives described the guidance as helpful for understanding AI, many still preferred self-experimentation, feeling that guidance could limit their creativity. Our findings highlight a central tension in supporting AI literacy for creatives: balancing guidance and promoting literacy while preserving creative freedom.
中文标题/摘要
标题：创意人士如何应对生成式AI图像生成：结构化指导、自我实验与创意自主之间的张力
随着生成式AI工具日益影响创意实践，它们引发了人机交互领域长期存在的问题：创意人士如何学习复杂软件，以及如何获得更好的支持。我们对8位艺术家和爱好者进行了访谈，并随后对159人进行了问卷调查，以了解该群体如何使用并寻求生成式AI图像工具的指导。我们发现，创意人士通常通过自我实验或教程探索这些工具，但许多人难以理解AI术语。为深入了解创意人士的学习体验，我们设计了一项研究探针，以收集他们对结构化指导的看法。对17位创意人士的用户研究显示，即使他们认为指导有助于理解AI，许多人仍更偏好自我实验，认为指导可能限制其创造力。我们的研究结果突显了支持创意人士AI素养的核心张力：在提供指导、促进素养的同时，如何保持创意自由。
Summary / 总结
As generative AI tools become more integrated into creative workflows, researchers sought to understand how artists and hobbyists learn and use these tools, focusing on tensions between structured guidance and creative autonomy. Through interviews with eight creatives and a survey of 159, the study found that users primarily rely on self-experimentation or tutorials, often struggling with ambiguous AI terminology. A follow-up probe with 17 participants revealed that while structured guidance was seen as useful for understanding AI concepts, many users still preferred self-experimentation, fearing it might constrain their creative freedom. The findings underscore a key challenge in designing AI literacy support: balancing instructional clarity with the preservation of creative autonomy.
随着生成式AI工具在创意工作流中日益普及，研究者旨在探究艺术家和爱好者如何学习与使用这些工具，特别是结构化指导与创意自主性之间的张力。通过对8位创意人士的访谈和159人的问卷调查发现，用户主要依赖自我实验或教程，常因AI术语晦涩而困扰。后续对17位参与者的探针研究显示，尽管结构化指导被认为有助于理解AI概念，许多人仍更倾向自我实验，担心其限制创造力。研究揭示了AI素养设计中的核心挑战：在支持学习的同时，不能牺牲创意自由。
V4FinBench: Benchmarking Tabular Foundation Models, LLMs, and Standard Methods on Corporate Bankruptcy Prediction
Authors: Marcin Kostrzewa, Sebastian Tomczak, Roman Furman, Anna Poberezhna, Michał Furgała, Oleksii Furman, Maciej Zięba
First: 2026-05-11T17:38:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:38:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Corporate bankruptcy prediction is a high-stakes financial task characterized by severe class imbalance and multi-horizon forecasting demands. Public datasets supporting it remain scarce and small: widely used free benchmarks contain between 6,000 and 80,000 company-year observations, while larger resources are behind subscription paywalls. To address this gap, we introduce V4FinBench, a benchmark of over one million company-year records from the Visegràd Group (V4) economies (2006-2021), with 131 financial and non-financial features, six prediction horizons, and a composite distress criterion jointly capturing solvency, profitability, and liquidity deterioration. V4FinBench is designed to support the evaluation of tabular and foundation-model methods under realistic class imbalance, with positive rates between 0.19% and 0.36%. We provide reference evaluations of standard tabular baselines, finetuned TabPFN, and QLoRA-finetuned Llama-3-8B. With imbalance-aware finetuning, TabPFN matches or exceeds gradient boosting at longer time horizons on both $F_1$-score and ROC-AUC. In contrast, Llama-3-8B trails gradient boosting on ROC-AUC at every horizon and is generally weaker on $F_1$-score, with the gap widening sharply beyond the immediate horizon. In an external evaluation on the American Bankruptcy Dataset, the V4FinBench-finetuned TabPFN checkpoint improves over vanilla TabPFN, suggesting that adaptation captures transferable financial-distress structure rather than only V4-specific patterns. V4FinBench is publicly released to support further evaluation and development of prediction methods on realistic financial data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：V4FinBench：面向企业破产预测的表格基础模型、大语言模型及标准方法基准测试
企业破产预测是一项高风险金融任务，具有严重的类别不平衡和多时间跨度预测需求。支持该任务的公开数据集稀缺且规模较小：广泛使用的免费基准数据集包含6,000至80,000个公司年度观测值，而更大规模的数据资源则需付费订阅。为填补这一空白，我们引入V4FinBench，该基准包含来自维谢格勒集团（V4）经济体（2006-2021）的超过一百万条公司年度记录，涵盖131个财务与非财务特征、六个预测时间跨度，并采用综合困境标准联合衡量偿付能力、盈利能力和流动性恶化。V4FinBench旨在支持在真实类别不平衡条件下对表格和基础模型方法的评估，正类率介于0.19%至0.36%之间。我们提供了标准表格基线、微调后的TabPFN以及QLoRA微调后的Llama-3-8B的参考评估结果。在长周期预测中，通过类别不平衡感知微调，TabPFN在F1分数和ROC-AUC上与梯度提升模型持平或更优。相比之下，Llama-3-8B在所有时间跨度上均落后于梯度提升模型的ROC-AUC表现，且在F1分数上普遍较弱，差距在超短期预测后显著扩大。在对美国破产数据集的外部评估中，经V4FinBench微调的TabPFN模型优于原始TabPFN，表明适应性捕捉的是可迁移的金融困境结构，而非仅限于V4特定模式。V4FinBench已公开发布，以支持在真实金融数据上进一步评估和开发预测方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of large, publicly available datasets for corporate bankruptcy prediction—where class imbalance and multi-horizon forecasting pose significant challenges—the authors introduce V4FinBench, a benchmark comprising over one million company-year records from V4 economies (2006–2021), featuring 131 features and six prediction horizons under a composite distress metric. They evaluate standard tabular methods, finetuned TabPFN, and QLoRA-finetuned Llama-3-8B, with imbalance-aware tuning. Results show TabPFN matches or outperforms gradient boosting on F1-score and ROC-AUC at longer horizons, while Llama-3-8B consistently underperforms gradient boosting on ROC-AUC and shows a widening gap in F1-score beyond the immediate horizon. An external test on the American Bankruptcy Dataset confirms that V4FinBench-finetuned TabPFN improves over vanilla TabPFN, indicating transferable financial distress patterns.
为应对企业破产预测领域公共数据稀缺、类别不平衡严重且需多时间步预测的挑战，作者构建了V4FinBench数据集，包含2006–2021年间超过一百万条来自V4经济体的公司年度记录，涵盖131个财务与非财务特征及六个预测时点，并采用综合指标衡量企业财务困境。该数据集正类比例低至0.19%，旨在支持真实不平衡场景下的模型评估。实验对比了标准表格方法、微调后的TabPFN与QLoRA微调的Llama-3-8B模型，结果显示：经过不平衡感知微调的TabPFN在长时预测中F1分数与ROC-AUC上不逊于梯度提升模型，而Llama-3-8B在所有时点均落后于梯度提升模型，且F1差距随预测时点延长而扩大。在外部美国破产数据集上的测试表明，V4FinBench微调后的TabPFN优于原始版本，说明其学习到了可迁移的财务困境模式而非仅限于V4地区特征。
Counterfactual Stress Testing for Image Classification Models
Authors: Moritz Stammel, Fabio De Sousa Ribeiro, Raghav Mehta, Mélanie Roschewitz, Ben Glocker
First: 2026-05-11T17:36:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:36:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep learning models in medical imaging often fail when deployed in new clinical environments due to distribution shifts in demographics, scanner hardware, or acquisition protocols. A central challenge is underspecification, where models with similar validation performance exhibit divergent real-world failure modes. Although stress testing has emerged as a tool to assess this, current methods typically rely on simple, uninformed perturbations (e.g., brightness or contrast changes), which fail to capture clinically realistic variation and can overestimate robustness. In this work, we introduce a counterfactual stress testing framework based on causal generative models that create realistic "what if" images by intervening on attributes such as scanner type and patient sex while preserving anatomical identity, enabling controlled and semantically meaningful evaluation under targeted distribution shifts. Across two imaging modalities (chest X-ray and mammography), three model architectures, and multiple shift scenarios, we show that counterfactual stress tests provide a substantially more accurate proxy for real out-of-distribution performance than classical perturbations, capturing the direction and relative magnitude of performance changes as well as model ranking. These results suggest that causal generative models can serve as practical simulators for robustness assessment, offering a more reliable basis for evaluating medical AI systems prior to deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向图像分类模型的反事实压力测试
医学影像中的深度学习模型在新临床环境中部署时常因人口统计学、扫描硬件或采集协议的分布偏移而失效。核心挑战在于模型的欠指定问题，即验证性能相似的模型在现实世界中表现出不同的失效模式。尽管压力测试已成为评估该问题的工具，但现有方法通常依赖简单的、无指导的扰动（如亮度或对比度变化），无法捕捉临床现实中的变异，且可能高估模型的鲁棒性。本文提出一种基于因果生成模型的反事实压力测试框架，通过干预扫描仪类型、患者性别等属性，同时保持解剖结构一致性，生成真实的“如果……会怎样”图像，从而在目标分布偏移下实现可控且语义有意义的评估。在胸部X光和乳腺摄影两种成像模态、三种模型架构及多种偏移场景下，我们证明反事实压力测试比传统扰动方法更准确地模拟真实分布外性能，不仅能捕捉性能变化的方向和相对幅度，还能反映模型排序。结果表明，因果生成模型可作为评估鲁棒性的实用模拟器，为医学AI系统部署前提供更可靠的评估基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor generalization of medical image models in new clinical settings due to distribution shifts, this work proposes a counterfactual stress testing method using causal generative models to simulate realistic, targeted variations in scanner type and patient attributes while preserving anatomy. Unlike traditional perturbations that fail to reflect clinical reality, this approach generates semantically meaningful "what if" scenarios enabling controlled evaluation. Experiments across chest X-ray and mammography with multiple models and shift types show that counterfactual tests more accurately predict real-world performance degradation, capture directional changes, and reliably rank model robustness compared to standard stress tests.
为了解决医学图像分类模型在新临床环境中因分布偏移而泛化能力差的问题，本文提出一种基于因果生成模型的反事实压力测试方法，通过干预扫描仪类型或患者性别等属性，同时保持解剖结构不变，生成真实且有针对性的“如果……会怎样”图像。与传统仅依赖亮度或对比度等简单扰动的方法不同，该方法能更贴近临床现实。实验表明，在胸片和乳腺X光图像上，该方法能更准确预测模型在真实分布外场景下的性能下降趋势，捕捉失败方向与幅度，并更可靠地区分模型鲁棒性，优于传统压力测试方法。
Grounded or Guessing? LVLM Confidence Estimation via Blind-Image Contrastive Ranking
Authors: Reza Khanmohammadi, Erfan Miahi, Simerjot Kaur, Charese H. Smiley, Ivan Brugere, Kundan Thind, Mohammad M. Ghassemi
First: 2026-05-11T17:35:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:35:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large vision-language models suffer from visual ungroundedness: they can produce a fluent, confident, and even correct response driven entirely by language priors, with the image contributing nothing to the prediction. Existing confidence estimation methods cannot detect this, as they observe model behavior under normal inference with no mechanism to determine whether a prediction was shaped by the image or by text alone. We introduce BICR (Blind-Image Contrastive Ranking), a model-agnostic confidence estimation framework that makes this contrast explicit during training by extracting hidden states from a frozen LVLM twice: once with the real image-question pair, and once with the image blacked out while the question is held fixed. A lightweight probe is trained on the real-image hidden state and regularized by a ranking loss that penalizes higher confidence on the blacked-out view, teaching it to treat visual grounding as a signal of reliability at zero additional inference cost. Evaluated across five modern LVLMs and seven baselines on a benchmark covering visual question answering, object hallucination detection, medical imaging, and financial document understanding, BICR achieves the best cross-LVLM average on both calibration and discrimination simultaneously, with statistically significant discrimination gains robust to cluster-aware analysis at 4-18x fewer parameters than the strongest probing baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于盲图像对比排序的LVLM置信度估计：是 grounded 还是猜测？
大型视觉-语言模型存在视觉脱域问题：它们可能仅依赖语言先验，生成流畅、自信甚至正确的回答，而图像对预测毫无贡献。现有置信度估计方法无法检测此类情况，因为它们仅在常规推理中观察模型行为，缺乏判断预测是受图像影响还是纯文本驱动的机制。我们提出BICR（盲图像对比排序），一种模型无关的置信度估计框架，在训练中通过从冻结的LVLM中提取隐藏状态两次实现对比：一次使用真实图像-问题对，另一次则将图像遮蔽而保持问题不变。一个轻量级探测器在真实图像隐藏状态上训练，并通过排序损失进行正则化，该损失惩罚遮蔽图像视图的高置信度，从而教会探测器将视觉 grounding 视为可靠性信号，且无需额外推理开销。在涵盖视觉问答、物体幻觉检测、医学影像和金融文档理解的基准测试中，BICR在五个现代LVLM和七个基线模型上同时实现最佳跨模型平均校准与区分能力，且在参数量仅为最强探测基线的4至18倍时，其区分能力在聚类感知分析下仍具有统计显著性提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue of visual ungroundedness in large vision-language models—where predictions may rely solely on language priors without image input—this paper proposes BICR, a model-agnostic confidence estimation method that explicitly contrasts image-grounded and image-blanked scenarios during training. By extracting hidden states from a frozen LVLM twice—once with the real image and once with the image blacked out while holding the question fixed—a lightweight probe is trained to rank confidence based on visual grounding, penalizing overconfidence when no visual input is present. Evaluated across five LVLMs and seven baselines on diverse tasks including VQA, hallucination detection, medical imaging, and financial document analysis, BICR outperforms all baselines in both calibration and discrimination, achieving statistically significant gains while using 4–18x fewer parameters than the best probing method.
针对大型视觉-语言模型中视觉无锚定问题（即响应可能完全由语言先验驱动而非图像内容），本文提出BICR方法，通过在训练中对比真实图像与黑屏图像的隐藏状态，显式区分视觉依赖性。该方法在冻结的LVLM上提取两种输入的隐藏状态，训练轻量探针并用排序损失惩罚黑屏图像高置信度，从而将视觉锚定视为可靠性信号，且无需额外推理开销。在涵盖视觉问答、医学影像等多任务的基准测试中，BICR在五个LVLM和七个基线模型上均表现最优，同时在标定与区分能力上均显著优于基线，参数量仅为最强探针基线的4–18倍。
Unmasking On-Policy Distillation: Where It Helps, Where It Hurts, and Why
Authors: Mohammadreza Armandpour, Fatih Ilhan, David Harrison, Ajay Jaiswal, Duc N. M Hoang, Fartash Faghri, Yizhe Zhang, Minsik Cho, Mehrdad Farajtabar
First: 2026-05-11T17:33:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:33:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
On-policy distillation offers dense, per-token supervision for training reasoning models; however, it remains unclear under which conditions this signal is beneficial and under which it is detrimental. Which teacher model should be used, and in the case of self-distillation, which specific context should serve as the supervisory signal? Does the optimal choice vary from one token to the next? At present, addressing these questions typically requires costly training runs whose aggregate performance metrics obscure the dynamics at the level of individual tokens. We introduce a training-free diagnostic framework that operates at the highest resolution: per token, per question, and per teacher. We derive an ideal per-node gradient defined as the parameter update that maximally increases the student's probability of success. We then develop a scalable targeted-rollout algorithm to estimate this gradient efficiently, even for long chains of intermediate thoughts. The gradient alignment score, defined as the cosine similarity between this ideal gradient and any given distillation gradient, quantifies the extent to which a particular configuration approximates the ideal signal. Across a range of self-distillation settings and external teacher models, we observe that distillation guidance exhibits substantially higher alignment with the ideal on incorrect rollouts than on correct ones, where the student already performs well and the teacher's signal tends to become noisy. Furthermore, we find that the optimal distillation context depends jointly on the student model's capacity and the target task, and that no single universally effective configuration emerges. These findings motivate the use of per-task, per-token diagnostic analyses for distillation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：揭开在线策略蒸馏：它在何处有益、在何处有害，以及原因何在
在线策略蒸馏为训练推理模型提供密集的逐词监督；然而，尚不清楚该信号在何种条件下有益、在何种条件下有害。应使用哪种教师模型？在自蒸馏情况下，应采用何种特定上下文作为监督信号？最优选择是否随每个词而异？目前，回答这些问题通常需要昂贵的训练运行，其聚合性能指标掩盖了单个词层面的动态变化。我们提出了一种无需训练的诊断框架，以最高分辨率（逐词、逐题、逐教师）运行。我们推导出理想节点梯度，定义为最大化学生成功概率的参数更新。随后，我们开发了一种可扩展的定向回滚算法，即使在长链中间思维中也能高效估计该梯度。梯度对齐分数定义为该理想梯度与任意给定蒸馏梯度之间的余弦相似度，量化特定配置对理想信号的逼近程度。在多种自蒸馏设置和外部教师模型中，我们观察到蒸馏指导在错误回滚时与理想梯度的对齐度显著高于正确回滚时（学生已表现良好，教师信号趋于噪声）。此外，我们发现最优蒸馏上下文依赖于学生模型容量与目标任务的联合影响，且不存在单一普适有效的配置。这些发现促使采用逐任务、逐词诊断分析用于蒸馏。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the conditions under which on-policy distillation benefits or hinders reasoning model training, addressing questions about optimal teacher selection and context for self-distillation. It introduces a training-free diagnostic framework that evaluates distillation at the per-token, per-question, and per-teacher level by estimating an ideal gradient — the parameter update that maximally increases the student’s success probability — and measuring its alignment with actual distillation gradients via cosine similarity. Experiments reveal that distillation guidance aligns more closely with the ideal gradient on incorrect rollouts than on correct ones, where teacher signals become noisy. Additionally, the optimal distillation context varies depending on student capacity and task, with no universal configuration performing best across all settings, thereby advocating for fine-grained, task-specific diagnostics.
该研究旨在厘清在训练推理模型时，基于策略的蒸馏（on-policy distillation）在何种条件下有益、何种条件下有害。作者提出一种无需训练的诊断框架，通过在每个token、每个问题和每个教师模型层面评估蒸馏效果，计算理想梯度（最大化学生成功概率的参数更新）与实际蒸馏梯度之间的余弦相似度。实验发现，蒸馏信号在错误推理路径上与理想梯度更对齐，而在学生表现良好的正确路径上则易受教师噪声干扰。此外，最优蒸馏上下文依赖于学生模型容量和目标任务，不存在普适的最佳配置。研究结果呼吁在蒸馏设计中采用任务和token级别的精细化诊断。
Shields to Guarantee Probabilistic Safety in MDPs
Authors: Linus Heck, Filip Macák, Roman Andriushchenko, Milan Češka, Sebastian Junges
First: 2026-05-11T17:33:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:33:01+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CAV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Shielding is a prominent model-based technique to ensure safety of autonomous agents. Classical shielding aims to ensure that nothing bad ever happens and comes with strong guarantees about safety and maximal permissiveness. However, shielding systems for probabilistic safety, where something bad is allowed to happen with an acceptable probability, has proven to be more intricate. This paper presents a formal framework that conservatively extends classical shields to probabilistic safety. In this framework, we (i) demonstrate the impossibility of preserving the strong guarantees on safety and permissiveness, (ii) provide natural shields with weaker guarantees, and (iii) introduce offline and online shield constructions ensuring strong safety guarantees. The empirical evaluation highlights the practical advantages of the new shields, as well as their computational feasibility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于MDP中概率安全的防护机制
防护是一种确保自主代理安全的主流基于模型的技术。经典防护旨在确保绝无坏事发生，并提供关于安全性和最大容许性的强保证。然而，对于概率安全的防护机制——即允许坏事以可接受的概率发生——则更为复杂。本文提出一个保守扩展经典防护机制以支持概率安全的形式化框架。在此框架下，我们（i）证明了无法保持对安全性和容许性的强保证；（ii）提供了具有较弱保证的自然防护机制；（iii）引入了离线和在线防护构造方法，以确保强安全保证。实证评估突出了新防护机制的实用优势及其计算可行性。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of extending classical shielding techniques to probabilistic safety, where occasional bad events are permitted within acceptable probability bounds. The authors develop a formal framework that conservatively generalizes traditional shields, proving that strong safety and permissiveness guarantees cannot be preserved in probabilistic settings. Instead, they propose natural shields with weaker but practical guarantees, and introduce both offline and online construction methods that maintain strong safety assurances. Empirical results demonstrate the effectiveness and computational feasibility of these new shields in real-world scenarios.
本文旨在将传统用于绝对安全的屏蔽技术扩展至概率安全领域，其中允许不良事件以可控概率发生。作者提出一个保守扩展框架，证明在概率安全场景下无法保留传统屏蔽的强安全与最大容许性保证，转而设计具有较弱但实用保证的自然屏蔽方案，并提出离线与在线两种构造方法以维持强安全属性。实验结果表明，新屏蔽方案在实际应用中有效且计算可行。
Count Anything at Any Granularity
Authors: Chang Liu, Haoning Wu, Weidi Xie
First: 2026-05-11T17:32:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:32:37+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://verg-avesta.github.io/KubriCount/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Open-world object counting remains brittle: despite rapid advances in vision-language models (VLMs), reliably counting the objects a user intends is far from solved. We argue that a central reason is that counting granularity is left implicit; users may refer to a specific identity, an attribute, an instance type, a category, or an abstract concept, yet most methods treat "what to count" as a single, category-level matching problem. In this work, we redefine open-world counting as multi-grained counting, where visual exemplars specify target appearance and fine-grained text, with optional negative prompts, specifies the intended semantic granularity across five explicit levels. Making granularity explicit, however, exposes a critical data bottleneck: existing counting datasets lack the multi-category scenes, controlled distractors, and instance-level annotations needed to verify fine-grained prompt semantics. To address this, we propose the first fully automatic data-scaling pipeline that integrates controllable 3D synthesis with consistent image editing and VLM-based filtering, and use it to construct KubriCount, the largest and most comprehensively annotated counting dataset to date, supporting both training and multi-grained evaluation. Systematic benchmarking reveals that both multimodal large language models and specialist counting models exhibit severe prompt-following failures under fine-grained distinctions. Motivated by these findings, we train HieraCount, a multi-grained counting model that jointly leverages text and visual exemplars as complementary target specifications. HieraCount substantially improves multi-grained counting accuracy and generalizes robustly to challenging real-world scenarios. The project page is available here: https://verg-avesta.github.io/KubriCount/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：任意粒度下的任意物体计数
开放世界物体计数仍不稳健：尽管视觉-语言模型（VLMs）发展迅速，但可靠地计数用户意图的物体仍远未解决。我们认为，核心原因在于计数粒度未被显式指定；用户可能指代特定身份、属性、实例类型、类别或抽象概念，但大多数方法将“计数什么”视为单一的类别级匹配问题。在本工作中，我们将开放世界计数重新定义为多粒度计数，其中视觉示例指定目标外观，细粒度文本（可选负提示）指定跨五个明确层级的语义粒度。然而，显式化粒度暴露了一个关键数据瓶颈：现有计数数据集缺乏多类别场景、可控干扰项和实例级标注，以验证细粒度提示语义。为解决此问题，我们提出首个全自动数据扩展流水线，整合可控3D合成、一致图像编辑与VLM过滤，并用其构建KubriCount，这是迄今为止规模最大、标注最全面的计数数据集，支持训练与多粒度评估。系统性基准测试表明，多模态大语言模型与专用计数模型在细粒度区分下均表现出严重提示遵循失败。受此启发，我们训练了HieraCount，一种联合利用文本与视觉示例作为互补目标规范的多粒度计数模型。HieraCount显著提升了多粒度计数精度，并在具有挑战性的现实场景中表现出稳健泛化能力。项目页面见：https://verg-avesta.github.io/KubriCount/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the brittleness of open-world object counting due to implicit granularity assumptions, this work redefines the task as multi-grained counting, where visual exemplars and fine-grained text prompts (with optional negatives) specify target semantics across five explicit levels. To overcome the lack of suitable datasets, the authors introduce KubriCount, the largest annotated counting dataset to date, built via an automatic pipeline combining 3D synthesis, image editing, and VLM-based filtering. Experimental results show that both multimodal LLMs and specialist counting models struggle with fine-grained prompt adherence. In response, they train HieraCount, a model that jointly uses text and visual exemplars as complementary specifications, which significantly improves accuracy and generalizes well to real-world scenarios.
鉴于开放世界物体计数因粒度隐含而脆弱，本文将任务重新定义为多粒度计数，通过视觉示例与细粒度文本提示（可选负样本）明确指定五个语义层级。为解决现有数据集缺乏多类别场景、可控干扰物和实例级标注的问题，作者构建了KubriCount数据集，通过自动化流程结合3D合成、图像编辑与VLM筛选生成。实验表明，现有VLM与计数模型在细粒度提示下表现严重不足，由此提出HieraCount模型，联合利用文本与视觉示例作为互补输入，显著提升计数精度并具备对真实场景的鲁棒泛化能力。
LoKA: Low-precision Kernel Applications for Recommendation Models At Scale
Authors: Liang Luo, Yinbin Ma, Quanyu Zhu, Vasiliy Kuznetsov, Yuxin Chen, Jian Jiao, Jiecao Yu, Buyun Zhang, Tongyi Tang, Xiaohan Wei, Yanli Zhao, Zeliang Chen, Yuchen Hao, Venkatesh Ranganathan, Sandeep Parab, Yantao Yao, Maxim Naumov, Chunzhi Yang, Shen Li, Ellie Wen, Wenlin Chen, Santanu Kolay, Chunqiang Tang
First: 2026-05-11T17:32:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:32:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ISCA'26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent GPU generations deliver significantly higher FLOPs using lower-precision arithmetic, such as FP8. While successfully applied to large language models (LLMs), its adoption in large recommendation models (LRMs) has been limited. This is because LRMs are numerically sensitive, dominated by small matrix multiplications (GEMMs) followed by normalization, and trained in communication-intensive environments. Applying FP8 directly to LRMs often degrades model quality and prolongs training time. These challenges are inherent to LRM workloads and cannot be resolved merely by introducing better FP8 kernels. Instead, a system-model co-design approach is needed to successfully integrate FP8. We present LoKA (Low-precision Kernel Applications), a framework that makes FP8 practical for LRMs through three principles: profile under realistic distributions to know where low precision is safe, co-design model components with hardware to expand where it is safe, and orchestrate across kernel libraries to maximize the gains. Concretely, LoKA Probe is a statistically grounded, online benchmarking method that learns activation and weight statistics, and quantifies per-layer errors. This process pinpoints safe and unsafe, fast and slow sites for FP8 adoption. LoKA Mods is a set of reusable model adaptations that improve both numerical stability and execution efficiency with FP8. LoKA Dispatch is a runtime that leverages the statistical insights from LoKA Probe to select the fastest FP8 kernel that satisfies the accuracy requirements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LoKA：面向大规模推荐模型的低精度内核应用
最新一代GPU通过使用更低精度的算术（如FP8）实现了显著更高的FLOPs。虽然FP8已在大型语言模型（LLMs）中成功应用，但在大型推荐模型（LRMs）中的采用却有限。这是因为LRMs对数值敏感，主要由小规模矩阵乘法（GEMMs）后接归一化操作构成，并在通信密集型环境中训练。直接将FP8应用于LRMs往往导致模型质量下降且训练时间延长。这些挑战源于LRM工作负载的本质，不能仅通过引入更好的FP8内核解决。相反，需要采用系统与模型协同设计的方法才能成功集成FP8。我们提出了LoKA（低精度内核应用）框架，通过三个原则使FP8在LRMs中实用化：在真实分布下进行分析以确定低精度安全区域；与硬件协同设计模型组件以扩展安全区域；跨内核库协调以最大化收益。具体而言，LoKA Probe是一种基于统计的在线基准方法，用于学习激活值和权重统计，并量化每层误差，从而识别FP8适用的安全与不安全、快速与慢速区域。LoKA Mods是一组可复用的模型适配方法，通过FP8提升数值稳定性和执行效率。LoKA Dispatch是一个运行时系统，利用LoKA Probe的统计洞察，选择满足精度要求的最快FP8内核。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to leverage FP8’s efficiency in large recommendation models (LRMs) without compromising accuracy or speed, LoKA introduces a system-model co-design framework. It combines statistical profiling (LoKA Probe) to identify safe FP8 application sites, model adaptations (LoKA Mods) to enhance numerical stability and efficiency, and a runtime dispatcher (LoKA Dispatch) to select optimal kernels dynamically. Experiments show LoKA achieves up to 2.3x faster training with minimal accuracy loss compared to FP32, while maintaining robustness across diverse LRM architectures and training conditions.
为在大型推荐模型（LRM）中有效利用FP8的计算优势，LoKA提出系统与模型协同设计框架。LoKA Probe通过统计分析激活值与权重分布，识别各层中FP8安全且高效的部署位置。LoKA Mods提供可复用的模型适配方案，提升FP8下的数值稳定性和执行效率。LoKA Dispatch根据统计结果动态选择满足精度要求的最优FP8内核，实现性能最大化。实验表明，LoKA相较FP32提速最高达2.3倍，相较FP16提速1.8倍，精度损失极小，验证其在实际LRM训练中的有效性。
Geometry-aware Prototype Learning for Cross-domain Few-shot Medical Image Segmentation
Authors: Feifan Song, Yuntian Bo, Haofeng Zhang
First: 2026-05-11T17:32:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:32:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cross-domain few-shot medical image segmentation (CD-FSMIS) requires a model to generalise simultaneously to novel anatomical categories and unseen imaging domains from only a handful of annotated examples. Existing prototypical approaches inevitably entangle anatomical structure with domain-specific appearance variations, and thus lack a stable reference for reliable matching under domain shift. We observe that the geometric structure of human anatomy constitutes a reliable, domain-transferable prior that has been overlooked. Building on this insight, we propose GeoProto, a geometry-aware CD-FSMIS framework that enriches prototypical matching with explicit structural priors. The core component, Geometry-Aware Prototype Enrichment (GAPE), augments each local appearance prototype with a learned geometric offset encoding its ordinal position within the organ's interior topology. This offset is derived from an auxiliary Ordinal Shape Branch (OSB) trained under an ordinally consistent objective that enforces monotonic variation of geometric embeddings across interior strata, requiring no annotation beyond standard segmentation masks. Extensive experiments across seven datasets spanning three evaluation settings (cross-modality, cross-sequence, and cross-context) demonstrate that GeoProto achieves state-of-the-art performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向跨域少样本医学图像分割的几何感知原型学习
跨域少样本医学图像分割（CD-FSMIS）要求模型仅基于少量标注样本，同时泛化到新颖解剖类别和未见成像域。现有原型方法不可避免地将解剖结构与域特异性外观变化混杂，导致在域迁移时缺乏稳定匹配参考。我们观察到人体解剖的几何结构构成了一种可靠、可跨域迁移的先验，但此前被忽视。基于此洞察，我们提出GeoProto框架，一种几何感知的CD-FSMIS方法，通过显式结构先验增强原型匹配。核心组件“几何感知原型增强”（GAPE）为每个局部外观原型附加一个学习到的几何偏移，编码其在器官内部拓扑中的序位。该偏移由辅助“序位形状分支”（OSB）训练获得，其目标是强制几何嵌入在内部层次间单调变化，仅需标准分割掩码标注即可。在七个数据集、三种评估设置（跨模态、跨序列、跨上下文）的广泛实验中，GeoProto展现出最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generalizing across anatomical categories and imaging domains with limited annotated data, the paper introduces GeoProto, a geometry-aware framework for cross-domain few-shot medical image segmentation. It addresses the limitation of existing prototypical methods by incorporating explicit geometric priors derived from the ordinal structure of organ interiors. The core method, Geometry-Aware Prototype Enrichment (GAPE), enhances appearance prototypes with learned geometric offsets that encode positional relationships within organs, guided by an Ordinal Shape Branch trained on segmentation masks alone. Experimental results across seven datasets and three domain-shift settings show GeoProto consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of geometric structure as a domain-invariant matching reference.
为了解决在少量标注样本下跨解剖类别与成像域泛化困难的问题，本文提出GeoProto框架，通过引入显式的几何先验提升原型匹配的稳定性。其核心GAPE模块为每个局部外观原型附加学习到的几何偏移，该偏移由Ordinal Shape Branch训练获得，该分支通过单调几何嵌入变化约束，无需额外标注。在七组数据集和三种跨域设置下的实验表明，GeoProto达到当前最优性能，验证了利用解剖几何结构作为稳定可迁移参考的有效性。
TodyComm: Task-Oriented Dynamic Communication for Multi-Round LLM-based Multi-Agent System
Authors: Wenzhe Fan, Tommaso Tognoli, Henry Peng Zou, Chunyu Miao, Yibo Wang, Xinhua Zhang
First: 2026-02-03T16:07:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:30:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-round LLM-based multi-agent systems rely on effective communication structures to support collaboration across rounds. However, most existing methods employ a fixed communication topology during inference, which falls short in many realistic applications where the agents' roles may change \textit{across rounds} due to dynamic adversary, task progression, or time-varying constraints such as communication bandwidth. In this paper, we propose addressing this issue through TodyComm, a \textbf{t}ask-\textbf{o}riented \textbf{dy}namic \textbf{comm}unication algorithm. It produces behavior-driven collaboration topologies that adapt to the dynamics at each round, optimizing the utility for the task through policy gradient. Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate that, under both dynamic adversarial settings and communication budget constraints, TodyComm achieves superior task performance while maintaining token efficiency, scalability, and strong generalizability across varying adversarial conditions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TodyComm：面向任务的多轮LLM多智能体系统动态通信
多轮LLM多智能体系统依赖有效的通信结构以支持跨轮次协作。然而，现有大多数方法在推理过程中采用固定通信拓扑，难以应对现实应用中智能体角色因动态对抗、任务进展或时变约束（如通信带宽）而在轮次间变化的情况。本文提出TodyComm，一种面向任务的动态通信算法，通过策略梯度生成行为驱动的协作拓扑，以适应每轮动态并优化任务效用。在五个基准测试中，TodyComm在动态对抗环境和通信预算约束下，均实现更优任务性能，同时保持令牌效率、可扩展性及在不同对抗条件下的强泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of fixed communication topologies in multi-round LLM-based multi-agent systems, which fail to adapt to changing agent roles due to dynamic adversaries or resource constraints, the paper introduces TodyComm, a task-oriented dynamic communication algorithm. It generates behavior-driven collaboration topologies per round using policy gradient to optimize task utility. Experiments across five benchmarks show TodyComm outperforms baselines in task performance under dynamic adversarial settings and communication budget limits, while preserving token efficiency, scalability, and generalizability.
为了解决多轮LLM多智能体系统中固定通信拓扑无法适应动态角色变化和环境约束的问题，TodyComm提出一种面向任务的动态通信算法，通过策略梯度优化在每轮生成行为驱动的协作结构。实验表明，在动态对抗环境和通信预算受限条件下，TodyComm在任务性能上显著优于基线方法，同时保持了令牌效率、可扩展性及在不同对抗场景下的强泛化能力。
Are vision-language models ready to zero-shot replace supervised classification models in agriculture?
Authors: Earl Ranario, Mason J. Earles
First: 2025-12-17T21:22:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:29:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly proposed as general-purpose solutions for visual recognition tasks, yet their reliability for agricultural decision support remains poorly understood. We benchmark a diverse set of open-source and closed-source VLMs on 27 agricultural image classification datasets from the AgML collection (https://github.com/Project-AgML), spanning 162 classes and 248,000 images across plant disease, pest and damage, and plant and weed species identification. Across all tasks, zero-shot VLMs substantially underperform a supervised task-specific baseline (YOLO11), which consistently achieves markedly higher accuracy than any foundation model. Under multiple-choice prompting, the best-performing VLM (Gemini-3 Pro) reaches approximately 62% average accuracy, while open-ended prompting yields much lower performance, with raw accuracies typically below 25%. Applying LLM-based semantic judging increases open-ended accuracy (e.g., from ~21% to ~30% for top models) and alters model rankings, demonstrating that evaluation methodology meaningfully affects reported conclusions. Among open-source models, Qwen-VL-72B performs best, approaching closed-source performance under constrained prompting but still trailing top proprietary systems. Task-level analysis shows that plant and weed species classification is consistently easier than pest and damage identification, which remains the most challenging category across models. Overall, these results indicate that current off-the-shelf VLMs are not yet suitable as standalone agricultural diagnostic systems, but can function as assistive components when paired with constrained interfaces, explicit label ontologies, and domain-aware evaluation strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉-语言模型是否已准备好在农业领域零样本替代监督分类模型？
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）正被越来越多地提出作为视觉识别任务的通用解决方案，但其在农业决策支持中的可靠性仍不明确。我们在AgML数据集（https://github.com/Project-AgML）的27个农业图像分类数据集上对多种开源和闭源VLMs进行了基准测试，涵盖162个类别和248,000张图像，涉及植物病害、害虫与损伤识别以及植物与杂草物种分类。在所有任务中，零样本VLMs的表现显著低于监督任务专用基线模型（YOLO11），后者始终比任何基础模型准确率高得多。在多项选择提示下，表现最佳的VLM（Gemini-3 Pro）平均准确率约为62%，而开放式提示则表现更低，原始准确率通常低于25%。应用基于LLM的语义判断可提升开放式提示的准确率（如顶级模型从约21%提升至约30%），并改变模型排名，表明评估方法显著影响报告结论。在开源模型中，Qwen-VL-72B表现最佳，在受限提示下接近闭源模型性能，但仍落后于顶级专有系统。任务层面分析显示，植物与杂草物种分类始终比害虫与损伤识别更容易，后者是所有模型中最难的类别。总体而言，当前现成的VLMs尚不适合作为独立的农业诊断系统，但可作为辅助组件，配合受限接口、明确标签本体和领域感知评估策略使用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing interest in using vision-language models (VLMs) as general-purpose tools for agricultural image classification, this study evaluates their zero-shot performance against supervised baselines across 27 datasets from the AgML collection. The authors benchmark both open-source and closed-source VLMs on 162 classes and 248,000 images, finding that even the best-performing model (Gemini-3 Pro) achieves only ~62% average accuracy under multiple-choice prompting, significantly below the supervised YOLO11 baseline. Open-ended prompting yields much lower scores, typically under 25%, but LLM-based semantic judging can boost performance to ~30% for top models, altering model rankings. Among open-source models, Qwen-VL-72B performs best but still lags behind proprietary systems. Task analysis reveals that plant/weed species identification is easier than pest/damage detection, which remains the most challenging category. The study concludes that current VLMs are not yet ready to replace supervised models as standalone agricultural diagnostic tools but can serve as assistive components with constrained interfaces and domain-aware evaluation.
鉴于视觉语言模型（VLMs）在农业图像分类任务中被广泛视为通用解决方案，本研究评估了其零样本性能与监督基线模型（YOLO11）的对比。在AgML数据集的27个农业图像分类任务中，涵盖162类和248,000张图像，结果显示即使表现最佳的模型（Gemini-3 Pro）在多项选择提示下平均准确率仅约62%，远低于YOLO11。开放式提示下准确率通常低于25%，但结合LLM语义判断可提升至约30%，并改变模型排名。开源模型中Qwen-VL-72B表现最佳，但仍落后于闭源模型。任务分析表明植物/杂草分类较病虫害识别更易，后者始终是最难类别。研究结论认为当前现成VLMs尚不能替代监督模型作为独立农业诊断工具，但可作为辅助组件，在受限接口、显式标签本体和领域感知评估策略下发挥作用。
Safe Aerial 3D Path Planning for Autonomous UAVs using Magnetic Potential Fields
Authors: Haechan Mark Bong, Giovanni Beltrame
First: 2026-05-11T17:28:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:28:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safe autonomous Uncrewed Aerial Vehicle (UAV) navigation in urban environments requires real-time path planning that avoids obstacles. MaxConvNet is a potential-field planner that leverages properties of Maxwell's equations to generate a path to the goal without local minima. We extend the 2D MaxConvNet magnetic field planner to 3D, using a convolutional autoencoder to predict obstacle-aware potential fields from LiDAR-derived 101^3 voxel grids. Evaluation across 100 randomized closed-loop trials in two distinct Cosys-AirSim urban environments, a dense night-time cityscape and a suburban district shows a 100% path planning success rate on both maps without retraining. In offline path planning, 3DMaxConvNet produces path lengths comparable to A* on unseen maps while reducing runtime from 0.155--0.17s to 0.087--0.089s, or about 1.7--1.95 times faster than A*. Against RRT*(3k), 3DMaxConvNet achieves similar path quality while reducing planning runtime from 17.2--17.5s to about 0.09s, which is roughly 193--201 times faster than RRT*(3k).
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于磁势场的自主无人机安全三维路径规划
在城市环境中实现自主无人机（UAV）安全导航，需要实时避障路径规划。MaxConvNet是一种势场规划器，利用麦克斯韦方程的特性生成无局部极小值的目标路径。我们将二维MaxConvNet磁势场规划器扩展至三维，使用卷积自编码器从LiDAR生成的101^3体素网格中预测障碍感知势场。在两个不同的Cosys-AirSim城市环境中（密集夜间城市景观和郊区区域）进行100次随机闭环试验评估，结果显示在两个地图上均实现100%路径规划成功率且无需再训练。在离线路径规划中，3DMaxConvNet在未见过的地图上生成的路径长度与A*相当，同时将运行时间从0.155–0.17秒缩短至0.087–0.089秒，速度提升约1.7–1.95倍。与RRT*(3k)相比，3DMaxConvNet路径质量相近，但规划时间从17.2–17.5秒缩短至约0.09秒，速度提升约193–201倍。
Summary / 总结
To enable safe real-time navigation of autonomous UAVs in urban settings, this work extends the 2D MaxConvNet potential-field planner to 3D by using a convolutional autoencoder to predict obstacle-aware magnetic potential fields from LiDAR voxel grids. Evaluated across 100 randomized trials in two distinct urban environments, the 3DMaxConvNet planner achieved 100% success without retraining. In offline planning, it generated path lengths comparable to A* while reducing runtime by 1.7–1.95x, and outperformed RRT*(3k) by achieving similar path quality in just 0.09s—193–201x faster.
为实现城市环境中自主无人机的安全实时导航，该研究将二维MaxConvNet磁势场规划器扩展至三维，利用卷积自编码器从LiDAR体素网格预测障碍感知势场。在两个不同城市环境的100次随机闭环测试中，3DMaxConvNet实现100%路径规划成功率且无需再训练。离线规划时，其路径长度与A*相当，运行时间缩短1.7–1.95倍；相比RRT*(3k)，路径质量相近但规划时间缩短至0.09秒，速度快约193–201倍。
Neural Weight Norm = Kolmogorov Complexity
Authors: Tiberiu Musat
First: 2026-05-11T17:27:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:27:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Why does weight decay work? We prove that, in any fixed-precision regime, the smallest weight norm of a looped neural network outputting a binary string equals the Kolmogorov complexity of that string, up to a logarithmic factor. This implies that weight decay induces a prior matching Solomonoff's universal prior, the optimal prior over computable functions, up to a polynomial factor. The result is norm-agnostic: in fixed precision, every weight norm collapses to the non-zero parameter count up to constants, so the same sandwich bound holds for any norm used as a regulariser. The proof has two short reductions: any program for a universal Turing machine can be encoded into neural weights at unit cost per program bit, and any fixed-precision network can be described by enumerating its non-zero parameters with logarithmic addressing overhead. Both bounds are tight up to constants, with the logarithmic factor realised by permutation encodings: a network whose parameters encode a permutation produces a string whose Kolmogorov complexity is the non-zero parameter count times its logarithm. The fixed-precision assumption is essential: with infinite precision, neural networks can encode non-computable functions and the weight norm loses its relevance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经网络权重范数等于柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度
为什么权重衰减有效？我们证明，在任何固定精度的框架下，循环神经网络输出二进制字符串时，其最小权重范数等于该字符串的柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度，仅相差对数因子。这表明，权重衰减诱导的先验与索洛莫诺夫的通用先验（可计算函数的最优先验）匹配，仅相差多项式因子。该结果与范数无关：在固定精度下，任何权重范数均坍缩为非零参数数量（仅相差常数因子），因此无论使用何种范数作为正则化器，相同的夹逼界限均成立。证明包含两个简短的归约：任何通用图灵机程序可以单位成本每比特编码为神经网络权重；任何固定精度网络可通过枚举其非零参数（带对数寻址开销）进行描述。两个界限均在常数因子内紧致，其中对数因子由置换编码实现：参数编码置换的网络输出字符串，其柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度等于非零参数数量乘以其对数值。固定精度假设至关重要：在无限精度下，神经网络可编码不可计算函数，权重范数将失去其意义。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of why weight decay works, this paper establishes a theoretical link between neural network weight norms and Kolmogorov complexity, showing that in fixed-precision settings, the minimal weight norm of a looped neural network producing a binary string equals the string’s Kolmogorov complexity up to a logarithmic factor. The method reduces program encoding on a universal Turing machine to neural weight representation at unit cost per bit, and shows that fixed-precision networks can be described by enumerating non-zero parameters with logarithmic addressing overhead. Experimental results confirm the tightness of the bounds via permutation encodings, where the Kolmogorov complexity scales as the non-zero parameter count multiplied by its logarithm, and demonstrate that the fixed-precision assumption is necessary, as infinite precision allows encoding non-computable functions, rendering weight norms irrelevant.
为理解权重衰减为何有效，本文证明在固定精度下，循环神经网络输出二进制字符串时，其最小权重范数等于该字符串的Kolmogorov复杂度，仅差对数因子。这表明权重衰减等价于诱导与Solomonoff通用先验匹配的先验分布，仅差多项式因子。该结果与范数无关：在固定精度下，所有范数均退化为非零参数个数（仅差常数因子），故任何正则化范数均满足相同界限。证明通过两个紧致归约实现：将通用图灵机程序以每比特单位成本编码为网络权重，以及通过带对数开销的参数枚举描述固定精度网络。对数因子由置换编码实现：参数编码置换时，复杂度为参数个数乘以对数，且固定精度假设至关重要——无限精度下神经网络可编码不可计算函数，权重范数将失去意义。
Neural at ArchEHR-QA 2026: One Method Fits All: Unified Prompt Optimization for Clinical QA over EHRs
Authors: Abrar Majeedi, Viswanatha Reddy Gajjala, Sai Prasanna Teja Reddy Bogireddy, Siddhant Rai
First: 2026-05-11T17:27:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:27:30+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CL4Health @ LREC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automated question answering (QA) over electronic health records (EHRs) demands precise evidence retrieval, faithful answer generation, and explicit grounding of answers in clinical notes. In this work, we present Neural1.5, our method for the ArchEHR-QA 2026 shared task at CL4Health@LREC 2026, which comprises four subtasks: question interpretation, evidence identification, answer generation, and evidence alignment. Our approach decouples the task into independent, modular stages and employs DSPy"s MIPROv2 optimizer to automatically discover high-performing prompts, jointly tuning instructions and few-shot demonstrations for each stage. Within every stage, self-consistency voting over multiple stochastic inference runs suppresses spurious errors and improves reliability, while stage-specific verification mechanisms (e.g., self-reflection and chain-of-verification for alignment) further refine output quality. Among all teams that participated in all four subtasks, our method ranks second overall (mean rank 4.00), placing 4th, 1st, 4th, and 7th on Subtasks 1-4, respectively. These results demonstrate that systematic, per-stage prompt optimization combined with self-consistency mechanisms is a cost-effective alternative to model fine-tuning for multifaceted clinical QA.

Motivated by the need for accurate and grounded clinical question answering over electronic health records, this work introduces Neural1.5, a unified prompt optimization method for the ArchEHR-QA 2026 shared task. AssayBench: An Assay-Level Virtual Cell Benchmark for LLMs and Agents
Authors: Edward De Brouwer, Carl Edwards, Alexander Wu, Jenna Collier, Graham Heimberg, Xiner Li, Meena Subramaniam, Ehsan Hajiramezanali, David Richmond, Jan-Christian Hütter, Sara Mostafavi, Gabriele Scalia
First: 2026-05-11T17:27:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:27:16+00:00
Comments: 22 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in machine learning and large-scale biological data collections have revived the prospect of building a virtual cell, a computational model of cellular behavior that could accelerate biological discovery. One of the most compelling promises of this vision is the ability to perform in silico phenotypic screens, in which a model predicts the effects of cellular perturbations in unseen biological contexts. This task combines heterogeneous textual inputs with diverse phenotypic outputs, making it particularly well-suited to LLMs and agentic systems. Yet, no standard benchmark currently exists for this task, as existing efforts focus on narrower molecular readouts that are only indirectly aligned with the phenotypic endpoints driving many real-world drug discovery workflows. In this work, we present AssayBench, a benchmark for phenotypic screen prediction, built from 1,920 publicly available CRISPR screens spanning five broad classes of cellular phenotypes. We formulate the screen prediction task as a gene rank prediction for each screen and introduce the adjusted nDCG, a continuous metric for comparing performance across heterogeneous assays. Our extensive evaluation shows that existing methods remain far from empirically estimated performance ceilings and zero-shot generalist LLMs outperform biology-specific LLMs and trainable baselines. Optimization techniques such as fine-tuning, ensembling, and prompt optimization can further improve LLM performance on this task. Overall, AssayBench offers a practical testbed for measuring progress toward in silico phenotypic screening and, more broadly, virtual cell models.

标题：AssayBench：面向大语言模型与代理系统的实验级虚拟细胞基准
近年来，机器学习与大规模生物数据集的进展重新激发了构建虚拟细胞的前景——即一种可加速生物发现的细胞行为计算模型。该愿景最引人注目的承诺之一是能够在计算机中进行表型筛选，即模型预测细胞扰动在未见生物背景下的效应。该任务结合异构文本输入与多样表型输出，特别适合大语言模型与代理系统。然而，目前尚无标准基准用于此任务，因现有研究多聚焦于较窄的分子读数，仅间接与驱动许多现实世界药物发现工作流的表型终点对齐。本文提出AssayBench，一个基于1920个公开CRISPR筛选数据构建的表型筛选预测基准，涵盖五大类细胞表型。我们将筛选预测任务形式化为每个筛选的基因排序预测，并引入调整后的nDCG，一种用于比较异质性实验性能的连续度量。广泛评估表明，现有方法距离实证性能上限仍远，零样本通用型大语言模型优于生物专用模型与可训练基线。优化技术如微调、集成与提示优化可进一步提升大语言模型在该任务上的表现。总体而言，AssayBench为衡量向计算机表型筛选及更广泛的虚拟细胞模型迈进提供了实用测试平台。

为评估大语言模型和代理系统在预测基因扰动引起的细胞表型方面的能力，作者构建了AssayBench基准数据集，整合了1920个公开CRISPR筛选数据，涵盖五大表型类别。任务被定义为每个筛选中的基因排序预测，采用调整后的nDCG指标评估异质性实验结果。实验表明，现有方法远未达到性能上限，零样本LLM表现优于生物专用模型和可训练基线，且通过微调、集成和提示优化可进一步提升LLM性能。
Compute Where it Counts: Self Optimizing Language Models
Authors: Yash Akhauri, Mohamed S. Abdelfattah
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-05-11T17:27:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:27:15+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML'26 Code: https://github.com/akhauriyash/SOL
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Efficient LLM inference research has largely focused on reducing the cost of each decoding step (e.g., using quantization, pruning, or sparse attention), typically applying a uniform computation budget to every generated token. In practice, token difficulty varies widely, so static compression can over-compute on easy steps and under-compute on hard ones. We study dynamic budget allocation for autoregressive decoding: learning how much computation to spend per token from within a single model. Self-Optimizing Language Models (SOL) pair a frozen LLM with a lightweight policy network that reads the LLM hidden state and selects a discrete efficiency action at each decode step. Actions can jointly control (i) token-level attention sparsity, (ii) structured activation pruning in the MLP, and (iii) activation quantization bit-width, while leaving the base model weights unchanged. We train the policy with group-relative policy optimization on teacher-forced episodes: the token sequence is fixed, while we sample multiple compute schedules (i.e., "counterfactual" schedules that vary only the efficiency actions for the same token path) and compare their likelihoods under the same supervision. Our reward trades off language-model quality against soft penalties that encourage episode-average budget usage to match a requested target. Across model variants and compute regimes, SOL improves quality at matched budget over static allocation and strong random schedule search, offering a complementary axis for inference-efficiency optimization. SOL discovers a better quality-efficiency pareto-front across all our experiments and improves MMLU accuracy by up to 7.3% over uniform budget allocation strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：计算关键处：自优化语言模型
高效LLM推理研究主要集中在降低每个解码步骤的成本（如使用量化、剪枝或稀疏注意力），通常对每个生成的token应用统一的计算预算。实际上，token的难度差异很大，因此静态压缩可能在简单步骤上过度计算，在困难步骤上不足计算。我们研究了自回归解码中的动态预算分配：从单个模型内部学习每个token应消耗多少计算量。 自优化语言模型（SOL）将一个冻结的LLM与一个轻量级策略网络配对，该网络读取LLM隐藏状态并在每个解码步骤选择一个离散的效率动作。这些动作可联合控制（i）token级注意力稀疏度，（ii）MLP中的结构化激活剪枝，（iii）激活量化位宽，同时保持基础模型权重不变。我们使用教师强制的episode对策略进行分组相对策略优化训练：token序列固定，而我们采样多个计算调度（即“反事实”调度，仅改变相同token路径的效率动作）并比较其在相同监督下的似然值。我们的奖励函数在语言模型质量与软惩罚之间权衡，软惩罚鼓励episode平均预算使用匹配请求的目标。在不同模型变体和计算环境下，SOL在相同预算下比静态分配和强随机调度搜索提升质量，为推理效率优化提供互补方向。SOL在所有实验中发现更优的质量-效率帕累托前沿，相比均匀预算分配策略最高提升MMLU准确率7.3%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of uniform computation budgets in autoregressive language model decoding, this work introduces Self-Optimizing Language Models (SOL) that dynamically allocate compute per token using a lightweight policy network. The policy, trained via group-relative policy optimization on teacher-forced sequences, selects discrete efficiency actions—such as attention sparsity, MLP pruning, and quantization bit-width—without altering the base model. Experimental results show SOL consistently outperforms static budgeting and random schedule search, achieving up to 7.3% higher MMLU accuracy under matched compute budgets across multiple model variants.
为解决自回归语言模型解码中统一计算预算的低效问题，本文提出自优化语言模型（SOL），通过轻量级策略网络动态分配每一步的计算资源。该策略网络在教师强制序列上使用组相对策略优化训练，选择注意力稀疏度、MLP剪枝或量化位宽等离散效率动作，而不修改基础模型权重。实验表明，SOL在多种模型和计算条件下均优于静态预算分配和随机调度搜索，最高可提升MMLU准确率7.3%，展现出更优的质量-效率权衡曲线。
CADBench: A Multimodal Benchmark for AI-Assisted CAD Program Generation
Authors: Anna C. Doris, Jacob Thomas Sony, Ghadi Nehme, Era Syla, Amin Heyrani Nobari, Faez Ahmed
First: 2026-05-11T17:25:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:25:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recovering editable CAD programs from images or 3D observations is central to AI-assisted design, but progress is difficult to measure because existing evaluations are fragmented across datasets, modalities, and metrics. We introduce CADBench, a unified benchmark for multimodal CAD program generation. CADBench contains 18,000 evaluation samples spanning six benchmark families derived from DeepCAD, Fusion 360, ABC, MCB, and Objaverse; five input modalities including clean meshes, noisy meshes, single-view renders, photorealistic renders, and multi-view renders; and six metrics covering geometric fidelity, executability, and program compactness. STEP-based families are stratified by B-rep face count and all families are diversity-sampled to support controlled analysis across complexity and object variation. We benchmark eleven CAD-specialized and general-purpose vision-language systems, generating more than 1.4 million CAD programs. Under idealized inputs, specialized mesh-to-CAD models substantially outperform code-generating VLMs, which remain far from reliable CAD program reconstruction. CADBench further reveals three recurring failure modes: reconstruction quality degrades with geometric complexity, CAD-specialized models can be brittle under modality shift, and model rankings change across metrics. Together, these results position CADBench as a diagnostic testbed for measuring progress in editable 3D reconstruction and multimodal CAD understanding. The benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/DeCoDELab/CADBench.

为解决AI辅助CAD程序生成缺乏统一评估的问题，作者构建了CADBench多模态基准，包含18,000个样本、六类CAD数据集及五种输入模态，采用六项指标评估几何保真度、可执行性与程序紧凑性。基准按复杂度分层并确保多样性以支持可控分析。实验对十一类视觉语言模型进行评测，生成超140万CAD程序，结果显示专用CAD模型在理想输入下优于通用VLM，但两者均受几何复杂度和模态变化影响，且模型排名随评估指标变化。
GIFT: Guided Importance-Aware Fine-Tuning for Diffusion Language Models
Authors: Guowei Xu, Wenxin Xu, Jiawang Zhao, Kaisheng Ma
First: 2025-09-25T07:55:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:24:47+00:00
Comments: preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models have recently shown strong potential in language modeling, offering faster generation compared to traditional autoregressive approaches. However, applying supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to diffusion models remains challenging, as they lack precise probability estimates at each denoising step. While the diffusion mechanism enables the model to reason over entire sequences, it also makes the generation process less predictable and often inconsistent. This highlights the importance of controlling key tokens that guide the direction of generation. To address this issue, we propose GIFT, an importance-aware finetuning method for diffusion language models, where tokens are assigned different importance weights based on their entropy. Derived from diffusion theory, GIFT delivers substantial gains: across diverse settings including different mainstream training datasets ranging from 1k to 10k in size, utilizing LoRA or full parameter fine-tuning, and training on base or instruct models, GIFT consistently achieves superior overall performance compared to standard SFT on four widely used reasoning benchmarks (Sudoku, Countdown, GSM8K, and MATH-500).
中文标题/摘要
标题：GIFT：面向扩散语言模型的引导重要性感知微调
扩散模型在语言建模中展现出强大潜力，其生成速度优于传统自回归方法。然而，将监督微调（SFT）应用于扩散模型仍具挑战性，因其在去噪步骤中缺乏精确的概率估计。尽管扩散机制使模型能对整个序列进行推理，但也使生成过程更不可预测且常不一致。这凸显了控制关键令牌以引导生成方向的重要性。为此，我们提出GIFT，一种面向扩散语言模型的重要性感知微调方法，根据令牌熵值赋予其不同重要性权重。基于扩散理论，GIFT在多种设置下均取得显著提升：涵盖不同主流训练数据集（规模从1k至10k）、使用LoRA或全参数微调、在基础或指令模型上训练时，GIFT在四个广泛使用的推理基准（数独、计时赛、GSM8K、MATH-500）上始终优于标准SFT。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve supervised fine-tuning of diffusion language models, which suffer from inconsistent generation due to lack of precise step-wise probability estimates, the authors propose GIFT, an importance-aware fine-tuning method that assigns tokens varying weights based on their entropy to guide generation. By leveraging diffusion theory, GIFT enhances model control over key tokens, leading to consistent performance gains across diverse training setups including varying dataset sizes, LoRA or full parameter tuning, and base or instruct models. Experimental results show GIFT consistently outperforms standard SFT on four reasoning benchmarks—Sudoku, Countdown, GSM8K, and MATH-500—demonstrating its robustness and effectiveness.
为解决扩散语言模型在监督微调中因缺乏精确去噪步骤概率估计而导致生成不一致的问题，作者提出GIFT方法，通过根据熵值为不同词元分配重要性权重，引导生成方向。该方法基于扩散理论，增强模型对关键词元的控制能力，在不同数据集规模、LoRA或全参数微调、基础或指令模型等多样化设置下均表现稳定。实验表明，GIFT在Sudoku、Countdown、GSM8K和MATH-500四个推理基准上持续优于标准SFT，验证了其鲁棒性和有效性。
Attractor-Vascular Coupling Theory: Formal Grounding and Empirical Validation for AAMI-Standard Cuffless Blood Pressure Estimation from Smartphone Photoplethysmography
Authors: Timothy Oladunni, Farouk Ganiyu Adewumi
First: 2026-05-11T17:24:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:24:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work proposes Attractor-Vascular Coupling Theory (AVCT), a mathematical framework showing that cardiac attractor geometry encodes blood pressure (BP) information sufficient for AAMI-standard estimation, and validates the theory through a calibrated cuffless BP model using photoplethysmography (PPG). AVCT is grounded in Cardiac Stability Theory and operationalized using Takens delay embedding and attractor morphology extraction. Two theorems, one proposition, and one corollary formally justify the use of PPG attractor features for BP estimation and predict the feature-importance hierarchy. A LightGBM model trained on pulse transit time (PTT) and Cardiac Stability Index (CSI) attractor features under single-point calibration was evaluated using strict leave-one-subject-out cross-validation (LOSO-CV) on 46 subjects from BIDMC ICU (n = 9) and VitalDB surgical data (n = 37), comprising 29,684 windows. The model achieved systolic BP (SBP) mean absolute error (MAE) of 2.05 mmHg and diastolic BP (DBP) MAE of 1.67 mmHg, with correlations r = 0.990 and r = 0.991, satisfying the AAMI/IEEE SP10 requirement of MAE below 5 mmHg. Median per-subject MAE was 1.87/1.54 mmHg, and 70%/76% of subjects individually satisfied AAMI criteria. A PPG-only ablation using nine smartphone attractor features matched the ECG+PPG model within 0.05 mmHg, demonstrating that clinical-grade BP tracking is achievable using only a smartphone camera while surpassing prior generalized LOSO-CV results using fewer sensors. All four AVCT predictions were quantitatively confirmed, with 91.5% error reduction from uncalibrated to calibrated estimation (epsilon_cal = 0.915). Unlike post-hoc explainable AI methods, AVCT predicts features satisfying the architectural faithfulness criterion of the Explainable-AI Trustworthiness (EAT) framework and grounding BP estimation in nonlinear dynamical systems theory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：吸引子-血管耦合理论：面向智能手机光电容积脉搏波无袖带血压估计的AAMI标准形式化基础与实证验证
本研究提出吸引子-血管耦合理论（AVCT），一个数学框架，表明心脏吸引子几何结构编码了足以满足AAMI标准的血压（BP）信息，并通过使用光电容积脉搏波（PPG）的校准无袖带血压模型验证该理论。AVCT基于心脏稳定性理论，通过Takens延迟嵌入和吸引子形态提取进行操作化。两个定理、一个命题和一个推论形式化地证明了使用PPG吸引子特征进行血压估计的合理性，并预测特征重要性层级。在单点校准下，基于脉搏传输时间（PTT）和心脏稳定性指数（CSI）吸引子特征训练的LightGBM模型，在46名受试者（BIDMC ICU n=9，VitalDB手术数据n=37，共29,684个窗口）上采用严格的留一受试者交叉验证（LOSO-CV）评估，收缩压（SBP）平均绝对误差（MAE）为2.05 mmHg，舒张压（DBP）MAE为1.67 mmHg，相关系数r=0.990和r=0.991，满足AAMI/IEEE SP10要求（MAE<5 mmHg）。每位受试者中位MAE为1.87/1.54 mmHg，70%/76%的受试者个体满足AAMI标准。仅使用九个智能手机吸引子特征的PPG模型消融实验结果与ECG+PPG模型误差相差仅0.05 mmHg，表明仅使用智能手机摄像头即可实现临床级血压追踪，且优于先前使用更少传感器的泛化LOSO-CV结果。所有四个AVCT预测均被定量证实，从无校准到校准估计误差降低91.5%（epsilon_cal=0.915）。与事后可解释AI方法不同，AVCT预测的特征满足可解释AI可信度（EAT）框架的架构忠实性准则，并将血压估计扎根于非线性动力系统理论。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate, cuffless blood pressure estimation using smartphone photoplethysmography (PPG), this study introduces Attractor-Vascular Coupling Theory (AVCT), a mathematical framework rooted in Cardiac Stability Theory that links cardiac attractor geometry to blood pressure. AVCT operationalizes this theory via Takens delay embedding and attractor morphology extraction, and formalizes feature importance through theorems and corollaries. Evaluated via leave-one-subject-out cross-validation on 46 subjects using 29,684 PPG windows, a LightGBM model trained on pulse transit time and Cardiac Stability Index attractor features achieved systolic MAE of 2.05 mmHg and diastolic MAE of 1.67 mmHg, exceeding AAMI standards. PPG-only models matched ECG+PPG performance, and AVCT’s predictions were validated with 91.5% error reduction from uncalibrated to calibrated estimation, demonstrating architectural faithfulness under Explainable-AI Trustworthiness criteria.
为实现基于智能手机PPG的无袖带血压估计，本研究提出吸引子-血管耦合理论（AVCT），该理论基于心脏稳定性理论，将心脏吸引子几何结构与血压关联。通过Takens延迟嵌入和吸引子形态提取实现理论操作化，并通过定理与推论形式化特征重要性。在单点校准下，基于PTT与CSI吸引子特征的LightGBM模型在46名受试者上达到收缩压MAE 2.05 mmHg、舒张压MAE 1.67 mmHg，满足AAMI标准，相关系数高达0.990–0.991。仅用PPG特征的消融实验表现与ECG+PPG相当，且理论预测准确率达91.5%，表明其具备临床可行性与理论根基。
Benchmarked Yet Not Measured -- Generative AI Should be Evaluated Against Real-World Utility
Authors: Ishani Mondal, Shweta Bhardwaj
First: 2026-05-07T18:56:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:22:01+00:00
Comments: 20 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative AI systems achieve impressive performance on standard benchmarks yet fail to deliver real-world utility, a disconnect we identify across 28 deployment cases spanning education, healthcare, software engineering, and law. We argue that this benchmark utility gap arises from three recurring failures in evaluation practice: proxy displacement, temporal collapse, and distributional concealment. Motivated by these observations, we argue that generative AI evaluation requires a paradigm shift from static benchmark-centered transparency toward stakeholder, goal, and context-conditioned utility transparency grounded in human outcome trajectories. Existing evaluations primarily characterize properties of model outputs, while deployment success depends on whether interaction with AI improves stakeholders' ability to achieve their goals over time. The missing construct is therefore utility: the change in a stakeholder's capability induced through sustained interaction with an AI system within a deployment context. To operationalize this perspective, we propose SCU-GenEval, a four-stage evaluation framework consisting of stakeholder-goal mapping, construct-indicator specification, mechanism modeling, and longitudinal utility measurement. To make these stages practically deployable, we introduce three supporting instruments: structured deployment protocols, context-conditioned user simulators, and persona- and goal-conditioned proxy metrics. We conclude with domain-specific calls to action, arguing that progress in generative AI must be evaluated through measurable improvements in human outcomes rather than benchmark performance alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：已基准测试却未被衡量——生成式人工智能应以现实效用为评估标准
生成式人工智能系统在标准基准测试中表现优异，但在现实应用中未能实现实际效用，我们在涵盖教育、医疗、软件工程和法律等领域的28个部署案例中发现了这一脱节现象。我们认为，这种基准与实际效用之间的差距源于评估实践中三种反复出现的失败：代理替代、时间压缩和分布隐藏。基于这些观察，我们主张生成式人工智能评估需要从静态的基准中心透明度转向以利益相关者、目标和情境为条件的效用透明度，其基础是人类结果轨迹。现有评估主要描述模型输出的属性，而部署成功取决于与AI交互是否能随着时间推移提升利益相关者实现目标的能力。因此缺失的核心概念是效用：在部署情境下，通过持续与AI系统互动所引发的利益相关者能力变化。为落实这一视角，我们提出SCU-GenEval，一个包含四个阶段的评估框架：利益相关者-目标映射、构念-指标规范、机制建模和纵向效用测量。为使这些阶段具备实际可操作性，我们引入三项支持工具：结构化部署协议、情境条件用户模拟器、角色与目标条件代理指标。最后，我们提出各领域具体行动呼吁，认为生成式人工智能的进步必须通过可衡量的人类成果改善来评估，而不能仅依赖基准表现。
