The paper argues that generative AI systems, despite strong benchmark performance, often fail to deliver real-world utility, as shown across 28 deployment cases in education, healthcare, and other domains. The authors identify three key flaws in current evaluation: proxy displacement, temporal collapse, and distributional concealment, which lead to a disconnect between benchmark scores and actual stakeholder outcomes. To address this, they propose SCU-GenEval, a four-stage framework that maps stakeholder goals, specifies utility indicators, models interaction mechanisms, and measures longitudinal utility changes. Supporting tools include deployment protocols, context-aware user simulators, and proxy metrics conditioned on personas and goals. Experimental results across domains show that utility-focused evaluation better predicts deployment success than benchmark scores alone, emphasizing the need to measure human capability change over time rather than static model outputs.
该论文指出，尽管生成式AI在标准基准上表现优异，但在实际应用中却难以产生真实效用，这一现象在教育、医疗、软件工程和法律等28个部署案例中普遍存在。作者认为当前评估存在三大缺陷：代理替代、时间压缩和分布隐藏，主张评估应从静态基准转向以用户目标、情境和长期结果为导向的效用评估。为此，提出SCU-GenEval四阶段框架，涵盖利益相关者目标映射、效用指标定义、机制建模与长期效用测量，并配套部署协议、情境化用户模拟器和目标导向代理指标。实验表明，以效用为核心的评估更能预测实际应用成效，而非单纯依赖基准分数。
Remember the Decision, Not the Description: A Rate-Distortion Framework for Agent Memory
Authors: Mingxi Zou, Zhihan Guo, Langzhang Liang, Zhuo Wang, Qifan Wang, Qingsong Wen, Irwin King, Lizhen Qu, Zenglin Xu
First: 2026-05-11T17:20:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:20:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-horizon language agents must operate under limited runtime memory, yet existing memory mechanisms often organize experience around descriptive criteria such as relevance, salience, or summary quality. For an agent, however, memory is valuable not because it faithfully describes the past, but because it preserves the distinctions between histories that must remain separated under a fixed budget to support good decisions. We cast this as a decision-centric rate-distortion problem, measuring memory quality by the loss in achievable decision quality induced by compression. This yields an exact forgetting boundary for what can be safely forgotten, and a memory-distortion frontier characterizing the optimal tradeoff between memory budget and decision quality. Motivated by this decision-centric view of memory, we propose DeMem, an online memory learner that refines its partition only when data certify that a shared state would induce decision conflict, and prove near-minimax regret guarantees. On both controlled synthetic diagnostics and long-horizon conversational benchmarks, DeMem yields consistent gains under the same runtime budget, supporting the principle that memory should preserve the distinctions that matter for decisions, not descriptions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：记住决策而非描述：面向代理记忆的率失真框架
长时程语言代理必须在有限运行时内存下工作，但现有记忆机制常围绕相关性、显著性或摘要质量等描述性标准组织经验。然而，对代理而言，记忆的价值不在于忠实地描述过去，而在于保留必须在固定预算下分离的历史差异，以支持良好决策。我们将此视为以决策为中心的率失真问题，通过压缩导致的决策质量损失衡量记忆质量。这给出了可安全遗忘内容的精确遗忘边界，以及刻画内存预算与决策质量之间最优权衡的记忆失真前沿。受此决策中心化记忆观启发，我们提出DeMem，一种在线记忆学习器，仅当数据证明共享状态将引发决策冲突时才调整其分区，并证明其近最小最大遗憾保证。在受控合成诊断和长时程对话基准测试中，DeMem在相同运行时预算下持续表现更优，支持了记忆应保留对决策至关重要的区分性而非描述性的原则。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient memory in long-horizon language agents, this work proposes a decision-centric rate-distortion framework that evaluates memory quality by its impact on decision performance rather than descriptive fidelity. The method, DeMem, dynamically refines memory partitions only when data indicate potential decision conflicts, and is theoretically grounded in near-minimax regret guarantees. Experiments on synthetic and conversational benchmarks show consistent performance gains under fixed runtime budgets, demonstrating that memory should prioritize preserving decision-relevant distinctions over descriptive accuracy.
为提升长时程语言代理在有限内存下的决策能力，本文提出以决策为中心的率失真框架，用压缩导致的决策质量损失衡量记忆价值，而非描述准确性。提出DeMem在线记忆学习器，仅在共享状态可能引发决策冲突时动态调整记忆分区，并证明其近最小最大遗憾保证。在合成与对话基准测试中，相同运行预算下表现持续优于基线，验证记忆应保留对决策关键的区分信息而非描述性内容。
BEACON: A Multimodal Dataset for Learning Behavioral Fingerprints from Gameplay Data
Authors: Ishpuneet Singh, Gursmeep Kaur, Uday Pratap Singh Atwal, Guramrit Singh, Gurjot Singh, Maninder Singh
First: 2026-05-11T17:17:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:17:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Continuous authentication in high-stakes digital environments requires datasets with fine-grained behavioral signals under realistic cognitive and motor demands. But current benchmarks are often limited by small scale, unimodal sensing or lack of synchronised environmental context. To address this gap, this paper introduces BEACON ( Behavioral Engine for Authentication \& Continuous Monitoring), a large-scale multimodal dataset that captures diverse skill tiers in competitive \textit{Valorant} gameplay. BEACON contains approximately 430 GB of synchronised modality data (461 GB total on-disk including auxiliary \textit{Valorant} configuration captures) from 79 sessions across 28 distinct players, estimated at 102.51 hours of active gameplay, including high-frequency mouse dynamics, keystroke events, network packet captures, screen recordings, hardware metadata, and in-game configuration context. BEACON leverages the high precision motor skills and high cognitive load that are inherent to tactical shooters, making it a rigorous stress test for the robustness of behavioral biometrics. The dataset allows for the study of continuous authentication, behavioral profiling, user drift and multimodal representation learning in a high-fidelity esports setting. The authors release the dataset and code on Hugging Face and GitHub to create a reproducible benchmark for evaluating next-generation behavioral fingerprinting and security models

BRIDGE: Background Routing and Isolated Discrete Gating for Coarse-Mask Local Editing
Authors: Peilin Xiong, Honghui Yuan, Junwen Chen, Keiji Yanai
First: 2026-05-08T15:09:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:16:17+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Coarse-mask local image editing asks a model to modify a user-indicated region while preserving the surrounding scene. In practice, however, rough masks often become unintended shape priors: instead of serving as flexible edit support, the mask can pull the generated object toward its accidental boundary. We study this failure as mask-shape bias and frame the task through a Two-Zone Constraint, where the background should remain stable while the editable region should follow the instruction without being forced to inherit the mask contour. BRIDGE addresses this setting by keeping masks outside the DiT backbone for support construction and blending, avoiding DiT-internal mask injection and copied control branches. It uses BridgePath generation, where a Main Path preserves background context and a Subject Path generates editable content from independent noise. Motivated by a diagnostic Qwen-Image experiment showing that positional embeddings and attention connectivity regulate which image context visual tokens reuse, BRIDGE introduces a learnable Discrete Geometric Gate for token-level positional-embedding routing. This gate lets subject tokens borrow background-anchored coordinates near fusion regions or keep subject-centric coordinates for geometric freedom. We evaluate BRIDGE on BRIDGE-Bench, MagicBrush, and ICE-Bench. On BRIDGE-Bench, BRIDGE improves Local SigLIP2-T from 0.262 with FLUX.1-Fill and 0.390 with ACE++ to 0.503, with parallel gains in local DINO and DreamSim. Zero-shot results on MagicBrush and ICE-Bench further indicate competitive alignment and source preservation beyond the curated benchmark, while the added routing module remains compact at 13.31M parameters compared with ControlNet-style copied branches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BRIDGE：用于粗掩码局部编辑的背景路由与孤立离散门控
粗掩码局部图像编辑要求模型在修改用户指定区域的同时保留周围场景。然而在实践中，粗糙掩码常意外成为形状先验：掩码不仅未能提供灵活的编辑支持，反而会将生成对象拉向其偶然边界。我们将此失败归因于掩码形状偏置，并通过双区约束框架任务：背景应保持稳定，而可编辑区域应遵循指令而不被强制继承掩码轮廓。BRIDGE通过将掩码保留在DiT主干之外用于支持构建与融合，避免DiT内部掩码注入和复制控制分支。它采用BridgePath生成机制，其中主路径保留背景上下文，主体路径从独立噪声生成可编辑内容。受诊断Qwen-Image实验启发（显示位置嵌入与注意力连接调节视觉标记重用的图像上下文），BRIDGE引入可学习的离散几何门控，用于标记级位置嵌入路由。该门控允许主体标记在融合区域附近借用背景锚定坐标，或保持主体中心坐标以实现几何自由度。我们在BRIDGE-Bench、MagicBrush和ICE-Bench上评估BRIDGE。在BRIDGE-Bench上，BRIDGE将Local SigLIP2-T从使用FLUX.1-Fill时的0.262和ACE++时的0.390提升至0.503，同时在局部DINO和DreamSim上实现并行增益。在MagicBrush和ICE-Bench上的零样本结果进一步表明，其在超出精选基准的对齐与源保留方面具有竞争力，而新增的路由模块相比ControlNet风格的复制分支仅需13.31M参数，保持紧凑。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue that coarse masks often impose unintended shape priors during local image editing, BRIDGE introduces a Two-Zone Constraint to decouple background stability from editable region generation. It avoids injecting masks into the DiT backbone and instead uses BridgePath: a Main Path for background context and a Subject Path for editable content from independent noise. To enhance token-level control, BRIDGE adds a learnable Discrete Geometric Gate that routes positional embeddings to either background-anchored or subject-centric coordinates, enabling flexible geometric editing. Evaluated on BRIDGE-Bench, MagicBrush, and ICE-Bench, BRIDGE significantly improves local SigLIP2-T scores from 0.262 to 0.503, with parallel gains in DINO and DreamSim, while maintaining a compact 13.31M parameter count compared to ControlNet-style architectures.
为了解决粗略用户掩码常导致生成物体被错误边界引导的问题，BRIDGE提出双区约束机制，分离背景稳定与可编辑区域的忠实性。它避免将掩码注入DiT主干，转而采用BridgePath生成：主路径保持背景上下文，子路径从独立噪声生成内容。为增强令牌级控制，BRIDGE引入可学习的离散几何门控机制，动态路由位置嵌入至背景锚定或主体中心坐标，实现几何自由编辑。在BRIDGE-Bench、MagicBrush和ICE-Bench上评估，BRIDGE将Local SigLIP2-T分数从0.262提升至0.503，同时在DINO和DreamSim上同步提升，零样本表现优异，且模型仅13.31M参数，远小于ControlNet风格的复制分支。
When Less is More: The LLM Scaling Paradox in Context Compression
Authors: Ruishan Guo, Yibing Liu, Guoxin Ma, Yan Wang, Yueyang Zhang, Long Xia, Kecheng Chen, Zhiyuan Sun, Daiting Shi
First: 2026-02-10T13:49:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:16:12+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 7 figures, conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scaling up model parameters has long been a prevalent training paradigm driven by the assumption that larger models yield superior generation capabilities. However, under lossy context compression in a compressor--decoder setup, we find a \textbf{\textit{Size-Fidelity Paradox}}: increasing compressor size can lessen the faithfulness of reconstructed contexts though reconstruction error decreases. Across 27 compressor setups spanning model families, scales, and compression rates, we coin this paradox arising from two dominant factors: 1) \textit{knowledge overwriting}: larger models increasingly replace source facts with their own prior beliefs, \textit{e.g.}, ``the white strawberry`` $\to$ ``the red strawberry``; and 2) \textit{semantic drift}: larger models tend to paraphrase or restructure content instead of reproducing it verbatim, \textit{e.g.}, ``Alice hit Bob`` $\to$ ``Bob hit Alice``. Interestingly, this paradox persists across varied settings, with mid-sized compressors often outperforming larger ones in faithful recovery. By analyzing the compressed memory via embedding geometry and reconstruction determinacy, we further reveal that compressors tend to organize memory across broader semantic subspaces, yielding more ambiguous representations prone to overwriting, drift, and weakened recovery. These findings complement existing evaluations of context compression and expose a breakdown of scaling laws when the objective shifts from plausible generation to faithful preservation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：少即是多：上下文压缩中的大语言模型缩放悖论
长期以来，增加模型参数规模被视为主流训练范式，其背后假设是更大的模型能带来更优的生成能力。然而，在压缩器-解码器设置下进行有损上下文压缩时，我们发现了一种\textbf{\textit{规模-保真度悖论}}：增大压缩器规模虽可降低重建误差，却可能降低重建上下文的忠实度。在涵盖27种不同压缩器设置（涵盖不同模型家族、规模和压缩率）的实验中，该悖论源于两个主要因素：1) \textit{知识覆盖}：更大模型更倾向于用自身先验信念替代原始事实，\textit{例如}，``白草莓`` $\to$ ``红草莓``；2) \textit{语义漂移}：更大模型倾向于改写或重组内容而非逐字复现，\textit{例如}，``爱丽丝打了鲍勃`` $\to$ ``鲍勃打了爱丽丝``。有趣的是，该悖论在不同设置下均存在，中等规模压缩器往往在忠实重建方面优于大型压缩器。通过分析压缩记忆的嵌入几何结构与重建确定性，我们进一步揭示压缩器倾向于在更广泛的语义子空间中组织记忆，导致更模糊的表示，易引发覆盖、漂移及重建能力减弱。这些发现补充了现有上下文压缩评估，并揭示当目标从合理生成转向忠实保留时，缩放定律的失效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that larger language models may not always improve context fidelity under compression, this study investigates the 'Size-Fidelity Paradox'—where increasing compressor size reduces faithfulness despite lowering reconstruction error. Through experiments across 27 compressor configurations, the authors identify two key mechanisms: knowledge overwriting, where larger models replace factual content with prior beliefs, and semantic drift, where models paraphrase rather than reproduce text verbatim. Counterintuitively, mid-sized compressors often outperform larger ones in faithful recovery. Analysis of embedding geometry and reconstruction determinacy reveals that larger compressors organize memory across broader semantic spaces, leading to ambiguous representations that exacerbate overwriting and drift, thereby undermining faithful preservation.
该研究旨在探究在上下文压缩场景下，模型规模扩大是否真的能提升生成质量。研究发现‘规模-保真度悖论’：压缩器规模越大，重建上下文的忠实度反而下降，尽管重建误差降低。通过27种压缩器配置的实验，作者识别出两个核心机制：知识覆盖（模型用自身先验覆盖原始事实）和语义漂移（模型改写而非原样复现内容）。有趣的是，中等规模压缩器在忠实度上表现更优。进一步分析表明，大模型压缩器倾向于在更宽泛的语义空间组织记忆，导致表示模糊，加剧覆盖与漂移，从而破坏了传统规模定律在忠实性目标下的适用性。
Workspace-Bench 1.0: Benchmarking AI Agents on Workspace Tasks with Large-Scale File Dependencies
Authors: Zirui Tang, Xuanhe Zhou, Yumou Liu, Linchun Li, Weizheng Wang, Hongzhang Huang, Jun Zhou, Jiachen Song, Shaoli Yu, Jinqi Wang, Zihang Zhou, Hongyi Zhou, Yuting Lv, Jinyang Li, Jiashuo Liu, Ruoyu Chen, Chunwei Liu, GuoLiang Li, Jihua Kang, Fan Wu
First: 2026-05-05T10:17:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:14:45+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Workspace learning requires AI agents to identify, reason over, exploit, and update explicit and implicit dependencies among heterogeneous files in a worker's workspace, enabling them to complete both routine and advanced tasks effectively. Despite its importance, existing relevant benchmarks largely evaluate agents on pre-specified or synthesized files with limited real-world dependencies, leaving workspace-level evaluation underexplored. To this end, we introduce Workspace-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating AI agents on Workspace Learning invOlving Large-Scale File Dependencies. We construct realistic workspaces with 5 worker profiles, 74 file types, 20,476 files (up to 20GB) and curate 388 tasks, each with its own file dependency graph, evaluated across 7,399 total rubrics that require cross-file retrieval, contextual reasoning, and adaptive decision-making. We further provide Workspace-Bench-Lite, a 100-task subset that preserves the benchmark distribution while reducing evaluation costs by about 70%. We evaluate 3 popular agent harnesses and 5 foundation models. Experimental results show that current agents remain far from reliable workspace learning, where the best reaches only about 60%, substantially below the human result of 80.7%, and the average performance across agents is only 45.1%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Workspace-Bench 1.0：基于大规模文件依赖的AI代理工作区任务基准测试
工作区学习要求AI代理识别、推理、利用并更新工作者工作区中异构文件间的显式与隐式依赖关系，从而有效完成常规与高级任务。尽管其重要性显著，现有相关基准测试大多仅评估预设或合成文件，且依赖关系有限，导致工作区级评估尚未充分展开。为此，我们提出Workspace-Bench，用于评估涉及大规模文件依赖的工作区学习的AI代理。我们构建了包含5种工作者画像、74种文件类型、20,476个文件（最高达20GB）的现实工作区，并精心设计388项任务，每项任务均配备独立的文件依赖图，总计7,399项评估标准，涵盖跨文件检索、上下文推理与自适应决策能力。此外，我们还提供Workspace-Bench-Lite，即100项任务的子集，保留基准分布的同时将评估成本降低约70%。我们评估了3种流行代理框架及5种基础模型。实验结果表明，当前代理在工作区学习方面仍远未可靠，表现最佳者仅约60%，显著低于人类80.7%的水平，平均性能仅为45.1%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate AI agents on realistic workspace tasks involving complex file dependencies, the authors introduce Workspace-Bench 1.0, a benchmark featuring 5 worker profiles, 74 file types, 20,476 files, and 388 tasks with detailed dependency graphs. The benchmark assesses agents across 7,399 rubrics requiring cross-file reasoning and adaptive decision-making. Evaluating three agent frameworks and five foundation models, results show that even the best agent achieves only 60% performance, significantly below the human benchmark of 80.7%, while the average agent performance is just 45.1%.
为评估AI代理在真实工作空间中处理复杂文件依赖的能力，本研究构建了Workspace-Bench 1.0基准，包含5种用户角色、74类文件、20,476个文件和388个任务，每个任务配有文件依赖图并由7,399项评估标准衡量，涵盖跨文件检索、上下文推理与自适应决策。实验表明，表现最好的代理仅达到60%准确率，远低于人类80.7%的水平，所有评估代理平均仅45.1%，凸显当前AI在工作空间学习能力上的显著不足。
NoTVLA: Semantics-Preserving Robot Adaptation via Narrative Action Interfaces
Authors: Zheng Huang, Mingyu Liu, Xiaoyi Lin, Muzhi Zhu, Canyu Zhao, Zongze Du, Ye Lin, Xiaoman Li, Yiduo Jia, Hao Zhong, Hao Chen, Chunhua Shen
First: 2025-10-04T18:26:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:14:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models represent a pivotal advance in embodied intelligence, yet they confront critical barriers to real-world deployment, most notably catastrophic forgetting. This issue stems from their overreliance on continuous action sequences or action chunks, which inadvertently create isolated data silos that disrupt knowledge retention across tasks. To tackle these challenges, we propose the Narrowing of Trajectory VLA (NoTVLA) framework: a novel approach that narrows its focus to sparse trajectories, thereby avoiding the catastrophic forgetting associated with dense trajectory fine-tuning. A key innovation of NoTVLA lies in its trajectory planning strategy: instead of centering on the target object's trajectory, it leverages temporal compression and spatial reasoning pruning specifically for the robot end effector's trajectory. Furthermore, training is conducted using these sparse trajectories rather than dense action trajectories, an optimization that delivers remarkable practical advantages with better performance in zero-shot. In multi-task evaluation scenarios, NoTVLA achieves superior performance and generalization compared to pi0 while operating under two critical constraints: it uses over an order of magnitude less computing power than pi0 and requires no wrist-mounted camera. This design ensures that NoTVLA's operational accuracy closely approximates that of single-task expert models. Crucially, it also preserves the model's inherent language capabilities, enabling zero-shot generalization in specific scenarios, supporting unified model deployment across multiple robot platforms, and fostering a degree of generalization even when perceiving tasks from novel perspectives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NoTVLA：通过叙事动作接口实现语义保持的机器人自适应
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型是具身智能的关键进展，但其在实际部署中面临重大障碍，尤其是灾难性遗忘问题。该问题源于其对连续动作序列或动作块的过度依赖，无意中形成孤立数据孤岛，破坏跨任务知识保留。为应对这些挑战，我们提出窄轨迹VLA（NoTVLA）框架：一种聚焦稀疏轨迹的新方法，从而避免密集轨迹微调带来的灾难性遗忘。NoTVLA的核心创新在于其轨迹规划策略：不以目标物体轨迹为中心，而是针对机器人末端执行器轨迹进行时序压缩与空间推理剪枝。此外，训练采用稀疏轨迹而非密集动作轨迹，这一优化在零样本场景下表现卓越，且具备显著实用优势。在多任务评估中，NoTVLA在两项关键约束下优于pi0：计算量仅为pi0的十倍以下，且无需腕部摄像头。该设计确保NoTVLA的运行精度接近单任务专家模型，同时保留模型固有的语言能力，支持特定场景下的零样本泛化、多机器人平台统一部署，并在从新颖视角感知任务时仍具备一定程度的泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the catastrophic forgetting issue in Vision-Language-Action models caused by dense trajectory fine-tuning, the NoTVLA framework introduces a semantics-preserving adaptation method by focusing on sparse trajectories for robot action planning. Instead of tracking target object motion, it employs temporal compression and spatial pruning tailored to the robot’s end effector trajectory, enabling efficient training with sparse data. Experimental results show that NoTVLA outperforms pi0 in multi-task settings while consuming over ten times less compute and requiring no wrist-mounted camera, achieving performance comparable to single-task experts and preserving language capabilities for zero-shot generalization across platforms and novel viewpoints.
为解决视觉-语言-动作模型因密集轨迹微调导致的灾难性遗忘问题，NoTVLA提出聚焦机器人末端执行器轨迹的稀疏轨迹框架，通过时序压缩与空间剪枝避免数据孤岛。在稀疏轨迹上训练后，该模型在多任务场景下零样本表现优于pi0，计算开销低一个数量级且无需腕部相机。其设计保留语言能力，支持跨平台部署，并在面对新视角任务时仍能保持较高泛化精度。
ReaMOT: A Benchmark and Framework for Reasoning-based Multi-Object Tracking
Authors: Sijia Chen, Yanqiu Yu, En Yu, Wenbing Tao
First: 2025-05-26T17:55:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:14:02+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/chen-si-jia/ReaMOT
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Referring Multi-Object Tracking (RMOT) aims to track targets specified by language instructions. However, existing RMOT paradigms heavily rely on explicit visual-textual matching and consequently fail to generalize to complex instructions that require logical reasoning. To overcome this, we propose Reasoning-based Multi-Object Tracking (ReaMOT), a novel task that elevates tracking to a cognitive level, requiring models to infer and track specific targets satisfying implicit constraints via logical reasoning. To advance this field, we construct the ReaMOT Challenge, a comprehensive benchmark featuring a tailored metric suite and a large scale dataset. This dataset comprises 1,156 language instructions, 423,359 image language pairs, and 869 distinct video sequences systematically categorized into six distinct evaluation scenarios, with over 75\% of the instructions dedicated to High Level Reasoning. Furthermore, recognizing that traditional trackers lack cognitive capacity while direct application of Large Vision-Language Model (LVLM) yields severe temporal inconsistencies, we propose ReaTrack. Driven by the insight to decouple high-level cognitive localization from low-level physical motion continuity, this training-free framework dynamically aligns the semantic detections of a Thinking-variant LVLM with the robust motion priors of SAM2. Extensive experiments on the ReaMOT Challenge benchmark demonstrate that ReaTrack establishes a new leading performance standard. Notably, it achieves a more than threefold improvement in RHOTA on the High Level Reasoning subset. Our dataset and code will be available at https://github.com/chen-si-jia/ReaMOT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReaMOT：基于推理的多目标跟踪基准与框架
指代多目标跟踪（RMOT）旨在根据语言指令跟踪指定目标。然而，现有RMOT范式严重依赖显式的视觉-文本匹配，因而难以泛化到需要逻辑推理的复杂指令。为解决此问题，我们提出基于推理的多目标跟踪（ReaMOT），一种将跟踪提升至认知层面的新任务，要求模型通过逻辑推理推断并跟踪满足隐式约束的特定目标。为推动该领域发展，我们构建了ReaMOT挑战赛，包含定制化评估指标和大规模数据集。该数据集包含1,156条语言指令、423,359个图像-语言对及869个不同视频序列，系统划分为六类评估场景，其中超过75%的指令涉及高级推理。此外，考虑到传统跟踪器缺乏认知能力，而直接应用大视觉-语言模型（LVLM）会导致严重的时间不一致性，我们提出ReaTrack。该训练免框架基于“将高级认知定位与低级物理运动连续性解耦”的洞察，动态对齐Thinking变体LVLM的语义检测与SAM2的鲁棒运动先验。在ReaMOT挑战赛上的大量实验表明，ReaTrack建立了新的性能标杆。尤其在高级推理子集上，其RHOTA指标提升超过三倍。我们的数据集与代码将在https://github.com/chen-si-jia/ReaMOT发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing RMOT methods that rely on explicit visual-textual matching and fail with complex reasoning instructions, the paper introduces ReaMOT, a new task requiring models to track targets via logical inference rather than direct matching. To evaluate this, the authors build a large-scale benchmark with 1,156 instructions, 423,359 image-language pairs, and 869 video sequences, 75% of which involve high-level reasoning. They propose ReaTrack, a training-free framework that combines a thinking-enabled LVLM with SAM2’s motion priors to resolve temporal inconsistencies, decoupling semantic reasoning from physical motion continuity. Experiments show ReaTrack achieves over threefold improvement in RHOTA on high-level reasoning tasks, setting a new performance standard.
为解决现有多目标跟踪方法依赖显式视觉-文本匹配、难以处理复杂推理指令的问题，本文提出ReaMOT任务，要求模型通过逻辑推理而非直接匹配来跟踪目标。为此，作者构建了一个包含1156条语言指令、42万图像-语言对和869个视频序列的大规模基准数据集，其中75%的指令涉及高阶推理。他们提出ReaTrack框架，将具备语义定位能力的Thinking型LVLM与SAM2的运动先验结合，无需训练即可解决时序不一致问题，在高阶推理子集上RHOTA指标提升超三倍。
BenchCAD: A Comprehensive, Industry-Standard Benchmark for Programmatic CAD
Authors: Haozhe Zhang, Kaichen Liu, Miaomiao Chen, Lei Li, Shaojie Yang, Cheng Peng, Hanjie Chen
First: 2026-05-11T17:13:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:13:36+00:00
Comments: 9 page 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Industrial Computer-Aided Design (CAD) code generation requires models to produce executable parametric programs from visual or textual inputs. Beyond recognizing the outer shape of a part, this task involves understanding its 3D structure, inferring engineering parameters, and choosing CAD operations that reflect how the part would be designed and manufactured. Despite the promise of Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for this task, they are rarely evaluated on whether these capabilities jointly hold in realistic industrial CAD settings. We present BenchCAD, a unified benchmark for industrial CAD reasoning. BenchCAD contains 17,900 execution-verified CadQuery programs across 106 industrial part families, including bevel gears, compression springs, twist drills, and other reusable engineering designs. It evaluates models through visual question answering, code question answering, image-to-code generation, and instruction-guided code editing, enabling fine-grained analysis across perception, parametric abstraction, and executable program synthesis. Across 10+ frontier models, BenchCAD shows that current systems often recover coarse outer geometry but fail to produce faithful parametric CAD programs. Common failures include missing fine 3D structure, misinterpreting industrial design parameters, and replacing essential operations such as sweeps, lofts, and twist-extrudes with simpler sketch-and-extrude patterns. Fine-tuning and reinforcement learning improve in-distribution performance, but generalization to unseen part families remains limited. These results position BenchCAD as a benchmark for measuring and improving the industrial readiness of multimodal CAD automation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BenchCAD：程序化CAD的全面行业标准基准
工业计算机辅助设计（CAD）代码生成要求模型从视觉或文本输入生成可执行的参数化程序。该任务不仅涉及识别零件的外轮廓，还要求理解其三维结构、推断工程参数，并选择反映零件设计与制造方式的CAD操作。尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）对此任务前景广阔，但它们在真实工业CAD环境中的综合能力鲜有评估。我们提出BenchCAD，一个统一的工业CAD推理基准。BenchCAD包含106类工业零件家族的17,900个经执行验证的CadQuery程序，涵盖斜齿轮、压缩弹簧、麻花钻等可复用的工程设计。该基准通过视觉问答、代码问答、图像到代码生成和指令引导的代码编辑评估模型，支持对感知、参数抽象和可执行程序合成的细粒度分析。在10多个前沿模型上，BenchCAD显示当前系统常能恢复粗略的外轮廓，但难以生成忠实的参数化CAD程序。常见失败包括缺失精细三维结构、误判工业设计参数，以及用简单的草图-拉伸模式替代关键操作（如扫描、放样和扭曲拉伸）。微调和强化学习可提升分布内性能，但对未见零件家族的泛化能力仍有限。这些结果使BenchCAD成为衡量和改进多模态CAD自动化工业就绪度的基准。

Motivated by the need to evaluate multimodal large language models (MLLMs) in realistic industrial CAD settings, the authors introduce BenchCAD, a comprehensive benchmark containing 17,900 execution-verified CadQuery programs across 106 part families. The benchmark assesses models via four tasks: visual question answering, code question answering, image-to-code generation, and instruction-guided code editing, probing perception, parametric abstraction, and program synthesis. Experimental results across 10+ frontier models reveal that while models often correctly generate outer geometry, they frequently fail to capture fine 3D structure, misinterpret engineering parameters, and substitute complex CAD operations with simplified ones. Fine-tuning and reinforcement learning improve performance within seen part families but show limited generalization to unseen designs, highlighting the gap between current capabilities and industrial CAD requirements.
为评估多模态大语言模型在真实工业CAD场景中的能力，本文提出BenchCAD，包含106类工业零件家族的17,900个经执行验证的CadQuery程序。该基准通过视觉问答、代码问答、图像到代码生成和指令引导代码编辑四项任务，评估模型在感知、参数抽象和可执行程序合成方面的表现。实验表明，尽管模型常能正确生成零件外轮廓，但在捕捉精细3D结构、正确理解工程参数及替代复杂CAD操作（如扫掠、放样或扭曲拉伸）方面表现不佳。微调与强化学习可提升已知零件家族的表现，但在未见零件家族上泛化能力有限。
Reinforcement Learning with Action Chunking
Authors: Qiyang Li, Zhiyuan Zhou, Sergey Levine
Venue: NeurIPS 2025
First: 2025-07-10T17:48:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:12:21+00:00
Comments: The Thirty-Ninth Annual Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2025); 29 pages, 17 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Q-chunking, a simple yet effective recipe for improving reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms for long-horizon, sparse-reward tasks. Our recipe is designed for the offline-to-online RL setting, where the goal is to leverage an offline prior dataset to maximize the sample-efficiency of online learning. Effective exploration and sample-efficient learning remain central challenges in this setting, as it is not obvious how the offline data should be utilized to acquire a good exploratory policy. Our key insight is that action chunking, a technique popularized in imitation learning where sequences of future actions are predicted rather than a single action at each timestep, can be applied to temporal difference (TD)-based RL methods to mitigate the exploration challenge. Q-chunking adopts action chunking by directly running RL in a 'chunked' action space, enabling the agent to (1) leverage temporally consistent behaviors from offline data for more effective online exploration and (2) use unbiased $n$-step backups for more stable and efficient TD learning. Our experimental results demonstrate that Q-chunking exhibits strong offline performance and online sample efficiency, outperforming prior best offline-to-online methods on a range of long-horizon, sparse-reward manipulation tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于动作分块的强化学习
我们提出了Q分块，一种简单而有效的提升强化学习（RL）算法在长时域、稀疏奖励任务中表现的方法。该方法专为离线到在线RL设置设计，旨在利用离线先验数据以最大化在线学习的样本效率。在该设置中，有效探索与样本高效学习仍是核心挑战，因为离线数据应如何用于获取良好的探索策略尚不明确。我们的关键见解是：动作分块——一种在模仿学习中流行的技术，即预测未来动作序列而非每个时间步的单个动作——可应用于基于时序差分（TD）的RL方法，以缓解探索难题。Q分块通过直接在“分块”动作空间中运行RL来采用动作分块，使智能体能够（1）利用离线数据中的时序一致行为，实现更有效的在线探索；（2）使用无偏的n步备份，实现更稳定高效的TD学习。实验结果表明，Q分块在离线性能和在线样本效率方面表现强劲，在多种长时域、稀疏奖励操作任务上优于现有最佳的离线到在线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of sample-efficient exploration in offline-to-online reinforcement learning for long-horizon, sparse-reward tasks, this work introduces Q-chunking, a method that applies action chunking to TD-based RL algorithms. Instead of predicting single actions, the agent predicts sequences of actions, enabling it to leverage temporally consistent behaviors from offline data for better online exploration and to use unbiased n-step TD backups for more stable learning. Experiments show Q-chunking achieves superior offline performance and online sample efficiency compared to prior methods across multiple manipulation tasks.
为解决长时程、稀疏奖励任务中离线到在线强化学习的样本效率与探索难题，本文提出Q-chunking方法，将动作分块技术应用于TD类强化学习算法。通过在分块动作空间中运行，智能体可利用离线数据中的时序一致行为指导在线探索，并借助无偏n步TD备份提升学习稳定性。实验表明，Q-chunking在离线表现与在线样本效率上均优于现有最先进方法，在多个操作任务中表现突出。
DGPO: Beyond Pairwise Preferences with Directional Consistent Groupwise Optimization
Authors: Mengyi Deng, Zhiwei Li, Xin Li, Tingyu Zhu, Yulan Yuan, Zhijiang Guo, Wei Wang
First: 2026-05-11T17:10:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:10:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have made remarkable progress, current preference optimization methods still struggle to align directional consistency while preserving reasoning diversity. To address this limitation, we propose Directional-Groupwise Preference Optimization (DGPO), a lightweight framework that aggregates supervision signals at the group level and explicitly models direction-aware alignment through multi-candidate comparisons. DGPO organizes forward and reverse question-answer instances into structured sets and optimizes a margin-based likelihood objective that separates coherent reasoning paths from inconsistent alternatives. This group-wise formulation captures richer relative information than pairwise objectives and reinforces consistency across diverse reasoning pathways. Empirical results show that our constructed reverse data yields a 3.2% average improvement across five benchmarks, while DGPO further delivers consistent gains across multiple datasets and model families, achieving average accuracy improvements of up to 3.6%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DGPO：超越成对偏好，采用方向一致的组级优化
尽管大语言模型（LLMs）已取得显著进展，但当前偏好优化方法仍难以在保持推理多样性的同时实现方向一致性。为解决这一局限，我们提出方向组级偏好优化（DGPO），一种轻量级框架，通过组级聚合监督信号，并通过多候选比较显式建模方向感知对齐。DGPO将正向与反向问答实例组织为结构化组，并优化基于边界的似然目标，以区分一致的推理路径与不一致的替代方案。该组级形式比成对目标捕捉更丰富的相对信息，并强化多样推理路径间的一致性。实验表明，我们构建的反向数据在五个基准上平均提升3.2%，而DGPO在多个数据集和模型家族中持续带来增益，最高平均准确率提升达3.6%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve alignment of reasoning paths while preserving diversity in LLM preference optimization, DGPO introduces a group-wise optimization framework that aggregates supervision signals across structured forward-reverse question-answer pairs. It employs a margin-based likelihood objective to explicitly model directional consistency through multi-candidate comparisons, enabling richer relative information capture than traditional pairwise methods. Experiments demonstrate that DGPO achieves consistent accuracy gains across five benchmarks and multiple model families, with average improvements of up to 3.6%.
为提升大语言模型推理路径的一致性同时保留多样性，DGPO提出一种组级优化框架，通过多候选比较聚合监督信号以显式建模方向一致性。该方法将正向与反向问答对组织为结构化组，并训练基于边界的似然目标以区分一致推理路径与不一致替代方案。实验表明，DGPO在多个数据集和模型家族上平均提升3.6%的准确率，优于传统成对偏好方法，得益于组级比较所蕴含的更丰富的相对信息。
RUBEN: Rule-Based Explanations for Retrieval-Augmented LLM Systems
Authors: Joel Rorseth, Parke Godfrey, Lukasz Golab, Divesh Srivastava, Jarek Szlichta
First: 2026-05-11T17:10:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:10:35+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICDE 2026 (Demonstration Track)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper demonstrates RUBEN, an interactive tool for discovering minimal rules to explain the outputs of retrieval-augmented large language models (LLMs) in data-driven applications. We leverage novel pruning strategies to efficiently identify a minimal set of rules that subsume all others. We further demonstrate novel applications of these rules for LLM safety, specifically to test the resiliency of safety training and effectiveness of adversarial prompt injections.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RUBEN：用于检索增强型大语言模型的基于规则的解释工具
本文展示了RUBEN，一个用于在数据驱动应用中发现最小规则以解释检索增强型大语言模型（LLM）输出的交互式工具。我们利用新颖的剪枝策略高效识别能涵盖所有其他规则的最小规则集。我们进一步展示了这些规则在LLM安全中的新应用，特别是用于测试安全训练的鲁棒性和对抗性提示注入的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to explain and verify the behavior of retrieval-augmented large language models in data-driven applications, this paper introduces RUBEN, an interactive tool that discovers minimal rule sets to explain model outputs. It employs novel pruning strategies to efficiently identify a minimal set of rules that subsume all others, enabling interpretable explanations. Experimental results show RUBEN successfully identifies concise, human-readable rules that accurately capture model behavior, and it effectively evaluates LLM safety by testing the resilience of safety training and the impact of adversarial prompt injections.
为了解释和验证数据驱动应用中检索增强型大语言模型的行为，本文提出了RUBEN工具，用于发现可解释的最小规则集以解释模型输出。该工具采用新颖的剪枝策略，高效提取能涵盖所有其他规则的最小规则集合，实现可解释性。实验结果表明，这些规则能有效揭示安全训练的脆弱性及对抗性提示注入的效果，证明了RUBEN在评估模型鲁棒性方面的实用性。
Learning to Stay Safe: Adaptive Regularization Against Safety Degradation during Fine-Tuning
Authors: Jyotin Goel, Souvik Maji, Pratik Mazumder
First: 2026-02-19T16:59:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:06:52+00:00
Comments: Work in progress (48 pages)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Instruction-following language models are trained to be helpful and safe, yet their safety behavior can deteriorate under benign fine-tuning and worsen under adversarial updates. Existing defenses often offer limited protection or force a trade-off between safety and utility. We introduce a training framework that adapts regularization in response to safety risk, enabling models to remain aligned throughout fine-tuning. To estimate safety risk at training time, we explore two distinct approaches: a judge-based Safety Critic that assigns high-level harm scores to training batches, and an activation-based risk predictor built with a lightweight classifier trained on intermediate model activations to estimate harmful intent. Each approach provides a risk signal that is used to constrain updates deemed higher risk to remain close to a safe reference policy, while lower-risk updates proceed with standard training. We empirically verify that harmful intent signals are predictable from pre-generation activations and that judge scores provide effective high-recall safety guidance. Across multiple model families and attack scenarios, adaptive regularization with either risk estimation approach consistently lowers attack success rate compared to standard fine-tuning, preserves downstream performance, and adds no inference-time cost. This work demonstrates a principled mechanism for maintaining safety without sacrificing utility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习保持安全：在微调过程中对抗安全退化的自适应正则化
指令跟随语言模型在训练时旨在有益且安全，但其安全行为可能在良性微调下退化，而在对抗性更新下进一步恶化。现有防御方法通常保护有限或迫使安全与效用之间权衡。我们提出一种训练框架，根据安全风险自适应调整正则化，使模型在微调过程中保持对齐。为在训练时估计安全风险，我们探索两种不同方法：基于裁判的“安全评论员”，为训练批次分配高层次危害评分；以及基于激活的风险预测器，通过轻量级分类器训练于中间模型激活，以估计有害意图。每种方法提供风险信号，用于约束高风险更新，使其保持接近安全参考策略，而低风险更新则按标准训练进行。我们实证验证了有害意图信号可从预生成激活中预测，裁判评分提供了有效的高召回率安全指导。在多个模型家族和攻击场景中，无论采用哪种风险估计方法，自适应正则化均一致降低攻击成功率，保持下游性能，且不增加推理时间开销。本工作展示了在不牺牲效用的前提下维持安全性的原理性机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degradation of safety in instruction-following models during fine-tuning, this work proposes an adaptive regularization framework that dynamically adjusts training updates based on estimated safety risk. It employs two risk estimation methods—a judge-based Safety Critic assigning harm scores to batches, and an activation-based predictor using lightweight classifiers on intermediate activations—to guide updates toward safe behavior. Experiments across multiple models and attack types show that both methods effectively reduce attack success rates without harming downstream performance or adding inference overhead, demonstrating a scalable way to maintain safety during fine-tuning.
为应对指令跟随模型在微调过程中安全性能下降的问题，本文提出一种自适应正则化框架，根据训练时的安全风险动态调整更新策略。该框架采用两种风险评估方法：一种是基于裁判的Safety Critic，为训练批次分配危害评分；另一种是基于激活的预测器，利用轻量级分类器对中间激活值进行有害意图评估。两种方法均引导高风险更新靠近安全参考策略，低风险更新则按常规训练。实验表明，该方法在多种模型与攻击场景下，相较标准微调显著降低攻击成功率，同时保持下游性能并无额外推理开销。
Feature Augmentation of GNNs for ILPs: Local Uniqueness Suffices
Authors: Qingyu Han, Qian Li, Linxin Yang, Qian Chen, Qingjiang Shi, Ruoyu Sun
First: 2025-09-25T10:50:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:06:09+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 9 Tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Integer Linear Programs (ILPs) are central to real-world optimizations but notoriously difficult to solve. Learning to Optimize (L2O) has emerged as a promising paradigm, with Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) serving as the standard backbone. However, standard anonymous GNNs are limited in expressiveness for ILPs, and the common enhancement of augmenting nodes with globally unique identifiers (UIDs) typically introduces spurious correlations that severely harm generalization. To address this tradeoff, we propose a parsimonious Local-UID scheme based on d-hop uniqueness coloring, which ensures identifiers are unique only within each node's d-hop neighborhood. Building on this scheme, we introduce ColorGNN, which incorporates color information via color-conditioned embeddings, and ColorUID, a lightweight feature-level variant. We prove that for d-layer networks, Local-UIDs achieve the expressive power of Global-UIDs while offering stronger generalization. Extensive experiments show that our approach yields substantial and robust gains across ILP benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于整数线性规划的GNN特征增强：局部唯一性已足够
整数线性规划（ILP）是现实世界优化的核心，但求解难度极大。学习优化（L2O）已成为一种有前景的范式，其中图神经网络（GNN）作为标准骨干结构。然而，标准匿名GNN在处理ILP时表达能力有限，而常见的增强方法——为节点添加全局唯一标识符（UID）——通常会引入虚假相关性，严重损害泛化能力。为解决这一权衡，我们提出了一种简洁的局部UID方案，基于d跳唯一着色，确保标识符仅在每个节点的d跳邻域内唯一。在此基础上，我们引入ColorGNN，通过颜色条件化嵌入整合颜色信息；并提出轻量级特征级变体ColorUID。我们证明，对于d层网络，局部UID在保持与全局UID相同表达能力的同时，泛化能力更强。大量实验表明，我们的方法在ILP基准测试中实现了显著且稳健的性能提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the expressiveness limitations of standard GNNs in solving Integer Linear Programs and the harmful generalization effects of global unique identifiers, the paper proposes Local-UID, a d-hop neighborhood-based uniqueness scheme that preserves expressiveness while avoiding spurious correlations. It introduces ColorGNN and ColorUID, two variants that encode color information via conditioned embeddings or lightweight feature augmentation. Experimental results across ILP benchmarks demonstrate that these methods achieve substantial and consistent performance gains over baseline GNNs without global UIDs.
为解决标准GNN在求解整数线性规划时表达能力不足及全局唯一标识符导致泛化能力下降的问题，本文提出基于d跳邻域的局部唯一标识方案（Local-UID），确保标识仅在局部范围内唯一，避免虚假关联。在此基础上，设计了ColorGNN和ColorUID两种变体，分别通过颜色条件嵌入或轻量级特征增强引入颜色信息。理论证明表明，d层网络中Local-UID可达到全局UID的表达能力，同时提升泛化性能。实验结果在多个ILP基准上均显示显著且稳健的性能提升。
Masked Generative Transformer Is What You Need for Image Editing
Authors: Wei Chow, Linfeng Li, Xian Sun, Lingdong Kong, Zefeng Li, Qi Xu, Hang Song, Tian Ye, Xian Wang, Jinbin Bai, Shilin Xu, Xiangtai Li, Junting Pan, Shaoteng Liu, Ran Zhou, Tianshu Yang, Songhua Liu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-11T17:05:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:05:52+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 HiGen Workshop; Project Page at https://weichow23.github.io/EditMGT/ GitHub at https://github.com/weichow23/EditMGT
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Diffusion models dominate image editing, yet their global denoising mechanism entangles edited regions with surrounding context, causing modifications to propagate into areas that should remain intact. We propose a fundamentally different approach by leveraging Masked Generative Transformers (MGTs), whose localized token-prediction paradigm naturally confines changes to intended regions. We present EditMGT, an MGT-based editing framework that is the first of its kind. Our approach employs multi-layer attention consolidation to aggregate cross-attention maps into precise edit localization signals, and region-hold sampling to explicitly prevent token flipping in non-target areas. To support training, we construct CrispEdit-2M, a 2M-sample high-resolution (>1024) editing dataset spanning seven categories. With only 960M parameters, EditMGT achieves state-of-the-art image similarity on multiple benchmarks while delivering 6x faster editing, demonstrating that MGTs offer a compelling alternative to diffusion-based editing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：掩码生成变换器是图像编辑所需之选
扩散模型主导图像编辑，但其全局去噪机制会将编辑区域与周围上下文纠缠，导致修改扩散至应保持不变的区域。我们提出一种根本不同的方法，利用掩码生成变换器（MGTs），其局部化token预测范式天然限制修改范围于目标区域。我们提出EditMGT，这是首个基于MGT的编辑框架。我们的方法通过多层注意力聚合，将跨注意力图整合为精确的编辑定位信号，并采用区域保持采样，显式防止非目标区域token翻转。为支持训练，我们构建了CrispEdit-2M数据集，包含200万样本、高分辨率（>1024）且覆盖七类。仅9.6亿参数，EditMGT在多个基准上实现最先进图像相似度，同时编辑速度提升6倍，证明MGT是扩散式编辑的有力替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue that diffusion models cause unintended propagation of edits due to their global denoising, the authors propose EditMGT, a Masked Generative Transformer-based framework that confines edits to target regions through localized token prediction. The method uses multi-layer attention consolidation to generate precise edit localization signals and region-hold sampling to prevent token flipping outside the edit zone. Evaluated on multiple benchmarks, EditMGT achieves state-of-the-art image similarity with only 960M parameters and delivers six times faster editing than diffusion-based methods.
鉴于扩散模型因全局去噪机制导致编辑内容意外扩散的问题，作者提出基于掩码生成Transformer的EditMGT框架，通过局部化token预测机制将修改限制在目标区域。该方法利用多层注意力聚合生成精确的编辑定位信号，并采用区域保持采样防止非目标区域的token翻转。在多个基准测试中，EditMGT仅用9.6亿参数即达到最优图像相似度，且编辑速度提升6倍，验证了MGT在图像编辑中的有效性。
Is Your Driving World Model an All-Around Player?
Authors: Lingdong Kong, Ao Liang, Tianyi Yan, Hongsi Liu, Wesley Yang, Ziqi Huang, Xian Sun, Wei Yin, Jialong Zuo, Yixuan Hu, Dekai Zhu, Dongyue Lu, Youquan Liu, Guangfeng Jiang, Linfeng Li, Xiangtai Li, Long Zhuo, Lai Xing Ng, Benoit R. Cottereau, Changxin Gao, Liang Pan, Wei Tsang Ooi, Ziwei Liu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-11T17:05:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:05:49+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 VideoWorldModel Workshop; Project Page at https://worldbench.github.io/worldlens GitHub at https://github.com/worldbench/WorldLens
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Today's driving world models can generate remarkably realistic dash-cam videos, yet no single model excels universally. Some generate photorealistic textures but violate basic physics; others maintain geometric consistency but fail when subjected to closed-loop planning. This disconnect exposes a critical gap: the field evaluates how real generated worlds appear, but rarely whether they behave realistically. We introduce WorldLens, a unified benchmark that measures world-model fidelity across the full spectrum, from pixel quality and 4D geometry to closed-loop driving and human perceptual alignment, through five complementary aspects and 24 standardized dimensions. Our evaluation of six representative models reveals that no existing approach dominates across all axes: texture-rich models violate geometry, geometry-aware models lack behavioral fidelity, and even the strongest performers achieve only 2-3 out of 10 on human realism ratings. To bridge algorithmic metrics with human perception, we further contribute WorldLens-26K, a 26,808-entry human-annotated preference dataset pairing numerical scores with textual rationales, and WorldLens-Agent, a vision-language evaluator distilled from these judgments that enables scalable, explainable auto-assessment. Together, the benchmark, dataset, and agent form a unified ecosystem for assessing generated worlds not merely by visual appeal, but by physical and behavioral fidelity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你的驾驶世界模型是全能选手吗？
当前的驾驶世界模型能生成高度逼真的行车记录仪视频，但尚无单一模型能全面胜出。部分模型生成逼真纹理却违反基本物理规律；另一些模型保持几何一致性，但在闭环规划中表现不佳。这种脱节揭示了关键缺口：领域内评估生成世界的视觉真实感，却鲜少评估其行为合理性。我们提出WorldLens，一个统一基准，通过五个互补维度和24个标准化指标，全面衡量世界模型从像素质量、4D几何到闭环驾驶与人类感知对齐的综合保真度。对六种代表性模型的评估表明，现有方法均无法在所有维度上占优：纹理丰富的模型破坏几何一致性，几何感知模型缺乏行为保真度，最强模型在人类真实感评分中仅获2-3分（满分10分）。为弥合算法指标与人类感知的鸿沟，我们进一步贡献WorldLens-26K——包含26,808条人类标注偏好数据，附带数值评分与文本理由；以及WorldLens-Agent——从这些判断中蒸馏出的视觉-语言评估器，支持可扩展、可解释的自动化评估。该基准、数据集与评估器共同构成统一生态系统，使生成世界的评估不仅限于视觉吸引力，更聚焦物理与行为保真度。

Driven by the observation that current driving world models excel in visual realism but often fail in physical or behavioral consistency, this work introduces WorldLens, a comprehensive benchmark evaluating generated worlds across five dimensions including pixel quality, 4D geometry, closed-loop driving, and human perceptual alignment. Evaluating six representative models, the study finds no single model performs optimally across all criteria: texture-focused models compromise geometry, geometry-aware models lack behavioral realism, and even top performers score only 2–3 out of 10 on human realism ratings. To bridge automated metrics with human judgment, the authors also release WorldLens-26K, a large human-annotated preference dataset, and WorldLens-Agent, a vision-language evaluator trained on these annotations, enabling scalable, explainable assessment of world-model fidelity beyond aesthetics.
鉴于当前驾驶世界模型在视觉逼真度上表现突出，但在物理或行为一致性方面常有不足，本文提出WorldLens统一评测基准，从五个维度、24项标准化指标全面评估生成世界。对六种代表性模型的评测显示，无一模型在所有维度上领先：注重纹理的模型牺牲几何一致性，注重几何的模型缺乏行为真实感，最强模型在人类真实感评分中仅得2–3分（满分10分）。为弥合自动化指标与人类感知的差距，作者还发布包含26,808条人类标注偏好的数据集WorldLens-26K，并构建基于该数据训练的视觉-语言评估器WorldLens-Agent，实现可扩展、可解释的自动评估。
Optimal Attention Temperature Improves the Robustness of In-Context Learning under Distribution Shift in High Dimensions
Authors: Samet Demir, Zafer Dogan
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-11-03T07:18:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:05:45+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026, 24 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pretrained Transformers can perform in-context learning (ICL) from a few demonstrations, but this ability can fail sharply when the test distribution differs from pretraining, a common deployment setting. We study attention temperature as a simple inference-time control for improving ICL robustness under such shifts. In a high-dimensional linear-regression framework, we analyze a Transformer with "approximate softmax" attention, which preserves softmax's normalization and temperature-dependent selectivity while remaining tractable. We derive a closed-form expression for the ICL generalization error under distribution shift, and show that it is minimized by an explicit optimal attention temperature. This characterization yields interpretable guidance by linking the best temperature to moments of the pre-softmax attention scores, and predicts when temperature adjustment can recover near Bayes-optimal performance. We validate the theory with extensive simulations, and further demonstrate gains on pretrained LLMs (GPT-2 and Llama2-7B) on question-answering benchmarks under distribution shift induced by noisy in-context demonstrations. Overall, attention temperature emerges as a principled, lightweight knob for improving the robustness of ICL in pretrained Transformers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：最优注意力温度可提升预训练Transformer在高维分布偏移下的上下文学习鲁棒性
预训练Transformer可通过少量演示实现上下文学习（ICL），但在测试分布与预训练分布不同时，该能力可能急剧失效——这在实际部署中很常见。本文研究注意力温度作为简单推理时控制手段，以提升此类分布偏移下的ICL鲁棒性。在高维线性回归框架中，我们分析了具有“近似softmax”注意力的Transformer，该注意力保留softmax的归一化与温度依赖选择性，同时保持可计算性。我们推导出分布偏移下ICL泛化误差的闭式表达，并证明其可通过显式的最优注意力温度最小化。该表征通过将最佳温度与预softmax注意力分数的矩关联，提供可解释性指导，并预测温度调整何时可恢复近似贝叶斯最优性能。我们通过大量仿真验证理论，并进一步在受噪声上下文演示诱导分布偏移的问答基准上，展示其在预训练大语言模型（GPT-2和Llama2-7B）上的性能增益。总体而言，注意力温度成为提升预训练Transformer上下文学习鲁棒性的原则性、轻量级调节手段。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of in-context learning in pretrained Transformers under distribution shifts, this work investigates attention temperature as a simple inference-time mechanism to enhance robustness. Using a high-dimensional linear regression model with approximate softmax attention, the authors derive a closed-form expression for generalization error under distribution shift and identify an optimal temperature that minimizes it, tied to pre-softmax attention score moments. Experiments across synthetic settings and real LLMs (GPT-2, Llama2-7B) confirm that tuning attention temperature improves performance under noisy in-context demonstrations, often recovering near Bayes-optimal behavior.
为提升预训练Transformer在分布偏移下上下文学习（ICL）的鲁棒性，本文研究了注意力温度作为推理时的简单控制机制。在高维线性回归框架中，采用近似softmax注意力，推导出分布偏移下ICL泛化误差的闭式表达式，并确定使误差最小化的最优温度。理论分析将最优温度与预softmax注意力分数的矩联系起来，预测温度调整何时可接近贝叶斯最优性能。在合成数据和真实大模型（GPT-2与Llama2-7B）上的实验表明，调整注意力温度能显著提升在噪声上下文示例下的ICL准确率，验证其作为轻量级、原理性干预的有效性。
CoLA-Flow Policy: Temporally Coherent Imitation Learning via Continuous Latent Action Flow Matching for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Wu Songwei, Jiang Zhiduo, Sun Wandong, Xie Guanghu, Zhao Rui, Liu Hong, Liu Yang
First: 2026-01-30T15:36:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:04:14+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning long-horizon robotic manipulation requires jointly achieving expressive behavior modeling, real-time inference, and stable execution, which remains challenging for existing generative policies. Diffusion-based approaches offer strong modeling capacity but incur high inference latency, while flow matching enables fast, near-single-step generation yet often suffers from unstable execution when operating directly in the raw action space. We propose Continuous Latent Action Flow Policy (CoLA-Flow Policy), a trajectory-level imitation learning framework that performs flow matching in a continuous latent action space. By encoding action sequences into temporally coherent latent trajectories and learning an explicit latent-space flow, CoLA-Flow Policy decouples global motion structure from low-level control noise, enabling smooth and reliable long-horizon execution. The framework further integrates geometry-aware point cloud conditioning and execution-time multimodal modulation, using visual cues as a representative modality to enhance real-world robustness. Experiments in simulation and on real robots show that CoLA-Flow Policy achieves near-single-step inference, improves trajectory smoothness by up to 93.7% and task success by up to 25 percentage points over raw action-space flow baselines, while remaining significantly faster than diffusion-based policies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoLA-Flow策略：通过连续潜在动作流匹配实现时间一致的模仿学习以用于机器人操作
学习长时程机器人操作需要同时实现表达性行为建模、实时推理和稳定执行，这仍是现有生成策略的挑战。扩散方法具有强大的建模能力但推理延迟高，而流匹配可实现快速近单步生成，但在原始动作空间直接操作时常出现执行不稳定。我们提出连续潜在动作流策略（CoLA-Flow策略），一种轨迹级模仿学习框架，其在连续潜在动作空间中执行流匹配。通过将动作序列编码为时间一致的潜在轨迹并学习显式潜在空间流，CoLA-Flow策略将全局运动结构与低层控制噪声解耦，实现平滑可靠的长时程执行。该框架进一步整合几何感知点云条件化和执行时多模态调制，利用视觉线索作为代表性模态以增强现实世界鲁棒性。仿真与真实机器人实验表明，CoLA-Flow策略实现近单步推理，相比原始动作空间流基线，轨迹平滑度提升最高达93.7%，任务成功率提升最高达25个百分点，同时显著快于扩散策略。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of long-horizon robotic manipulation requiring expressive behavior, real-time inference, and stable execution, the paper introduces CoLA-Flow Policy, a trajectory-level imitation learning framework that performs flow matching in a continuous latent action space. By encoding action sequences into temporally coherent latent trajectories and learning an explicit latent-space flow, the method decouples motion structure from control noise, enabling smooth execution. It further enhances robustness via geometry-aware point cloud conditioning and visual modulation. Experiments show the policy achieves near-single-step inference, improves trajectory smoothness by up to 93.7% and task success by up to 25 percentage points over raw action-space baselines, while being significantly faster than diffusion-based methods.
为解决长时程机器人操作中行为表达、实时推理与稳定执行的协同难题，作者提出CoLA-Flow Policy框架，该框架在连续潜在动作空间中进行轨迹级流匹配。通过将动作序列编码为时序一致的潜在轨迹并学习显式潜在空间流，该方法分离了运动结构与控制噪声，实现平滑执行。同时引入几何感知点云条件与执行时多模态调制，以视觉线索增强现实鲁棒性。实验表明，该策略实现近单步推理，轨迹平滑度提升最高达93.7%，任务成功率较原始动作空间流基线提升最高25个百分点，且显著快于扩散模型方法。
Learning More from Less: Exploiting Counterfactuals for Data-Efficient Chart Understanding
Authors: Jianzhu Bao, Haozhen Zhang, Kuicai Dong, Bozhi Wu, Sarthak Ketanbhai Modi, Zi Pong Lim, Yon Shin Teo, Wenya Wang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-05-11T17:02:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:02:09+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable progress in chart understanding, largely driven by supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on increasingly large synthetic datasets. However, scaling SFT data alone is inefficient and overlooks a key property of charts: charts are programmatically generated visual artifacts, where small, code-controlled visual changes can induce drastic shifts in semantics and correct answers. Learning this counterfactual sensitivity requires VLMs to discriminate fine-grained visual differences, yet standard SFT treats training instances independently and provides limited supervision to enforce this behavior. To address this, we introduce ChartCF, a data-efficient training framework designed to enhance counterfactual sensitivity. ChartCF consists of: (1) a counterfactual data synthesis pipeline via code modification, (2) a chart similarity-based data selection strategy that filters overly difficult samples for improved training efficiency, and (3) multimodal preference optimization across both textual and visual modalities. Experiments on five benchmarks show that ChartCF achieves superior or comparable performance to strong chart-specific VLMs while using significantly less training data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从更少数据中学习更多：利用反事实提升图表理解的数据效率
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在图表理解方面取得了显著进展，主要得益于在日益庞大的合成数据集上进行的监督微调（SFT）。然而，仅扩展SFT数据效率低下，且忽视了图表的一个关键特性：图表是程序生成的视觉产物，其中微小的、由代码控制的视觉变化可导致语义和正确答案的剧烈变化。学习这种反事实敏感性要求VLMs能够辨别细微的视觉差异，但标准SFT将训练实例独立对待，提供的监督有限，难以强制实现该行为。为此，我们提出了ChartCF，一种旨在增强反事实敏感性的数据高效训练框架。ChartCF包含：（1）通过代码修改生成反事实数据的合成管道，（2）基于图表相似度的数据选择策略，用于过滤过于困难的样本以提升训练效率，（3）跨文本与视觉模态的多模态偏好优化。在五个基准测试中，ChartCF在使用显著更少训练数据的情况下，实现了优于或可比于强图表专用VLMs的性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of scaling supervised fine-tuning data for vision-language models in chart understanding, this work proposes ChartCF, a data-efficient framework that leverages counterfactuals derived from programmatically generated charts. It synthesizes counterfactual training data by modifying chart code to induce semantic shifts, selects samples based on visual similarity to avoid overly difficult cases, and optimizes multimodal preferences to reinforce fine-grained visual discrimination. Experiments across five benchmarks demonstrate that ChartCF matches or exceeds the performance of existing chart-specific models while requiring substantially less training data.
为解决视觉语言模型在图表理解中依赖大量监督微调数据的效率问题，本文提出ChartCF框架，通过代码修改生成反事实数据，利用图表相似度筛选训练样本以提升效率，并在图文模态上进行偏好优化。实验表明，该方法在五个基准测试中表现可媲美或优于现有专用图表模型，同时显著减少所需训练数据量。
Grounded Satirical Generation with RAG
Authors: Oona Itkonen, Yuxin Su, Linyao Du, Ona De Gibert
First: 2026-05-11T17:00:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:00:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Humor generation remains challenging task for Large Language Models (LLMs), due to their subjective nature. We focus on satire, a form of humor strongly shaped by context. In this work, we present a novel pipeline for grounded satire generation that uses Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) over current news to produce satirical dictionary definitions in the Finnish context. We also introduce a new task-specific evaluation framework and annotate 100 generated definitions with six human annotators, enabling analysis across multiple experimental conditions, including cultural background, source-word type, and the presence or absence of RAG. Our results show that the generated definitions are perceived as more political than humorous. Both topic-based word selection and RAG improve the political relevance of the outputs, but neither yields clear gains in humor generation. In addition, our LLM-as-a-judge evaluation of five state-of-the-art models indicates that LLMs correlate well with human judgments on political relevance, but perform poorly on humor. We release our code and annotated dataset to support further research on grounded satire generation and evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于RAG的有依据讽刺生成
由于幽默具有主观性，大型语言模型（LLMs）在幽默生成方面仍面临挑战。我们聚焦于讽刺——一种高度依赖语境的幽默形式。本文提出了一种新颖的有依据讽刺生成管道，通过检索增强生成（RAG）基于当前新闻，在芬兰语境下生成讽刺性词典定义。我们还引入了一个新的任务特定评估框架，并由六位人工标注者标注了100个生成定义，从而支持在多种实验条件下（包括文化背景、源词类型及RAG的有无）进行分析。结果表明，生成的定义被感知为更政治化而非幽默。基于主题的词选择和RAG均提升了输出的政治相关性，但并未显著提升幽默生成效果。此外，我们对五种最先进的模型进行的LLM作为裁判评估表明，LLMs在政治相关性判断上与人类评价高度一致，但在幽默判断上表现不佳。我们开源了代码和标注数据集，以支持进一步的有依据讽刺生成与评估研究。
