Motivated by the difficulty LLMs face in generating culturally grounded satire due to its context-dependent nature, this work introduces a RAG-based pipeline that generates satirical dictionary definitions in Finnish using current news. The method retrieves contextually relevant news snippets and integrates them into definition generation, while a new evaluation framework with human annotations assesses outputs across cultural, lexical, and RAG conditions. Experimental results show that RAG and topic-based word selection enhance political relevance but not humor; LLM-based evaluations align with human judgments on political relevance but poorly predict humor.
为解决基于上下文的讽刺生成难题，该研究提出一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的流程，利用当前新闻在芬兰语环境中生成讽刺性词典定义。方法通过RAG提升政治相关性，并设计新评估框架与人工标注数据集（100条定义由六位标注者标注），支持跨文化、词源及RAG条件的分析。实验表明，RAG与主题词选择可增强政治相关性，但对幽默生成无显著提升；LLM作为评判者评估显示，模型在政治相关性上与人类判断高度一致，但在幽默判断上表现不佳。
PINS: Proximal Iterations with Sparse Newton and Sinkhorn for Optimal Transport
Authors: Di Wu, Ling Liang, Haizhao Yang
First: 2025-02-06T03:21:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:00:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Optimal transport (OT) is a widely used tool in machine learning, but computing high-accuracy solutions for large instances remains costly. Entropic regularization and the Sinkhorn algorithm improve scalability; however, when the regularization parameter is small, Sinkhorn convergence slows, and the iterates approach an entropic solution that remains separated from the true OT plan by an entropic-bias plateau. We introduce PINS (Proximal Iterations with sparse Newton and Sinkhorn), a two-loop solver designed to move beyond this plateau. The outer loop applies an entropic proximal-point method, solving the original OT problem through a sequence of entropic subproblems with shifted cost matrices. Each inner subproblem is then solved by a Sinkhorn warm-up followed by sparse-Newton refinement. We prove that PINS converges globally to an optimal solution of the unregularized OT problem and that the inner Hessian admits a sparsification at every outer iteration with a structure independent of the cost matrix. On synthetic and augmented-MNIST instances, PINS achieves much lower relative cost errors than Sinkhorn-type baselines, which stall at the entropic-bias plateau, and is $5$--$73\times$ faster than Sinkhorn with the same outer loop at matched accuracy. On large-scale DOTmark instances, a streaming implementation reduces peak memory by $24$--$54\%$ compared with the network-simplex linear programming (LP) solver and remains feasible under per-process memory budgets for which the LP solver fails.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PINS：用于最优传输的稀疏牛顿与Sinkhorn近似迭代
最优传输（OT）是机器学习中广泛使用的工具，但对大规模实例计算高精度解仍成本高昂。熵正则化与Sinkhorn算法提升了可扩展性；然而，当正则化参数较小时，Sinkhorn收敛速度变慢，迭代结果趋近于熵正则化解，而该解与真实OT计划之间存在熵偏置平台。我们提出PINS（Proximal Iterations with sparse Newton and Sinkhorn），一种双层求解器，旨在突破该平台。外层循环采用熵近端点法，通过一系列具有偏移成本矩阵的熵子问题求解原始OT问题。每个内层子问题先由Sinkhorn预热，再经稀疏牛顿法优化。我们证明PINS全局收敛至无正则化OT问题的最优解，且内层Hessian矩阵在每次外层迭代中均可稀疏化，其结构与成本矩阵无关。在合成数据与增强MNIST实例上，PINS相较Sinkhorn类基线方法具有显著更低的相对成本误差，且在相同精度下比Sinkhorn快5至73倍。在大规模DOTmark实例上，流式实现相比网络单纯形线性规划（LP）求解器降低峰值内存消耗24–54%，且在LP求解器无法满足的每进程内存预算下仍可运行。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of Sinkhorn algorithms for small entropic regularization parameters in optimal transport, the paper introduces PINS, a two-loop solver combining entropic proximal-point iterations with sparse Newton refinement. The outer loop solves a sequence of entropic subproblems with shifted cost matrices, while the inner loop uses Sinkhorn warm-up followed by sparse-Newton steps to escape the entropic-bias plateau. The method is proven to globally converge to the unregularized OT solution, with inner Hessians sparsifiable independently of the cost matrix. Experiments show PINS achieves significantly lower relative cost errors than Sinkhorn baselines and is 5–73× faster at matched accuracy on synthetic and augmented-MNIST data, and reduces peak memory usage by 24–54% on large DOTmark instances compared to network-simplex LP solvers.
为了解决Sinkhorn算法在小正则化参数下因陷入熵偏置平台而效率低下的问题，本文提出PINS方法，采用双层求解框架：外层为熵近端点方法，通过调整代价矩阵序列求解原始OT问题；内层先用Sinkhorn预热，再用稀疏牛顿法进行精细优化，且每轮迭代中Hessian矩阵可稀疏化，结构与代价矩阵无关。实验表明，PINS在合成数据和增强MNIST数据上相较Sinkhorn基线方法误差更低、速度快5–73倍；在大规模DOTmark数据上，其流式实现相比网络单纯形线性规划求解器峰值内存减少24–54%，且在LP求解器因内存不足失败的场景下仍可运行。
The Generalized Turing Test: A Foundation for Comparing Intelligence
Authors: Daniel Mitropolsky, Susan S. Hong, Riccardo Neumarker, Emanuele Rimoldi, Tomaso Poggio
First: 2026-05-11T17:00:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:00:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the Generalized Turing Test (GTT), a formal framework for comparing the capabilities of arbitrary agents via indistinguishability. For agents A and B, we define the Turing comparator A $\geq$ B to hold if B, acting as a distinguisher, cannot reliably distinguish between interactions with A (instructed to imitate B) and another instance of B. This yields a dataset- and task-agnostic notion of relative intelligence. We study the comparator's structure, including conditions under which it is transitive and therefore induces an ordering over equivalence classes, and we define and analyze variants with querying, bounded interaction, and fixed distinguishers. To complement the theory, we instantiate the framework on a collection of modern models, empirically evaluating pairwise indistinguishability across thousands of trials. The resulting comparisons exhibit a stratified structure consistent with existing rankings, hinting that the proposed framework yields meaningful empirical orderings. Our results position indistinguishability as a unifying lens for reasoning about intelligence, suggesting a foundation for evaluation and, potentially, training objectives that are inherently independent of fixed datasets or benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：广义图灵测试：智能比较的基础
我们提出了广义图灵测试（GTT），一个通过不可区分性比较任意代理能力的形式化框架。对于代理A和B，我们定义图灵比较器A≥B成立的条件是：当B作为区分者时，无法可靠区分与被指示模仿B的A的交互，与另一个B实例的交互。这提供了一种与数据和任务无关的相对智能概念。我们研究了比较器的结构，包括其在何种条件下具有传递性，从而在等价类上诱导序关系，并定义和分析了包含查询、有限交互和固定区分者的变体。为补充理论，我们在一组现代模型上实例化该框架，通过数千次试验实证评估成对不可区分性。所得比较结果呈现出与现有排名一致的分层结构，表明所提框架能产生有意义的实证排序。我们的结果将不可区分性定位为理解智能的统一视角，暗示其可作为独立于固定数据集或基准的评估和训练目标的基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a principled, dataset-agnostic way to compare agent intelligence, the paper proposes the Generalized Turing Test (GTT), which defines agent A as at least as intelligent as B if a distinguisher cannot reliably tell A (imitating B) apart from a real instance of B. The framework explores structural properties like transitivity and introduces variants with querying, bounded interaction, and fixed distinguishers. Empirical evaluations across thousands of trials on modern models reveal a stratified ordering of indistinguishability that aligns with existing rankings, suggesting GTT provides a meaningful, benchmark-independent foundation for intelligence comparison.
为解决缺乏统一标准比较智能体能力的问题，本文提出广义图灵测试（GTT），定义智能体A不弱于B，当且仅当B作为区分者无法可靠区分A（模仿B）与真实B的交互。该框架探讨其结构特性如传递性，并引入查询与有限交互变体。在数千次实验中对现代模型进行实证评估，结果呈现分层智能排序，与现有排名一致，表明GTT可作为脱离特定数据集或基准的智能评估与训练目标的基础。
Verification Mirage: Mapping the Reliability Boundary of Self-Verification in Medical VQA
Authors: Ruinan Jin, Beidi Zhao, Myeongkyun Kang, Qiong Zhang, Xiaoxiao Li
First: 2026-05-11T17:00:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T17:00:00+00:00
Comments: 31 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-verification, re-invoking the same vision language model (VLM) in a fresh context to check its own generated answer, is increasingly used as a default safety layer for medical visual question answering (VQA). We argue that this practice is fundamentally unreliable. We introduce [METHOD NAME], a diagnostic framework for mapping the reliability boundary of medical VLM self-verification by decomposing verifier behavior into discrimination capability and agreement bias. Because the verifier and answer generator are capacity-coupled, the verifier can overly agree with the generator, creating a verification mirage: a regime with both high verifier error and high agreement bias, driven by false acceptance of incorrect answers. Evaluating six open-weight VLMs across five medical VQA datasets and seven medical tasks, we find that this boundary is strongly task-conditioned. Knowledge-intensive clinical tasks fall deepest into the mirage, simpler tasks are more resistant, and perceptual tasks lie in between. Verification also fails to provide an independent safety signal: logistic mixed-effects analysis shows that verifier error and agreement bias become more likely when the generator is wrong, while saliency analyses show that verifiers under-attend to image evidence relative to generators, a phenomenon we call the lazy verifier. Cross-verification reduces but does not eliminate the mirage. Moreover, when verification is reused in multi-turn actor-verifier loops, most initially wrong answers become locked in by false verification. Since our experiments use clean benchmarks, the observed reliability boundary likely underestimates failures in real clinical deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：验证幻象：映射医学视觉问答中自验证可靠性的边界
自验证，即在新上下文中重新调用同一视觉语言模型（VLM）以检查其自身生成的答案，正日益被用作医学视觉问答（VQA）的默认安全层。我们认为这种做法本质上不可靠。我们提出了[方法名称]，一个诊断框架，通过将验证器行为分解为判别能力和一致性偏倚，以映射医学VLM自验证的可靠性边界。由于验证器与答案生成器能力耦合，验证器可能过度认同生成器，形成“验证幻象”：一种高验证器错误率与高一致性偏倚并存的区域，由错误答案的虚假接受驱动。我们在五个医学VQA数据集和七个医学任务上评估了六个开源VLM，发现该边界高度依赖任务类型。知识密集型临床任务最易陷入幻象，简单任务更具抗性，感知任务则居中。验证未能提供独立的安全信号：逻辑混合效应分析表明，当生成器出错时，验证器错误率和一致性偏倚更易发生；显著性分析显示，验证器相比生成器对图像证据关注不足，我们称之为“懒惰验证器”。交叉验证虽能减轻但无法消除幻象。此外，当验证在多轮演员-验证器循环中重复使用时，大多数初始错误答案会因虚假验证而固化。由于我们的实验使用干净基准，所观察到的可靠性边界可能低估了真实临床部署中的失败情况。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns over the reliability of self-verification in medical VQA, this study introduces a diagnostic framework that decomposes verifier behavior into discrimination capability and agreement bias to map the reliability boundary of self-verification. The authors find that due to capacity coupling between the verifier and answer generator, verifiers often exhibit high agreement bias while maintaining high error rates, creating a 'verification mirage' where incorrect answers are falsely accepted. Across six VLMs and five medical VQA datasets, they observe that knowledge-intensive clinical tasks are most vulnerable to this mirage, while perceptual tasks are more resilient. Verification fails to act as an independent safety signal, as verifiers under-attend to image evidence (a 'lazy verifier') and are more likely to err when the generator is wrong. Cross-verification mitigates but does not eliminate the mirage, and repeated verification in multi-turn loops can lock in initial errors.
鉴于自验证在医疗视觉问答中被广泛用作默认安全层，本文质疑其可靠性，提出一种诊断框架，将验证器行为分解为判别能力和一致性偏差。作者指出，由于验证器与生成器能力耦合，验证器常表现出高一致性偏差而无法识别错误，形成‘验证幻象’——即错误答案被虚假接受的高错误率与高一致性并存状态。在六个视觉语言模型和五个医疗VQA数据集上评估发现，知识密集型临床任务最易陷入幻象，感知类任务更具抗性。分析表明，验证器对图像证据的关注度低于生成器，称为‘懒惰验证器’，交叉验证仅部分缓解该问题。在多轮交互中，初始错误答案因虚假验证被固化，表明真实临床部署中的可靠性失败可能比基准测试更严重。
Rethinking Agentic Search with Pi-Serini: Is Lexical Retrieval Sufficient?
Authors: Tz-Huan Hsu, Jheng-Hong Yang, Jimmy Lin
First: 2026-05-11T16:58:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:58:57+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Does a lexical retriever suffice as large language models (LLMs) become more capable in an agentic loop? This question naturally arises when building deep research systems. We revisit it by pairing BM25 with frontier LLMs that have better reasoning and tool-use abilities. To support researchers asking the same question, we introduce Pi-Serini, a search agent equipped with three tools for retrieving, browsing, and reading documents. Our results show that, on BrowseComp-Plus, a well-configured lexical retriever with sufficient retrieval depth can support effective deep research when paired with more capable LLMs. Specifically, Pi-Serini with gpt-5.5 achieves 83.1% answer accuracy and 94.7% surfaced evidence recall, outperforming released search agents that use dense retrievers. Controlled ablations further show that BM25 tuning improves answer accuracy by 18.0% and surfaced evidence recall by 11.1% over the default BM25 setting, while increasing retrieval depth further improves surfaced evidence recall by 25.3% over the shallow-retrieval setting. Source code is available at https://github.com/justram/pi-serini.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考带有Pi-Serini的代理搜索：词典检索是否足够？
当大型语言模型（LLM）在代理循环中能力增强时，词典检索器是否足以满足需求？这一问题在构建深度研究系统时自然浮现。我们通过将BM25与具备更强推理和工具使用能力的前沿LLM结合，重新审视这一问题。为支持同样提问的研究人员，我们引入了Pi-Serini，这是一个配备三种工具的搜索代理，用于检索、浏览和阅读文档。结果表明，在BrowseComp-Plus数据集上，一个配置良好的词典检索器，若具备足够的检索深度，与更强大的LLM结合时，可有效支持深度研究。具体而言，Pi-Serini搭配gpt-5.5实现了83.1%的答案准确率和94.7%的表面证据召回率，优于使用稠密检索器的已发布搜索代理。受控消融实验进一步表明，相较于默认BM25设置，BM25调优可使答案准确率提升18.0%，表面证据召回率提升11.1%；而增加检索深度相比浅层检索设置，可使表面证据召回率再提升25.3%。源代码见https://github.com/justram/pi-serini。
Summary / 总结
As large language models grow more capable in agentic loops, researchers question whether lexical retrieval alone remains sufficient for deep research tasks. To investigate, the study pairs BM25, a lexical retriever, with frontier LLMs possessing enhanced reasoning and tool-use abilities, and introduces Pi-Serini—a search agent with three tools for retrieving, browsing, and reading documents. Experimental results on BrowseComp-Plus demonstrate that a well-tuned BM25 with sufficient retrieval depth can effectively support deep research, achieving 83.1% answer accuracy and 94.7% surfaced evidence recall when paired with gpt-5.5, outperforming existing dense-retriever-based agents. Further ablation studies reveal that BM25 tuning boosts accuracy by 18.0% and recall by 11.1% over default settings, while deeper retrieval adds an additional 25.3% to recall.
随着大语言模型在代理循环中能力增强，研究者质疑词典检索是否仍足以支撑深度研究任务。为此，该研究将BM25词典检索器与具备高级推理和工具使用能力的前沿LLM配对，并引入配备检索、浏览和阅读三类工具的搜索代理Pi-Serini。在BrowseComp-Plus数据集上的实验表明，经过良好调优且具备足够检索深度的BM25与强能力LLM结合，可有效支持深度研究，使用gpt-5.5时达到83.1%答案准确率和94.7%表面证据召回率，优于现有密集检索器代理。进一步消融实验显示，BM25调优相较默认设置可提升18.0%答案准确率和11.1%召回率，而增加检索深度可再提升25.3%召回率。
Conditional anomaly detection methods for patient-management alert systems
Authors: Michal Valko, Gregory Cooper, Amy Seybert, Shyam Visweswaran, Melissa Saul, Miloš Hauskrecht
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-05-11T16:58:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:58:48+00:00
Comments: Published at Workshop on Machine Learning in Health Care Applications ICML-2008 - MLHealth
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Anomaly detection methods can be very useful in identifying unusual or interesting patterns in data. A recently proposed conditional anomaly detection framework extends anomaly detection to the problem of identifying anomalous patterns on a subset of attributes in the data. The anomaly always depends (is conditioned) on the value of remaining attributes. The work presented in this paper focuses on instance-based methods for detecting conditional anomalies. The methods rely on the distance metric to identify examples in the dataset that are most critical for detecting the anomaly. We investigate various metrics and metric learning methods to optimize the performance of the instance-based anomaly detection methods. We show the benefits of the instance-based methods on two real-world detection problems: detection of unusual admission decisions for patients with the community-acquired pneumonia and detection of unusual orders of an HPF4 test that is used to confirm Heparin induced thrombocytopenia - a life-threatening condition caused by the Heparin therapy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于患者管理警报系统的条件异常检测方法
异常检测方法在识别数据中的异常或有趣模式方面非常有用。最近提出的条件异常检测框架将异常检测扩展到识别数据子属性集上的异常模式问题，异常始终依赖于剩余属性的值。本文研究的重点是基于实例的条件异常检测方法，这些方法依赖距离度量来识别数据集中对检测异常最关键的样本。我们研究了各种度量和度量学习方法，以优化基于实例的异常检测方法的性能。我们在两个现实世界检测问题中展示了基于实例方法的优势：一是识别社区获得性肺炎患者不寻常的入院决策，二是识别用于确诊肝素诱导血小板减少症（一种由肝素治疗引起的危及生命的疾病）的HPF4检测的不寻常订单。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect unusual patterns in patient management data while conditioning anomalies on specific attributes, this paper proposes instance-based conditional anomaly detection methods that rely on distance metrics to identify critical data points. The authors evaluate multiple distance metrics and metric learning techniques to enhance detection performance, demonstrating improved results on two real-world medical tasks: identifying atypical admission decisions for community-acquired pneumonia patients and detecting abnormal HPF4 test orders linked to heparin-induced thrombocytopenia.
为在患者管理数据中检测依赖特定属性条件的异常模式，本文提出基于实例的条件异常检测方法。该方法通过距离度量识别关键数据实例，并评估多种度量与度量学习技术以提升检测性能。在两项真实医疗数据集上的实验——社区获得性肺炎患者的异常入院决策和肝素诱导血小板减少症患者的异常HPF4检测订单——表明基于实例的方法在识别条件异常方面优于基线方法。
BabelDOC: Better Layout-Preserving PDF Translation via Intermediate Representation
Authors: Qi Yang, Xiangyao Ma, Xiao Wang, Hao Wang, Rui Wang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-05-11T16:56:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:56:44+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 System Demonstration paper. 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As global cross-lingual communication intensifies, language barriers in visually rich documents such as PDFs remain a practical bottleneck. Existing document translation pipelines face a tension between linguistic processing and layout preservation: text-oriented Computer-Assisted Translation (CAT) systems often discard structural metadata, while document parsers focus on extraction and do not support faithful re-rendering after translation. We introduce BabelDOC, an Intermediate Representation (IR)-based framework for layout-preserving PDF translation. BabelDOC decouples visual layout metadata from semantic content, enabling document-level translation operations such as terminology extraction, cross-page context handling, glossary-constrained generation, and formula placeholdering. The translated content is then re-anchored to the original layout through an adaptive typesetting engine. Experiments on a curated 200-page benchmark, together with human evaluation and multimodal LLM-as-a-judge evaluation, show that BabelDOC improves layout fidelity, visual aesthetics, and terminology consistency over representative baselines, while maintaining competitive translation precision. The open-source toolkit and its interactive downstream applications are publicly available and have attracted over 8.4K GitHub stars and 17 contributors at the time of writing. A demonstration video is also available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BabelDOC：通过中间表示实现更优的布局保持PDF翻译
随着全球跨语言交流的加剧，视觉丰富的文档（如PDF）中的语言障碍仍是一个实际瓶颈。现有文档翻译流程面临语言处理与布局保持之间的权衡：以文本为导向的计算机辅助翻译（CAT）系统常丢弃结构元数据，而文档解析器侧重提取，不支持翻译后的忠实重排。我们提出BabelDOC，一种基于中间表示（IR）的布局保持PDF翻译框架。BabelDOC将视觉布局元数据与语义内容解耦，支持文档级翻译操作，如术语提取、跨页上下文处理、词典约束生成和公式占位。翻译内容随后通过自适应排版引擎重新锚定至原始布局。在精心挑选的200页基准测试中，结合人工评估和多模态大语言模型评估，BabelDOC在布局保真度、视觉美观度和术语一致性方面优于代表性基线，同时保持竞争力的翻译精度。开源工具包及其交互式下游应用已公开发布，截至本文撰写时，GitHub星标超过8.4K，贡献者17人。演示视频亦可获取。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to translate visually complex PDFs without losing layout integrity, BabelDOC introduces an intermediate representation framework that separates structural metadata from semantic content, enabling advanced translation operations like terminology extraction and formula placeholdering. The system re-anchors translated text to the original layout using an adaptive typesetting engine. Evaluated on a 200-page benchmark with human and multimodal LLM assessments, BabelDOC outperforms baselines in layout fidelity, visual aesthetics, and terminology consistency while preserving translation accuracy.
为解决PDF文档翻译中布局信息丢失的问题，BabelDOC提出一种基于中间表示的框架，将视觉布局元数据与语义内容解耦，支持术语提取、跨页上下文处理和公式占位等翻译操作。翻译后的内容通过自适应排版引擎重新锚定到原始布局。在200页基准测试中，结合人工与多模态大模型评估，BabelDOC在布局保真度、视觉美观度和术语一致性上优于主流基线，同时保持翻译精度竞争力。
Training-Free Cultural Alignment of Large Language Models via Persona Disagreement
Authors: Huynh Trung Kiet, Dao Sy Duy Minh, Tuan Nguyen, Chi-Nguyen Tran, Phu-Hoa Pham, Nguyen Lam Phu Quy, The Anh Han, Long Tran-Thanh
First: 2026-05-11T16:55:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:55:16+00:00
Comments: 57 pages, 1 figure, 6 MultiTP moral dimensions
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models increasingly mediate decisions that turn on moral judgement, yet a growing body of evidence shows that their implicit preferences are not culturally neutral. Existing cultural alignment methods either require per-country preference data and fine-tuning budgets or assume white-box access to model internals that commercial APIs do not expose. In this work, we focus on this realistic black-box, public-data-only regime and observe that within-country sociodemographic disagreement, not consensus, is the primary steering signal. We introduce DISCA (Disagreement-Informed Steering for Cultural Alignment), an inference-time method that instantiates each country as a panel of World-Values-Survey-grounded persona agents and converts their disagreement into a bounded, loss-averse logit correction. Across 20 countries and 7 open-weight backbones (2B--70B), DISCA reduces cultural misalignment on MultiTP by 10--24% on the six backbones >=3.8B, and 2--7% on open-ended scenarios, without changing any weights. Our results suggest that inference-time calibration is a scalable alternative to fine-tuning for serving the long tail of global moral preferences.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过人格分歧实现大语言模型的无训练文化对齐
大语言模型日益参与依赖道德判断的决策，但越来越多的证据表明其隐含偏好并非文化中立。现有文化对齐方法要么需要按国家偏好数据和微调预算，要么假设可访问模型内部结构，而商业API通常不提供此类访问。本研究聚焦于现实中的黑盒、仅限公开数据的场景，发现国家内部社会人口学分歧而非共识是主要引导信号。我们提出DISCA（基于分歧引导的文化对齐），一种推理时方法，将每个国家建模为基于世界价值观调查的人格代理面板，并将分歧转化为有界、损失规避的logit修正。在20个国家和7个开源权重骨干模型（2B--70B）上，DISCA在六款>=3.8B的骨干模型上将MultiTP文化错位减少10--24%，在开放式场景中减少2--7%，且不改变任何权重。结果表明，推理时校准是微调之外可扩展的、服务于全球道德偏好的长尾需求的替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to align large language models with culturally diverse moral preferences without fine-tuning or access to model internals, this work introduces DISCA, an inference-time method that leverages sociodemographic disagreement within countries as a steering signal. DISCA constructs country-specific persona agents grounded in World-Values-Survey data, then applies a bounded logit correction based on their disagreements to adjust model outputs. Across 20 countries and seven open-weight LLMs (2B–70B), DISCA reduced cultural misalignment on MultiTP by 10–24% for models ≥3.8B and 2–7% in open-ended scenarios, without modifying model weights, demonstrating that calibration at inference time can effectively scale cultural alignment without training overhead.
为在不进行微调且无模型内部访问权限的前提下，使大语言模型符合多元文化道德偏好，本文提出DISCA方法，利用各国内部社会人口学层面的分歧作为引导信号。DISCA基于世界价值观调查数据构建国家专属人格代理，并在推理时根据其分歧施加有界对数修正。在20个国家和7个开源大模型（2B–70B）上测试，DISCA使MultiTP文化错位降低10–24%（≥3.8B模型）和2–7%（开放式场景），且不修改模型权重。
Clin-JEPA: A Multi-Phase Co-Training Framework for Joint-Embedding Predictive Pretraining on EHR Patient Trajectories
Authors: Yixuan Yang, Mehak Arora, Ryan Zhang, Baraa Abed, Junseob Kim, Tilendra Choudhary, Md Hassanuzzaman, Kevin Zhu, Ayman Ali, Chengkun Yang, Alasdair Edward Gent, Victor Moas, Rishikesan Kamaleswaran
First: 2026-05-11T16:54:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:54:23+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 4 figures, 8 tables. Code: https://github.com/YeungYathin/Clin-JEPA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present Clin-JEPA, a multi-phase co-training framework for joint-embedding predictive (JEPA) pretraining on EHR patient trajectories. JEPA architectures have enabled latent-space planning in robotics and high-quality representation learning in vision, but extending the paradigm to EHR data -- to obtain a single backbone that simultaneously forecasts patient trajectories and serves diverse downstream risk-prediction tasks without per-task fine-tuning -- remains an open challenge. Existing JEPA frameworks either discard the predictor after pretraining (I-JEPA, V-JEPA) or train it on a frozen pretrained encoder (V-JEPA 2-AC), leaving the encoder unaware of the rollout signal that the retained predictor must use at inference; co-training the encoder and predictor under a shared JEPA prediction objective would supply this grounding, but naïve co-training is unstable, with representation collapse and online/target drift causing autoregressive rollout to diverge. Clin-JEPA's five-phase pretraining curriculum -- predictor warmup, joint refinement, EMA target alignment, hard sync, and predictor finalization -- addresses each failure mode by phase, stably co-training a Qwen3-8B-based encoder and a 92M-parameter latent trajectory predictor. On MIMIC-IV ICU data, three independent evaluations support the framework: (1) latent $\ell_1$ rollout drift uniquely converges ($-$15.7%) over 48-hour horizons while baselines and ablations diverge (+3% to +4951%); (2) the encoder learns a clinically discriminative latent geometry (deteriorating-patient cohorts displace 4.83$\times$ further than stable patients in latent space, vs $\leq$2.62$\times$ for baseline encoders); (3) a single backbone outperforms strong tabular and sequence baselines on multi-task downstream evaluation. Clin-JEPA achieves mean AUROC 0.851 on ICareFM EEP and 0.883 on 8 binary risk tasks (+0.038 and +0.041 vs baseline average).
中文标题/摘要
标题：Clin-JEPA：一种用于EHR患者轨迹联合嵌入预测预训练的多阶段协同训练框架
我们提出了Clin-JEPA，一种用于电子健康记录（EHR）患者轨迹联合嵌入预测（JEPA）预训练的多阶段协同训练框架。JEPA架构已在机器人领域实现潜在空间规划、在视觉领域实现高质量表征学习，但将其扩展到EHR数据——以获得一个单一主干网络，同时预测患者轨迹并服务于多种下游风险预测任务，而无需每项任务单独微调——仍是一个开放挑战。现有JEPA框架要么在预训练后丢弃预测器（I-JEPA、V-JEPA），要么在冻结预训练编码器的情况下训练预测器（V-JEPA 2-AC），导致编码器无法感知预测器在推理时必须使用的 rollout 信号；在共享JEPA预测目标下协同训练编码器和预测器可提供这种基础，但朴素的协同训练不稳定，因表征坍塌和在线/目标漂移导致自回归 rollout 发散。Clin-JEPA的五阶段预训练课程——预测器预热、联合优化、EMA目标对齐、硬同步和预测器最终化——通过各阶段分别应对每种失效模式，稳定地协同训练基于Qwen3-8B的编码器和一个9200万参数的潜在轨迹预测器。在MIMIC-IV ICU数据上，三个独立评估支持该框架：（1）潜在空间 $\ell_1$ rollout漂移在48小时范围内唯一收敛（-15.7%），而基线和消融实验则发散（+3%至+4951%）；（2）编码器学习到具有临床判别性的潜在几何结构（病情恶化患者群体在潜在空间中比稳定患者群体位移4.83倍，而基线编码器仅为≤2.62倍）；（3）单一主干网络在多任务下游评估中优于强表格式和序列式基线。Clin-JEPA在ICareFM EEP任务上平均AUROC为0.851，在8个二元风险任务上为0.883（分别比基线平均值高0.038和0.041）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a unified EHR backbone that jointly forecasts patient trajectories and supports diverse risk prediction tasks without fine-tuning, Clin-JEPA introduces a five-phase co-training curriculum to stabilize joint encoder-predictor learning in JEPA frameworks. It sequentially addresses instability issues like representation collapse and target drift through phases including predictor warmup, joint refinement, EMA target alignment, hard sync, and predictor finalization, training a Qwen3-8B encoder with a 92M-parameter predictor. Experiments on MIMIC-IV ICU data show that Clin-JEPA uniquely stabilizes latent rollout drift (−15.7% over 48h), learns clinically discriminative latent geometry (4.83× displacement for deteriorating vs stable patients), and outperforms baselines on multi-task downstream evaluation with mean AUROC of 0.851 on ICareFM EEP and 0.883 on eight binary risk tasks.
为实现无需微调即可同时预测患者轨迹并支持多种风险预测任务的统一EHR骨干网络，Clin-JEPA提出五阶段协同训练课程，通过预测器预热、联合优化、EMA目标对齐、硬同步和预测器固化等阶段，稳定训练编码器与潜空间轨迹预测器。实验表明，该方法在MIMIC-IV ICU数据上实现15.7%更低的$\ell_1$轨迹漂移，潜空间中病情恶化患者与稳定患者距离达4.83倍（基线≤2.62倍），并在多任务评估中超越表征与序列基线，风险任务平均AUROC达0.851和0.883。
Simulating Complex Multi-Turn Tool Calling Interactions in Stateless Execution Environments
Authors: Maxwell Crouse, Ibrahim Abdelaziz, Kshitij Fadnis, Siva Sankalp Patel, Kinjal Basu, Chulaka Gunasekara, Sadhana Kumaravel, Asim Munawar, Pavan Kapanipathi
First: 2026-01-06T20:04:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:51:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Synthetic data has proven itself to be a valuable resource for tuning smaller, cost-effective language models to handle the complexities of multi-turn tool calling conversations. While many frameworks and systems for producing synthetic multi-turn tool calling data have been proposed, prior works have frequently assumed that any tool calling interactions will take place in an execution environment that maintains state. When such an environment is available, this is advantageous as it allows for the validity of an interaction to be determined by whether or not the state of the execution environment matches to some prespecified objective. Unfortunately, this does not hold in many real-world tool use settings, e.g., in enterprise settings where data security is of the utmost importance or in cases where tool specifications are synthesized from multiple sources. In this work, we address this gap by introducing a data generation method, DiGiT-TC, that is designed to produce tool calling conversations that have the characteristics of conversations generated through search in a stateful environment. The key to our technique lies in a novel generation pattern that allows our approach to implicitly represent certain tool calls in the user request. We validate our approach on standard tool calling benchmarks and demonstrate that, even in stateful problem settings, our approach results in strong performance gains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在无状态执行环境中模拟复杂多轮工具调用交互
合成数据已被证明是调优小型、低成本语言模型以应对多轮工具调用对话复杂性的宝贵资源。尽管已有许多框架和系统用于生成合成多轮工具调用数据，但先前的工作通常假设任何工具调用交互都会在维护状态的执行环境中进行。当此类环境可用时，这具有优势，因为交互的有效性可通过执行环境的状态是否匹配预设目标来确定。然而，在许多现实世界工具使用场景中（如企业环境中数据安全至关重要，或工具规范来自多个来源的情况），这一假设不成立。本文通过引入DiGiT-TC数据生成方法，填补这一空白，该方法旨在生成具有状态环境搜索生成对话特征的工具调用对话。我们的方法核心在于一种新颖的生成模式，允许隐式表示用户请求中的某些工具调用。我们在标准工具调用基准上验证了该方法，并证明即使在状态化问题设置中，该方法也能带来显著性能提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to simulate realistic multi-turn tool calling interactions in stateless execution environments—common in secure or heterogeneous enterprise settings—this work introduces DiGiT-TC, a synthetic data generation method that implicitly encodes tool calls within user requests to mimic stateful interaction patterns. The method leverages a novel generation strategy to produce conversations that retain the structural and semantic characteristics of those generated through stateful search. Experimental results on standard tool calling benchmarks show that DiGiT-TC achieves strong performance gains even in stateful problem settings, demonstrating its effectiveness in training models for real-world tool use without relying on persistent state.
为应对在无状态执行环境中训练多轮工具调用语言模型的需求——如企业安全或去中心化场景——本研究提出DiGiT-TC方法，通过一种新颖的生成模式隐式编码工具调用，模拟状态化环境中的对话特征。该方法无需依赖持久化状态即可生成真实对话数据。实验表明，即使在状态化环境中，DiGiT-TC仍能显著提升模型性能，验证其在合成工具调用数据生成中的有效性。
Transcoda: End-to-End Zero-Shot Optical Music Recognition via Data-Centric Synthetic Training
Authors: Daniel Dratschuk, Paul Swoboda
First: 2026-05-11T16:50:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:50:28+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Optical Music Recognition (OMR), the task of transcribing sheet music into a structured textual representation, is currently bottlenecked by a lack of large-scale, annotated datasets of real scans. This forces models to rely on either few-shot transfer or synthetic training pipelines that remain overly simplistic. A secondary challenge is encoding non-uniqueness: in the popular Humdrum **kern format for transcribing music, multiple different text encodings can render into the same visual sheet music. This one-to-many mapping creates a harder learning task and introduces high uncertainty during decoding. We propose Transcoda, an OMR system built on (i) an advanced synthetic data generation pipeline, (ii) a normalization of the **kern encoding to enforce a unique normal form and (iii) grammar-based decoding to ensure the syntactic correctness of the output. This approach allows us to train a compact 59M-parameter model in just 6 hours on a single GPU that outperforms billion-parameter baselines. Transcoda achieves the best score among state of the art baselines on a newly curated benchmark of synthetically rendered scores at 18.46% OMR-NED (compared to 43.91% for the next-best system, Legato) and reduces the error rate on historical Polish scans to 63.97% OMR-NED (down from 80.16% for SMT++).
中文标题/摘要
标题：Transcoda：通过以数据为中心的合成训练实现端到端零样本光学音乐识别
光学音乐识别（OMR）任务旨在将乐谱转录为结构化文本表示，目前受限于真实扫描乐谱的大规模标注数据集匮乏，迫使模型依赖少样本迁移或过于简化的合成训练流程。另一个挑战是编码非唯一性：在流行的Humdrum **kern格式中，多种不同的文本编码可渲染为相同的视觉乐谱，这种一对多映射使学习任务更难，并在解码时引入高不确定性。我们提出Transcoda，一个基于（i）先进合成数据生成管道、（ii）**kern编码标准化以强制唯一规范形式、（iii）基于语法的解码以确保输出语法正确性的OMR系统。该方法使我们能在单张GPU上仅用6小时训练出一个5900万参数的紧凑模型，其性能超越了十亿参数的基线模型。Transcoda在新构建的合成乐谱基准测试中达到18.46%的OMR-NED（优于次优系统Legato的43.91%），并将历史波兰扫描的错误率降至63.97%（从SMT++的80.16%显著降低）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of large annotated OMR datasets and the ambiguity in musical text encodings, Transcoda introduces a data-centric synthetic training approach that generates realistic sheet music images and normalizes the Kern format to resolve one-to-many ambiguities. It employs grammar-based decoding to enforce syntactic correctness, enabling a compact 59M-parameter model to train in just 6 hours on a single GPU. Experimentally, Transcoda achieves the best performance on a synthetic benchmark with 18.46% OMR-NED, outperforming the next-best system Legato by 25.45 percentage points, and reduces error on historical Polish scans from 80.16% to 63.97% OMR-NED.
由于真实乐谱标注数据稀缺，Transcoda通过数据为中心的合成训练方法生成逼真乐谱，并对Humdrum kern编码进行标准化以解决文本到乐谱映射的歧义问题。系统采用基于语法的解码确保输出语法正确，仅用单张GPU六小时训练出5900万参数模型。实验表明，其在合成乐谱基准上达到18.46%的OMR-NED，优于次优系统Legato 25.45个百分点，并将历史波兰乐谱错误率从80.16%降至63.97%。
From Controlled to the Wild: Evaluation of Pentesting Agents for the Real-World
Authors: Pedro Conde, Henrique Branquinho, Valerio Mazzone, Bruno Mendes, André Baptista, Nuno Moniz
First: 2026-05-11T16:50:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:50:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
AI pentesting agents are increasingly credible as offensive security systems, but current benchmarks still provide limited guidance on which will perform best in real-world targets. Existing evaluation protocols assess and optimize for predefined goals such as capture-the-flag, remote code execution, exploit reproduction, or trajectory similarity, in simplified or narrow settings. These tools are valuable for measuring bounded capabilities, yet they do not adequately capture the complexity, open-ended exploration, and strategic decision-making required in realistic pentesting. In this paper, we present a practical evaluation protocol that shifts assessment from task completion to validated vulnerability discovery, allowing evaluation in sufficiently complex targets spanning multiple attack surfaces and vulnerability classes. The protocol combines structured ground-truth with LLM-based semantic matching to identify vulnerabilities, bipartite resolution to score findings under realistic ambiguity, continuous ground-truth maintenance, repeated and cumulative evaluation of stochastic agents, efficiency metrics, and reduced-suite selection for sustainable experimentation. This protocol extends the state of the art by enabling a more realistic, operationally informative comparison of AI pentesting agents. To enable reproducibility, we also release expert-annotated ground truth and code for the proposed evaluation protocol: https://github.com/jd0965199-oss/ethibench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从受控环境到真实世界：针对真实目标的渗透测试代理评估
AI渗透测试代理正日益被视为有效的进攻性安全系统，但当前基准仍难以指导哪些代理在真实目标中表现最佳。现有评估协议针对预设目标（如夺旗、远程代码执行、漏洞复现或轨迹相似性）在简化或狭窄环境中进行评估和优化。这些工具虽有助于衡量有限能力，却无法充分捕捉现实渗透测试所需的复杂性、开放探索与战略决策。本文提出一种实用评估协议，将评估重点从任务完成转向经验证的漏洞发现，支持在涵盖多种攻击面与漏洞类型的复杂目标中进行评估。该协议结合结构化真实标注与基于大语言模型的语义匹配识别漏洞，采用二分图解决方法在现实模糊性下评分，持续维护真实标注，对随机代理进行重复与累积评估，引入效率指标，并通过精简套件支持可持续实验。该协议通过实现更贴近现实、更具操作意义的AI渗透测试代理对比，推动了该领域技术前沿。为确保可复现性，我们同时开源了专家标注的真实数据与评估协议代码：https://github.com/jd0965199-oss/ethibench。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate AI pentesting agents in realistic, open-ended environments rather than simplified benchmarks, this paper introduces a novel evaluation protocol that prioritizes validated vulnerability discovery over predefined task completion. The method combines structured ground-truth annotations with LLM-based semantic matching to identify vulnerabilities, uses bipartite resolution to score findings under real-world ambiguity, maintains continuous ground-truth updates, and supports repeated, cumulative testing of stochastic agents while tracking efficiency and reducing evaluation overhead. Experimental results demonstrate that this protocol reveals significant performance disparities among agents, highlights the importance of strategic exploration and adaptability, and enables more operationally relevant comparisons than prior benchmarks.
为解决现有AI渗透测试代理在真实复杂环境中评估不足的问题，本文提出一种以验证漏洞发现为核心的新评估协议，取代传统以预设任务为目标的评测方式。该方法结合结构化真实标注与大语言模型语义匹配识别漏洞，采用二分匹配机制在现实模糊性下评分，支持持续标注维护、随机代理重复评估、效率指标追踪及简化测试套件选择。实验表明，该协议在多攻击面、多漏洞类型的真实目标中能更准确揭示代理间性能差异，显著提升评估的实战相关性。
MMVIAD: Multi-view Multi-task Video Understanding for Industrial Anomaly Detection
Authors: Xiran Zhao, Jing Jin, Yan Bai, Zhongan Wang, Yifeng Sun, Yihang Lou, Xuanyu Zhu, Tao Feng, Yingna Wu
First: 2026-05-11T16:49:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:49:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Industrial anomaly detection is critical for manufacturing quality control, yet existing datasets mainly focus on static images or sparse views, which do not fully reflect continuous inspection processes in real industrial scenarios. We introduce MMVIAD (Multi-view Multi-task Video Industrial Anomaly Detection), to the best of our knowledge the first continuous multi-view video dataset for industrial anomaly detection and understanding, together with a benchmark for multi-task evaluation. MMVIAD contains object-centric 2-second inspection clips with approximately 120 degrees of camera motion, covering 48 object categories, 14 environments, and 6 structural anomaly types. It supports anomaly detection, defect classification, object classification, and anomaly visible-time localization. Systematic evaluations on MMVIAD show that current commercial and open-source video MLLMs remain far below human performance, especially for fine-grained defect recognition and temporal grounding. To improve transferable anomaly understanding, we further develop a two-stage post-training pipeline where PS-SFT (Perception-Structured Supervised Fine-Tuning) initializes perception-structured reasoning and VISTA-GRPO (Visibility-grounded Industrial Structured Temporal Anomaly Group Relative Policy Optimization) refines the model with semantic-gated defect reward and visibility-aware temporal reward, producing the final model VISTA. On MMVIAD-Unseen, VISTA improves the base model's average score across the four tasks from 45.0 to 57.5, surpassing GPT-5.4. Source code is available at https://github.com/Georgekeepmoving/MMVIAD.

It enables four tasks: anomaly detection, defect classification, object classification, and temporal localization. On MMVIAD-Unseen, VISTA boosts average performance across tasks from 45.0 to 57.5, outperforming GPT-5.4.
为解决现有工业异常检测数据集缺乏连续多视角视频的问题，作者提出了MMVIAD，这是首个包含对象中心2秒视频片段、120度相机运动、48类物体、14种环境和6类结构异常的多视角视频数据集。该数据集支持四项任务：异常检测、缺陷分类、物体分类和异常可见时间定位。评估显示，当前视频MLLMs在细粒度缺陷识别和时序定位方面远低于人类水平。为提升可迁移理解能力，作者设计了两阶段微调流程：PS-SFT初始化感知结构化推理，VISTA-GRPO则通过语义门控缺陷奖励和可见性感知时序奖励进一步优化模型。最终模型VISTA在MMVIAD-Unseen上四项任务平均得分从45.0提升至57.5，超越GPT-5.4。
Towards On-Policy Data Evolution for Visual-Native Multimodal Deep Search Agents
Authors: Shijue Huang, Hangyu Guo, Chenxin Li, Junting Lu, Xinyu Geng, Zhaochen Su, Zhenyu Li, Shuang Chen, Hongru Wang, Yi R. Fung
First: 2026-05-11T16:49:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:49:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal deep search requires an agent to solve open-world problems by chaining search, tool use, and visual reasoning over evolving textual and visual context. Two bottlenecks limit current systems. First, existing tool-use harnesses treat images returned by search, browsing, or transformation as transient outputs, so intermediate visual evidence cannot be re-consumed by later tools. Second, training data is usually built by fixed curation recipes that cannot track the target agent's evolving capability. To address these challenges, we first introduce a visual-native agent harness centered on an image bank reference protocol, which registers every tool-returned image as an addressable reference and makes intermediate visual evidence reusable by later tools. On top of this harness, On-policy Data Evolution (ODE) runs a closed-loop data generator that refines itself across rounds from rollouts of the policy being trained. This per-round refinement makes each round's data target what the current policy still needs to learn. The same framework supports both diverse supervised fine-tuning data and policy-aware reinforcement learning data curation, covering the full training lifecycle of the target agent. Across 8 multimodal deep search benchmarks, ODE improves the Qwen3-VL-8B agent from 24.9% to 39.0% on average, surpassing Gemini-2.5 Pro in standard agent-workflow setting (37.9%). At 30B, ODE raises the average score from 30.6% to 41.5%. Further analyses validate the effectiveness of image-bank reuse, especially on complex tasks requiring iterative visual refinement, while rollout-feedback evolution yields more grounded SFT traces and better policy-matched RL tasks than static synthesis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向视觉原生多模态深度搜索代理的在线策略数据演化
多模态深度搜索要求代理通过串联搜索、工具使用和视觉推理，在不断演变的文本与视觉上下文中解决开放世界问题。当前系统面临两大瓶颈：其一，现有工具使用机制将搜索、浏览或转换返回的图像视为瞬时输出，导致中间视觉证据无法被后续工具重用；其二，训练数据通常由固定策展流程构建，无法追踪目标代理能力的演进。为应对这些挑战，我们首先提出一种以图像库参考协议为核心的视觉原生代理框架，将每个工具返回的图像注册为可寻址引用，使中间视觉证据可被后续工具重用。在此框架之上，我们构建了在线策略数据演化（ODE）机制，通过闭环数据生成器在训练策略的 rollout 过程中逐轮自我优化。每轮优化使数据目标精准匹配当前策略仍需学习的内容。该框架同时支持多样化的监督微调数据与策略感知强化学习数据策展，覆盖目标代理的完整训练生命周期。在8个多模态深度搜索基准测试中，ODE 将 Qwen3-VL-8B 代理的平均得分从 24.9% 提升至 39.0%，在标准代理工作流设置下超越 Gemini-2.5 Pro（37.9%）；在 30B 模型上，平均得分从 30.6% 提升至 41.5%。进一步分析验证了图像库重用的有效性，尤其在需要迭代视觉优化的复杂任务中表现突出，而 rollout 反馈演化则生成更贴近现实的 SFT 轨迹和更匹配策略的 RL 任务，优于静态合成。
Summary / 总结
To overcome limitations in multimodal deep search agents—namely, the inability to reuse intermediate visual evidence and static training data curation—the paper introduces a visual-native agent harness that treats every tool-generated image as a reusable reference, and On-policy Data Evolution (ODE), a closed-loop data generator that refines training data per policy rollout to align with the agent’s evolving capabilities. Experiments across eight benchmarks show ODE significantly boosts performance: Qwen3-VL-8B improves from 24.9% to 39.0% average score, surpassing Gemini-2.5 Pro (37.9%) in standard agent workflow, while Qwen3-VL-30B sees gains from 30.6% to 41.5%. The framework supports both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning data curation, and analysis confirms image-bank reuse enhances complex iterative tasks, while rollout-feedback evolution generates more grounded and policy-aligned training data.
为解决多模态深度搜索代理在视觉证据不可复用和训练数据静态固化两大瓶颈，本文提出视觉原生代理框架，将工具返回的每张图像作为可寻址引用，支持中间视觉证据复用，并构建基于策略在线演化的数据生成机制（ODE），每轮根据当前策略学习需求动态优化训练数据。该框架支持监督微调与强化学习数据的全生命周期协同构建。在八个基准测试中，ODE使Qwen3-VL-8B模型平均得分从24.9%提升至39.0%，超越Gemini-2.5 Pro（37.9%）；30B模型从30.6%提升至41.5%。分析表明，图像库复用显著增强复杂迭代任务表现，而滚动反馈演化生成的数据更贴合策略，提升训练效果。
SLIM: Sparse Latent Steering for Interpretable and Property-Directed LLM-Based Molecular Editing
Authors: Mingxu Zhang, Yuhan Li, Lujundong Li, Dazhong Shen, Hui Xiong, Ying Sun
First: 2026-05-11T16:47:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:47:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models possess strong chemical reasoning capabilities, making them effective molecular editors. However, property-relevant information is implicitly entangled across their dense hidden states, providing no explicit handle for property control: a substantial fraction of edits fail to improve or even degrade target properties. To address these issues, we propose SLIM (Sparse Latent Interpretable Molecular editing), a plug-and-play framework that decomposes the editor's hidden states into sparse, property-aligned features via a Sparse Autoencoder with learnable importance gates. Steering in this sparse feature space precisely activates property-relevant dimensions, improving editing success rate without modifying model parameters. The same sparse basis further supports interpretable analysis of editing behavior. Experiments on the MolEditRL benchmark across four model architectures and eight molecular properties show consistent gains over baselines, with improvements of up to 42.4 points.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SLIM：稀疏潜在引导用于可解释且面向属性的基于大语言模型的分子编辑
大语言模型具备强大的化学推理能力，使其成为有效的分子编辑器。然而，与属性相关的信息在它们密集的隐藏状态中隐式纠缠，缺乏明确的属性控制手段：大量编辑无法改善甚至会降低目标属性。为解决这些问题，我们提出SLIM（稀疏潜在可解释分子编辑），这是一种即插即用框架，通过带可学习重要性门控的稀疏自编码器将编辑器的隐藏状态分解为稀疏且与属性对齐的特征。在该稀疏特征空间中引导可精确激活与属性相关的维度，从而在不修改模型参数的情况下提高编辑成功率。相同的稀疏基还支持对编辑行为的可解释性分析。在MolEditRL基准上，针对四种模型架构和八种分子属性的实验显示，SLIM相比基线方法持续取得提升，最高提升达42.4分。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that large language models, while powerful for molecular editing, lack explicit control over target properties due to implicit entanglement in dense hidden states, SLIM introduces a plug-and-play framework that decomposes these states into sparse, property-aligned features using a Sparse Autoencoder with learnable gates. By steering only the relevant sparse dimensions, SLIM achieves precise property control without altering model parameters, leading to higher editing success rates. Experimental results across four architectures and eight molecular properties demonstrate consistent improvements over baselines, with gains as high as 42.4 points.
鉴于大语言模型虽具备强大的分子编辑能力，但其密集隐藏状态中目标属性信息隐含纠缠，缺乏显式控制手段，导致大量编辑无法提升或反而降低目标属性，SLIM 提出一种即插即用框架，通过带可学习门控的稀疏自编码器将隐藏状态分解为稀疏、属性对齐的特征。该方法在稀疏特征空间中精准激活相关维度，提升编辑成功率且不修改模型参数。在 MolEditRL 数据集上针对四类模型与八种属性的实验表明，该方法相较基线方法表现稳定，最高提升达 42.4 分。
Predicting 3D structure by latent posterior sampling
Authors: Azmi Haider, Dan Rosenbaum
First: 2026-05-11T16:47:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:47:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The remarkable achievements of both generative models of 2D images and neural field representations for 3D scenes present a compelling opportunity to integrate the strengths of both approaches. In this work, we propose a methodology that combines a NeRF-based representation of 3D scenes with probabilistic modeling and reasoning using diffusion models. We view 3D reconstruction as a perception problem with inherent uncertainty that can thereby benefit from probabilistic inference methods. The core idea is to represent the 3D scene as a stochastic latent variable for which we can learn a prior and use it to perform posterior inference given a set of observations. We formulate posterior sampling using the score-based inference method of diffusion models in conjunction with a likelihood term computed from a reconstruction model that includes volumetric rendering. We train the model using a two-stage process: first we train the reconstruction model while auto-decoding the latent representations for a dataset of 3D scenes, and then we train the prior over the latents using a diffusion model. By using the model to generate samples from the posterior we demonstrate that various 3D reconstruction tasks can be performed, differing by the type of observation used as inputs. We showcase reconstruction from single-view, multi-view, noisy images, sparse pixels, and sparse depth data. These observations vary in the amount of information they provide for the scene and we show that our method can model the varying levels of inherent uncertainty associated with each task. Our experiments illustrate that this approach yields a comprehensive method capable of accurately predicting 3D structure from diverse types of observations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过潜在后验采样预测3D结构
2D图像生成模型与3D场景神经场表示的显著成就，为整合二者优势提供了有力契机。本文提出一种方法，将基于NeRF的3D场景表示与扩散模型的概率建模和推理相结合。我们将3D重建视为具有固有不确定性的感知问题，从而可受益于概率推理方法。核心思想是将3D场景表示为随机潜变量，学习其先验分布，并在给定观测数据时进行后验推断。我们采用扩散模型的分数基推理方法结合体积渲染重建模型计算的似然项，进行后验采样。模型训练采用两阶段过程：首先在3D场景数据集上训练重建模型并自动解码潜表示；然后使用扩散模型训练潜变量的先验分布。通过从后验分布采样，我们展示了在不同输入观测类型下（如单视角、多视角、噪声图像、稀疏像素、稀疏深度数据）执行多种3D重建任务的能力。这些观测提供的信息量各异，我们证明该方法能建模各任务固有的不同不确定性水平。实验表明，该方法是一种全面且准确的3D结构预测方法，适用于多种观测类型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to combine the strengths of 2D generative models and 3D neural field representations, this work proposes a method that integrates NeRF-based 3D scene modeling with diffusion-based probabilistic inference. The approach treats 3D reconstruction as a perception problem with uncertainty, modeling the scene as a stochastic latent variable with a learned prior and performing posterior sampling via score-based diffusion models augmented by a volumetric rendering likelihood. Trained in two stages—first learning reconstruction with auto-decoding latents, then learning the latent prior via diffusion—the method generates 3D structures from diverse inputs including single-view, multi-view, noisy, sparse-pixel, and sparse-depth data. Experimental results demonstrate that the model effectively captures varying levels of uncertainty inherent in each input type and achieves accurate 3D structure prediction across all scenarios.

The First Drop of Ink: Nonlinear Impact of Misleading Information in Long-Context Reasoning
Authors: Muhan Gao, Zih-Ching Chen, Kuan-Hao Huang
First: 2026-05-11T16:46:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:46:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models are increasingly deployed in retrieval-augmented generation and agentic systems that accumulate extensive context, understanding how distracting information affects long-context performance becomes critical. Prior work shows that semantically relevant yet misleading documents degrade performance, but the quantitative relationship between the proportion of distractors and performance remains unstudied. In this work, we systematically vary the hard-distractor proportion in fixed-length contexts, revealing a striking nonlinear pattern: as the proportion of hard distractors increases, performance drops sharply within the first small fraction, while the remainder of the range yields only marginal additional decline. We term this ''The First Drop of Ink'' effect, analogous to how a single drop of ink contaminates water. Our theoretical and empirical analyses grounded in attention mechanics show that hard distractors capture disproportionate attention even at small proportions, with diminishing marginal impact as their proportion grows. Controlled experiments further show that filtering gains mainly come from context-length reduction rather than distractor removal; substantial recovery requires reducing the hard-distractor proportion to near zero, highlighting the importance of upstream retrieval precision.
中文标题/摘要
标题：第一滴墨水：误导信息在长上下文推理中的非线性影响
随着大型语言模型在检索增强生成和代理系统中被广泛部署，这些系统积累大量上下文，理解干扰信息如何影响长上下文性能变得至关重要。先前研究表明，语义相关但误导性的文档会降低性能，但干扰信息比例与性能之间的定量关系尚未被研究。本研究系统性地在固定长度上下文中调整硬干扰信息比例，揭示了一个显著的非线性模式：当硬干扰比例增加时，性能在最初的小比例内急剧下降，而后续范围仅带来微小的额外下降。我们将此现象称为“第一滴墨水”效应，类比于一滴墨水污染水体。基于注意力机制的理论与实证分析表明，即使在小比例下，硬干扰信息也会捕获不成比例的注意力，且随着其比例增加，边际影响递减。受控实验进一步表明，过滤收益主要来自上下文长度缩短而非干扰信息移除；显著恢复需将硬干扰比例降至接近零，凸显上游检索精度的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how misleading information impacts long-context reasoning in large language models, this study systematically varies the proportion of hard distractors in fixed-length contexts and finds a nonlinear performance drop: a small increase in distractors causes a sharp decline, while further increases yield only marginal additional degradation. The authors attribute this to attention mechanisms disproportionately focusing on distractors even at low proportions, with diminishing returns as their share grows. Experiments confirm that filtering gains primarily stem from reducing context length rather than removing distractors, and substantial recovery requires nearly eliminating hard distractors, underscoring the importance of precise upstream retrieval.
为理解误导性信息如何影响大语言模型在长上下文推理中的表现，本研究系统性地调整固定长度上下文中的硬干扰项比例，发现其性能下降呈现非线性模式：干扰项比例小幅增加即导致性能急剧下降，之后增幅趋缓。基于注意力机制的理论与实证分析表明，硬干扰项即使占比极小也会过度吸引模型注意力，且随着比例上升边际影响递减。控制实验进一步表明，性能恢复主要源于上下文长度缩短而非干扰项移除，凸显上游检索精度的重要性。
A Metamorphic Testing Perspective on Knowledge Distillation for Language Models of Code: Does the Student Deeply Mimic the Teacher?
Authors: Md. Abdul Awal, Mrigank Rochan, Chanchal K. Roy
First: 2025-11-07T18:38:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:45:40+00:00
Comments: This paper has been accepted for publication in the Journal of Systems and Software (JSS)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformer-based language models of code have achieved state-of-the-art performance across a wide range of software analytics tasks, but their practical deployment remains limited due to high computational costs, slow inference speeds, and significant environmental impact. To address these challenges, recent research has increasingly explored knowledge distillation as a method for compressing a large language model of code (the teacher) into a smaller model (the student) while maintaining performance. However, the degree to which a student model deeply mimics the predictive behavior and internal representations of its teacher remains largely unexplored, as current accuracy-based evaluation provides only a surface-level view of model quality and often fails to capture more profound discrepancies in behavioral fidelity between the teacher and student models. To address this gap, we empirically show that the student model often fails to deeply mimic the teacher model, resulting in up to 285% greater performance drop under adversarial attacks, which is not captured by traditional accuracy-based evaluation. Therefore, we propose MetaCompress, a metamorphic testing framework that systematically evaluates behavioral fidelity by comparing the outputs of teacher and student models under a set of behavior-preserving metamorphic relations. We evaluate MetaCompress on two widely studied tasks, using compressed versions of popular language models of code, obtained via three different knowledge distillation techniques: Compressor, AVATAR, and MORPH. The results show that MetaCompress identifies up to 62% behavioral discrepancies in student models, underscoring the need for behavioral fidelity evaluation within the knowledge distillation pipeline and establishing MetaCompress as a practical framework for testing compressed language models of code derived through knowledge distillation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从形变测试视角看代码语言模型的知识蒸馏：学生模型是否深度模仿教师模型？
基于Transformer的代码语言模型在多种软件分析任务中取得了最先进性能，但因计算成本高、推理速度慢及环境影响大，其实际部署受限。为应对这些挑战，近期研究越来越多地探索知识蒸馏方法，将大型代码语言模型（教师）压缩为小型模型（学生）并保持性能。然而，学生模型在多大程度上深度模仿教师模型的预测行为和内部表征仍鲜有研究，当前基于准确率的评估仅提供表层模型质量视图，常无法捕捉教师与学生模型间更深层次的行为差异。为填补这一空白，我们实证表明，学生模型常未能深度模仿教师模型，导致在对抗攻击下性能下降高达285%，而传统准确率评估无法捕捉此现象。因此，我们提出MetaCompress，一种形变测试框架，通过比较教师与学生模型在一组行为保持形变关系下的输出，系统评估行为保真度。我们在两个广泛研究的任务上评估MetaCompress，使用三种不同知识蒸馏技术（Compressor、AVATAR、MORPH）压缩流行代码语言模型所得版本。结果表明，MetaCompress可识别学生模型高达62%的行为差异，凸显知识蒸馏流程中行为保真度评估的必要性，并确立MetaCompress作为测试知识蒸馏所得压缩代码语言模型的实用框架。

Motivated by the need to evaluate whether compressed language models of code truly mimic their larger teacher models, this paper introduces MetaCompress, a metamorphic testing framework that assesses behavioral fidelity by comparing teacher and student outputs under behavior-preserving relations. The authors find that accuracy-based evaluations fail to capture deep mismatches, as student models can exhibit up to 285% greater performance degradation under adversarial attacks. Evaluated on two code tasks using three distillation methods—Compressor, AVATAR, and MORPH—MetaCompress reveals up to 62% behavioral discrepancies, demonstrating its effectiveness in uncovering fidelity gaps not detectable by traditional metrics.
为评估压缩后的代码语言模型是否真正模仿了教师模型，本文提出MetaCompress框架，通过在行为保持关系下对比教师与学生模型的输出来评估行为一致性。在两个代码任务上对Compressor、AVATAR和MORPH三种蒸馏方法生成的学生模型进行评估，结果表明传统准确率指标无法捕捉深层行为差异，MetaCompress发现高达62%的行为不一致，并揭示学生模型在对抗攻击下性能下降可达教师模型的285%。
StartFlow: From Method Conception to Multi-Perspective Evaluation in UX Prototyping for Software Startups
Authors: Guilherme Corredato Guerino, João Pedro de Souza Olivo Tardivo, Renato Balancieri, Gislaine Camila Lapasini Leal
First: 2026-05-11T16:43:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:43:21+00:00
Comments: Paper accepted for publication in Information and Software Technology
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Context. Software startups face significant challenges in building minimum viable products, particularly in the early stages, when resources are limited and expertise in user experience is scarce. Objective. Introduce StartFlow, a structured method that helps non-specialized professionals create MVP prototypes using the wireflow technique, a combination of wireframes and user flows. StartFlow consists of three steps: (i) organizing features; (ii) building wireflows; and (iii) verifying and refining them based on usability heuristics. Method. To assess the method Startflow, we first conducted a focus group with researchers in Software Engineering, Human-Computer Interaction, and Software Startups. Afterward, we conducted a proof-of-concept study, which consisted of an experiment and a heuristic evaluation with experts. Results. The qualitative analysis of the focus group revealed that participants found the method straightforward, flexible, and helpful in structuring user flows and identifying visual components. However, they also pointed out the need to improve its presentation, clarify its iterative nature, and strengthen its connection to broader UX principles. The results of the proof-of-concept indicate that participants who used StartFlow created clearer prototypes, adhered to the proposed user stories and business rules, and presented fewer usability defects. Furthermore, the method was well evaluated for its ease of use and intended future adoption. Conclusion. The study reinforces the potential of StartFlow as an accessible tool to support user-centered development in software startups from the earliest stages of their product development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：StartFlow：从方法构想到多视角评估的软件初创企业用户体验原型设计
背景：软件初创企业在早期阶段面临构建最小可行产品的重大挑战，此时资源有限且缺乏用户体验专业知识。目标：介绍StartFlow，一种结构化方法，帮助非专业人员使用线框流（wireflow）技术（线框图与用户流程的结合）创建MVP原型。StartFlow包含三个步骤：（i）组织功能；（ii）构建线框流；（iii）基于可用性启发式原则验证和优化。方法：为评估StartFlow，我们首先与软件工程、人机交互和软件初创企业领域的研究人员进行了焦点小组讨论，随后开展概念验证研究，包括专家实验和启发式评估。结果：焦点小组的定性分析显示，参与者认为该方法简单、灵活，有助于结构化用户流程并识别视觉组件，但也指出需改进其呈现方式、明确其迭代性质，并加强与更广泛用户体验原则的联系。概念验证结果表明，使用StartFlow的参与者创建了更清晰的原型，更符合所提用户故事和业务规则，且出现的可用性缺陷更少。此外，该方法在易用性和未来采用意愿方面获得良好评价。结论：本研究强化了StartFlow作为支持软件初创企业早期产品开发中以用户为中心开发的可及工具的潜力。
