为了解决软件初创企业在资源有限、缺乏UX专业能力情况下构建MVP的难题，研究提出了StartFlow方法，通过结合线框图与用户流程的“线流”技术，帮助非专业人士进行原型设计。该方法包含三个步骤：整理功能、构建线流、依据可用性启发式原则进行验证与优化。通过焦点小组和概念验证实验评估发现，参与者认为该方法直观灵活，有助于梳理用户流程和识别视觉元素，但建议改进呈现方式、明确迭代机制并加强与通用UX原则的关联。实验结果表明，使用StartFlow的用户生成的原型更清晰，更符合用户故事与业务规则，可用性缺陷更少，且对方法的易用性与未来采用意愿评价较高。
Restoring Exploration after Post-Training: Latent Exploration Decoding for Large Reasoning Models
Authors: Wenhui Tan, Fiorenzo Parascandolo, Enver Sangineto, Jianzhong Ju, Zhenbo Luo, Qian Cao, Rita Cucchiara, Ruihua Song, Jian Luan
First: 2026-02-02T06:12:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:43:17+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://github.com/AlbertTan404/LED
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have recently achieved strong mathematical and code reasoning performance through Reinforcement Learning (RL) post-training. However, we show that modern reasoning post-training induces an unintended exploration collapse: temperature-based sampling no longer increases pass@$n$ accuracy. Empirically, the final-layer posterior of post-trained LRMs exhibit sharply reduced entropy, while the entropy of intermediate layers remains relatively high. Motivated by this entropy asymmetry, we propose Latent Exploration Decoding (LED), a depth-conditioned decoding strategy. LED aggregates intermediate posteriors via cumulative sum and selects depth configurations with maximal entropy as exploration candidates. Without additional training or parameters, LED consistently improves pass@1 and pass@16 accuracy by 0.61 and 1.03 percentage points across multiple reasoning benchmarks and models. Furthermore, integrating LED into reinforcement learning, e.g., using GRPO as the rollout strategy, yields faster reward improvement and higher final performance, due to the efficient exploration capability of LED. Project page: https://github.com/AlbertTan404/LED.
中文标题/摘要
标题：后训练阶段恢复探索：大型推理模型的潜在探索解码
大型推理模型（LRMs）通过强化学习（RL）后训练最近在数学和代码推理方面取得了优异表现。然而，我们发现现代推理后训练会导致意外的探索崩溃：基于温度的采样不再提升pass@$n$准确率。实证表明，后训练LRMs的最终层后验熵显著降低，而中间层熵仍保持较高水平。受此熵不对称性启发，我们提出潜在探索解码（LED），一种深度条件解码策略。LED通过累加和聚合中间后验，并选择熵最大的深度配置作为探索候选。无需额外训练或参数，LED在多个推理基准和模型上持续提升pass@1和pass@16准确率，分别提高0.61和1.03个百分点。此外，将LED集成到强化学习中（如使用GRPO作为 rollout 策略），可实现更快的奖励提升和更高的最终性能，得益于LED高效的探索能力。项目页面：https://github.com/AlbertTan404/LED。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that post-training of large reasoning models leads to an exploration collapse—where temperature-based sampling no longer boosts accuracy—the authors propose Latent Exploration Decoding (LED), a decoding strategy that leverages entropy asymmetry across model layers. LED aggregates intermediate layer posteriors using cumulative sums and selects depth configurations with maximal entropy as exploration candidates, without requiring additional training or parameters. Experiments show LED consistently improves pass@1 and pass@16 accuracy by 0.61 and 1.03 percentage points across multiple benchmarks and models, and when integrated with reinforcement learning methods like GRPO, it accelerates reward convergence and enhances final performance.
该论文针对大推理模型在强化学习后训练中出现的探索坍缩问题——即温度采样不再提升准确率——提出了解决方案。作者发现，虽然最终层后验熵显著降低，但中间层仍保持较高熵值，表明存在潜在探索空间。为此，他们设计了潜层探索解码（LED）方法，通过累积求和聚合中间层后验，并选择熵最大的深度配置作为探索候选。无需额外训练或参数，LED在多个基准测试中分别提升pass@1和pass@16准确率0.61和1.03个百分点。当与GRPO等强化学习算法结合时，LED能加速奖励收敛并提高最终性能，得益于其高效的探索能力。
NoRIN: Backbone-Adaptive Reversible Normalization for Time-Series Forecasting
Authors: Shun Zhang, Yuyang Xiao
First: 2026-05-11T16:42:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:42:52+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reversible instance normalization (RevIN) and its successors (Dish-TS, SAN, FAN) have become the de facto plug-in for time-series forecasting, yet the map they apply to each data point is strictly affine, $x \mapsto ax+b$, so they cannot reshape the underlying distribution -- heavy tails remain heavy and skewness remains uncorrected. We propose NoRIN, a non-linear reversible normalization based on the arcsinh-form Johnson $S_U$ transform with two shape parameters $(δ,\varepsilon)$ that control tailedness and skewness; the linear $Z$-score used by RevIN is recovered only in the limit $δ\to \infty$. Training $(δ,\varepsilon)$ jointly with the backbone via gradient descent reliably pushes them toward this linear limit within a few epochs -- a phenomenon we name the degeneration problem: the forecasting loss is locally indifferent to shape, and the high-capacity backbone compensates for any monotone reparameterization of its input. NoRIN escapes the degeneration by decoupling shape selection from gradient training: $(δ,\varepsilon)$ are initialized by a closed-form Slifker-Shapiro quantile fit and refined by Bayesian optimization on the validation objective, while the inner training loop is identical to standard RevIN-style training. Across six representative backbones x five real-world datasets x three prediction horizons (90 configurations), decoupled shape optimization recovers $(δ^\star,\varepsilon^\star)$ that sit systematically far from the linear limit, with values that vary in a backbone-dependent way. This empirically supports the central thesis: different backbones genuinely require different normalization parameters to reach their best performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NoRIN：面向时间序列预测的自适应可逆归一化骨干网络
可逆实例归一化（RevIN）及其后续方法（Dish-TS、SAN、FAN）已成为时间序列预测的默认插件，但它们对每个数据点应用的映射严格为仿射变换 $x \mapsto ax+b$，因而无法重塑底层分布——重尾保持重尾，偏度未被修正。我们提出NoRIN，一种基于arcsinh形式Johnson $S_U$变换的非线性可逆归一化方法，其包含两个形状参数 $(δ,\varepsilon)$，分别控制尾部厚度与偏度；RevIN所用的线性Z分数仅在 $δ\to \infty$ 极限下恢复。通过梯度下降联合训练骨干网络与 $(δ,\varepsilon)$，可在数个epoch内可靠地将其推向该线性极限——我们称之为退化问题：预测损失对形状参数局部无敏感性，高容量骨干网络可补偿其输入的任何单调重参数化。NoRIN通过将形状选择与梯度训练解耦来规避退化：$(δ,\varepsilon)$ 由闭式Slifker-Shapiro分位数拟合初始化，并通过验证目标上的贝叶斯优化进行精调，而内部训练循环与标准RevIN式训练相同。在六个代表性骨干网络 × 五个真实数据集 × 三个预测时长（共90种配置）的实验中，解耦形状优化恢复出的 $(δ^\star,\varepsilon^\star)$ 系统性地远离线性极限，其值随骨干网络而异。这实证支持核心论点：不同骨干网络确实需要不同的归一化参数以达到最佳性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of affine reversible normalization in time-series forecasting that fails to correct heavy tails and skewness, the paper introduces NoRIN, a non-linear reversible normalization using the arcsinh-form Johnson S_U transform with shape parameters δ and ε. These parameters are initialized via a closed-form quantile fit and refined using Bayesian optimization on validation data, decoupling shape selection from gradient training to avoid the degeneration problem where backbones compensate for normalization. Experiments across six backbones, five datasets, and three horizons show that NoRIN consistently recovers shape parameters far from the linear limit, with backbone-dependent optimal values, demonstrating that different architectures require distinct normalization settings to achieve peak performance.
为了解决现有可逆归一化方法（如RevIN）仅能进行仿射变换、无法调整数据分布尾部和偏度的问题，本文提出NoRIN，基于arcsinh形式的Johnson S_U变换，引入两个形状参数δ和ε分别控制尾部和偏度。与RevIN仅在δ趋于无穷时恢复线性Z-score不同，NoRIN将形状参数优化与梯度训练解耦：δ和ε通过闭式分位数拟合初始化，再用贝叶斯优化在验证集上微调，而骨干网络训练保持标准流程。在六种骨干网络、五个真实数据集和三种预测时长的实验中，解耦优化所得参数显著偏离线性极限，且随骨干网络变化，实证表明不同架构确实需要不同的归一化参数才能达到最佳性能。
Benchmarking Sensor-Fault Robustness in Forecasting
Authors: Alexander Windmann, Philipp Wittenberg, Gianluca Manca, Marcel Dix, Jens U. Brandt, Oliver Niggemann
First: 2026-05-11T16:41:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:41:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cyber-physical system (CPS) forecasting models depend on sensor streams with noisy, biased, missing, or temporally misaligned readings, yet standard forecasting evaluation often selects models by nominal error without showing whether they remain robust under such faults. We introduce SensorFault-Bench, a shared CPS-grounded sensor-fault stress-test protocol for evaluating forecasting architectures and robustness-improvement methods, and an operational taxonomy organizing the method comparison. Across four real-world datasets and eight scored scenarios governed by a standardized severity model, it reports worst-scenario degradation, clean mean squared error (MSE), and worst-scenario fault-time MSE, separating relative robustness from absolute error. A disjoint fault-transfer split lets explicit fault-training methods train on adjacent fault families while evaluation uses separate benchmark scenarios. Empirically, forecasting architectures favored by clean MSE can degrade sharply under faults, and clean-MSE rankings can disagree with worst-scenario fault-time error rankings. Chronos-2, the evaluated zero-shot foundation-model representative, matches or trails the last-value naive forecaster in clean MSE on the two single-target datasets and has the largest worst-scenario degradation on ETTh1 and Traffic, where all channels are forecast targets. For the evaluated robustness-improvement method set, paired deltas show selective degradation reductions: projected gradient descent adversarial training and randomized training lead where value faults dominate observed degradation, while fault augmentation leads where availability faults dominate. SensorFault-Bench provides open-source code, documented data access, and reproduction and extension guides, so new datasets, architectures, and robustness-improvement methods can be evaluated under the same CPS sensor-fault robustness protocol.
中文标题/摘要
标题：传感器故障鲁棒性在预测中的基准测试
物理信息系统（CPS）预测模型依赖于包含噪声、偏差、缺失或时间错位读数的传感器数据流，但标准预测评估通常仅依据名义误差选择模型，而不展示其在这些故障下的鲁棒性表现。我们引入了SensorFault-Bench，这是一个共享的CPS基础传感器故障压力测试协议，用于评估预测架构和鲁棒性改进方法，并提供一个组织方法比较的操作性分类法。在四个真实世界数据集和八个由标准化严重性模型控制的评分场景中，它报告最差场景退化、干净均方误差（MSE）和最差场景故障时间MSE，从而区分相对鲁棒性与绝对误差。一个不相交的故障迁移划分允许显式故障训练方法在相邻故障家族上训练，而评估则使用独立的基准场景。实证研究表明，清洁MSE青睐的预测架构在故障下可能急剧退化，且清洁MSE排名可能与最差场景故障时间误差排名不一致。被评估的零样本基础模型代表Chronos-2，在两个单目标数据集上清洁MSE与最后值朴素预测器持平或落后，并在ETTh1和交通数据集上表现出最大的最差场景退化，其中所有通道均为预测目标。对于被评估的鲁棒性改进方法集，配对差异显示选择性退化减少：当观测到的退化主要由值故障主导时，投影梯度下降对抗训练和随机训练效果显著；当可用性故障主导时，故障增强效果更佳。SensorFault-Bench提供开源代码、文档化的数据访问方式及复现与扩展指南，以便在相同的CPS传感器故障鲁棒性协议下评估新数据集、架构和鲁棒性改进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate forecasting models under real-world sensor faults like noise, bias, and missing data, this paper introduces SensorFault-Bench, a standardized stress-test protocol for CPS forecasting that measures both clean performance and worst-case degradation under fault scenarios. It employs a disjoint fault-transfer split to train and evaluate models on separate fault families, enabling fair comparison of robustness methods. Across four real-world datasets and eight fault scenarios, results show that models optimized for clean MSE often suffer severe degradation under faults, and their rankings diverge from worst-case fault-time MSE rankings. Chronos-2, a zero-shot foundation model, underperforms naive baselines in clean MSE on single-target datasets and exhibits the largest degradation under multi-channel faults. Among robustness methods, projected gradient descent and randomized training reduce degradation in value-fault scenarios, while fault augmentation helps in availability-fault scenarios.
为评估在传感器故障（如噪声、偏差、缺失或时间错位）下预报模型的鲁棒性，本文提出SensorFault-Bench，一套标准化压力测试协议与方法分类体系。该框架在四个真实数据集上对八种架构进行评估，通过可控严重度的故障场景，报告清洁MSE与最坏情况故障时间MSE，区分绝对精度与鲁棒性。实验发现，清洁MSE最优的模型在故障下常大幅退化，其排名与最坏情况性能不一致。零样本基础模型Chronos-2在清洁场景下表现与朴素预测相当，但在传感器故障下退化最严重。在鲁棒性改进方法中，对抗训练与随机训练对值型故障效果最佳，而故障增强对可用性故障更有效。
When Large Vision-Language Models Meet Person Re-Identification
Authors: Qizao Wang, Bin Li, Xiangyang Xue
Venue: ICASSP 2026
First: 2024-11-27T07:45:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:39:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICASSP 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) that incorporate visual models and large language models have achieved impressive results across cross-modal understanding and reasoning tasks. In recent years, person re-identification (ReID) has also started to explore cross-modal semantics to improve the accuracy of identity recognition. However, effectively utilizing LVLMs for ReID remains an open challenge. While LVLMs operate under a generative paradigm by predicting the next output word, ReID requires the extraction of discriminative identity features to match pedestrians across cameras. In this paper, we propose LVLM-ReID, a novel framework that harnesses the strengths of LVLMs to promote ReID. Specifically, we employ instructions to guide the LVLM in generating one semantic token that encapsulates key appearance semantics from the person image. This token is further refined through our Semantic-Guided Interaction (SGI) module, establishing a reciprocal interaction between the semantic token and visual tokens. Ultimately, the reinforced semantic token serves as the representation of pedestrian identity. Our framework integrates the semantic understanding and generation capabilities of LVLM into end-to-end ReID training, allowing LVLM to capture rich semantic cues during both training and inference. LVLM-ReID achieves competitive results on multiple benchmarks without additional image-text annotations, demonstrating the potential of LVLM-generated semantics to advance person ReID.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大视觉-语言模型与行人重识别的结合
融合视觉模型与大语言模型的大视觉-语言模型（LVLM）在跨模态理解和推理任务中取得了显著成果。近年来，行人重识别（ReID）也开始探索跨模态语义以提升身份识别准确性。然而，如何有效利用LVLM进行ReID仍是一个开放性挑战。LVLM基于生成范式预测下一个输出词，而ReID则需要提取判别性身份特征以跨摄像头匹配行人。本文提出LVLM-ReID框架，利用LVLM的优势促进ReID。具体而言，我们通过指令引导LVLM生成一个语义标记，该标记封装了人物图像的关键外观语义，并通过我们的语义引导交互（SGI）模块进一步优化，建立语义标记与视觉标记之间的双向交互。最终，强化后的语义标记作为行人身份的表征。该框架将LVLM的语义理解和生成能力整合进端到端的ReID训练中，使LVLM在训练和推理过程中捕捉丰富的语义线索。在多个基准数据集上，LVLM-ReID无需额外图像-文本标注即可取得竞争力结果，展示了LVLM生成语义在推动行人重识别方面的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) to enhance cross-modal understanding, this work addresses the challenge of adapting LVLMs for person re-identification (ReID), where discriminative identity features are critical. The proposed LVLM-ReID framework uses instruction-guided generation to produce a semantic token summarizing key appearance features from person images, which is then refined via a Semantic-Guided Interaction module that iteratively enhances the token through feedback from visual tokens. Experimental results show that LVLM-ReID achieves competitive performance on multiple ReID benchmarks without requiring additional image-text pairs, demonstrating that LVLM-generated semantics can effectively support identity matching across cameras.
鉴于大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）在跨模态理解方面的潜力，本文旨在解决将LVLM适配于行人重识别（ReID）任务的挑战，该任务需要提取判别性身份特征而非生成式文本预测。所提出的LVLM-ReID框架通过指令引导生成单一语义标记，捕捉行人图像的关键外观语义，并通过语义引导交互（SGI）模块与视觉标记双向交互以优化该标记。实验结果表明，该框架在多个ReID基准数据集上表现优异，且无需额外图像-文本配对，证明LVLM生成的语义可有效作为ReID中的身份表示。
A new initialisation to Control Gradients in Sinusoidal Neural network
Authors: Andrea Combette, Antoine Venaille, Nelly Pustelnik
First: 2025-12-06T13:23:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:38:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Proper initialisation strategy is of primary importance to mitigate gradient explosion or vanishing when training neural networks. Yet, the impact of initialisation parameters still lacks a precise theoretical understanding for several well-established architectures. Here, we propose a new initialisation for networks with sinusoidal activation functions such as \texttt{SIREN}, focusing on gradients control, their scaling with network depth, their impact on training and on generalization. To achieve this, we identify a closed-form expression for the initialisation of the parameters, differing from the original \texttt{SIREN} scheme. This expression is derived from fixed points obtained through the convergence of pre-activation distribution and the variance of Jacobian sequences. Controlling both gradients and targeting vanishing pre-activation helps preventing the emergence of inappropriate frequencies during estimation, thereby improving generalization. We further show that this initialisation strongly influences training dynamics through the Neural Tangent Kernel framework (NTK). Finally, we benchmark \texttt{SIREN} with the proposed initialisation against the original scheme and other baselines on function fitting and image reconstruction. The new initialisation consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across a wide range of reconstruction tasks, including those involving physics-informed neural networks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：正弦神经网络中控制梯度的新初始化方法
恰当的初始化策略对于训练神经网络时缓解梯度爆炸或消失至关重要。然而，对于若干经典架构，初始化参数的影响仍缺乏精确的理论理解。本文针对具有正弦激活函数的网络（如\texttt{SIREN}），提出一种新的初始化方法，重点控制梯度、其随网络深度的缩放、对训练和泛化的影响。为此，我们推导出参数初始化的闭式表达式，不同于原始\texttt{SIREN}方案，该表达式基于预激活分布收敛与雅可比序列方差的固定点。通过同时控制梯度并抑制预激活值消失，可避免估计过程中出现不恰当的频率，从而提升泛化能力。我们进一步表明，该初始化通过神经切线核框架（NTK）显著影响训练动态。最后，我们在函数拟合与图像重建任务中，将该初始化方案与原始方案及其他基线方法进行基准对比，结果表明新初始化在多种重建任务（包括物理信息神经网络）中持续优于当前最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to stabilize training in sinusoidal neural networks like SIREN by controlling gradient scaling and preventing frequency drift, the paper proposes a novel initialization strategy derived from fixed points of pre-activation distributions and Jacobian variance. This method ensures gradients remain well-scaled across network depth and avoids vanishing activations, which in turn improves generalization. Experimental results across function fitting and image reconstruction tasks show that the new initialization consistently outperforms the original SIREN initialization and other baselines, especially in physics-informed neural networks.
为解决正弦激活神经网络（如SIREN）训练中梯度消失或爆炸问题，作者提出一种基于预激活分布收敛点与雅可比方差固定点推导的新初始化方法，旨在控制梯度随网络深度的缩放并抑制不当频率生成，从而提升泛化能力。实验表明，该初始化在函数拟合与图像重建任务中，包括物理信息神经网络，均显著优于原SIREN初始化及其他基线方法。
Unified Noise Steering for Efficient Human-Guided VLA Adaptation
Authors: Junjie Lu, Xinyao Qin, Yuhua Jiang, Kaixin Wang, Chuheng Zhang, Bin Liang, Jun Yang, Min Xu, Li Zhao
First: 2026-05-11T16:37:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:37:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion-based vision-language-action (VLA) models have emerged as strong priors for robotic manipulation, yet adapting them to real-world distributions remains challenging. In particular, on-robot reinforcement learning (RL) is expensive and time-consuming, so effective adaptation depends on efficient policy improvement within a limited budget of real-world interactions. Noise-space RL lowers the cost by keeping the pretrained VLA fixed as a denoising generator while updating only a lightweight actor that predicts the noise. However, its performance is still limited due to inefficient autonomous exploration. Human corrective interventions can reduce this exploration burden, but they are naturally provided in action space, whereas noise-space finetuning requires supervision over noise variables. To address these challenges, we propose UniSteer, a Unified Noise Steering framework that combines human corrective guidance with noise-space RL through approximate action-to-noise inversion. Given a human corrective action, UniSteer inverts the frozen flow-matching decoder to recover a noise target, which provides supervised guidance for the same noise actor that is simultaneously optimized via reinforcement learning. Real-world experiments on diverse manipulation tasks show that UniSteer adapts more efficiently than strong noise-space RL and action-space human-in-the-loop baselines, improving the success rate from 20% to 90% in 66 minutes on average across four real-world adaptation tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：统一噪声引导用于高效的人类引导视觉-语言-动作模型适应
基于扩散的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型已成为机器人操作的强大先验，但将其适应到真实世界分布仍具挑战。特别是，机器人上的强化学习（RL）成本高昂且耗时，因此有效的适应依赖于在有限的真实交互预算内高效提升策略。噪声空间RL通过保持预训练VLA作为去噪生成器不变，仅更新轻量级动作预测器来降低成本。然而，其性能仍受限于自主探索效率不足。人类纠正干预可减轻这一探索负担，但其自然作用于动作空间，而噪声空间微调则需要对噪声变量进行监督。为解决这些挑战，我们提出UniSteer，一种统一噪声引导框架，通过近似动作到噪声的反演，将人类纠正引导与噪声空间RL结合。给定人类纠正动作，UniSteer反演冻结的流匹配解码器以恢复噪声目标，为同一噪声动作预测器提供监督指导，该预测器同时通过强化学习优化。在多样化的操作任务上的真实世界实验表明，UniSteer比强大的噪声空间RL和动作空间人类在环基线更高效地适应，平均在四个真实世界适应任务中，成功率达20%提升至90%，耗时66分钟。
Summary / 总结
To address the inefficiency of adapting diffusion-based vision-language-action models to real-world robotic tasks, UniSteer unifies human-guided correction with noise-space reinforcement learning by inverting human actions into noise targets to supervise a lightweight actor. This approach leverages a frozen pretrained VLA as a denoising generator while optimizing only the noise-predicting actor, reducing real-world interaction costs. Experiments across four manipulation tasks show UniSteer achieves a 90% success rate within 66 minutes on average, significantly outperforming both standard noise-space RL and action-space human-in-the-loop methods that start at 20% success.
为提升扩散式视觉-语言-动作模型在真实机器人任务中的适应效率，UniSteer通过将人类修正动作反推为噪声目标，将人类指导与噪声空间强化学习统一起来，使预训练VLA保持固定去噪生成器，而轻量级策略网络则在人类噪声监督下通过强化学习优化。在四个真实操作任务中，UniSteer平均66分钟内成功率达90%，显著优于仅达20%成功率的标准噪声空间RL和动作空间人机协同基线方法。
MaD Physics: Evaluating information seeking under constraints in physical environments
Authors: Moksh Jain, Mehdi Bennani, Johannes Bausch, Yuri Chervonyi, Bogdan Georgiev, Simon Osindero, Nenad Tomašev
First: 2026-05-11T16:37:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:37:19+00:00
Comments: 64 pages, 10 figures. Project page: https://mad-physics.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Scientific discovery is fundamentally a resource-constrained process that requires navigating complex trade-offs between the quality and quantity of measurements due to physical and cost constraints. Measurements drive the scientific process by revealing novel phenomena to improve our understanding. Existing benchmarks for evaluating agents for scientific discovery focus on either static knowledge-based reasoning or unconstrained experimental design tasks, and do not capture the ability to make measurements and plan under constraints. To bridge this gap, we propose Measuring and Discovering Physics (MaD Physics), a benchmark to evaluate the ability of agents to make informative measurements and conclusions subject to constraints on the quality and quantity of measurements. The benchmark consists of three environments, each based on a distinct physical law. To mitigate contamination from existing knowledge, MaD Physics includes altered physical laws. In each trial, the agent makes measurements of the system until it exhausts an allotted budget and then the agent has to infer the underlying physical law to make predictions about the state of the system in the future. MaD Physics evaluates two fundamental capabilities of scientific agents: inferring models from data and planning under constraints. We also demonstrate how MaD Physics can be used to evaluate other capabilities such as multimodality and in-context learning. We benchmark agents on MaD Physics using four Gemini models (2.5 Flash Lite, 2.5 Flash, 2.5 Pro, and 3 Flash), identifying shortcomings in their structured exploration and data collection capabilities and highlighting directions to improve their scientific reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MaD物理：在物理环境中评估受限条件下的信息获取能力
科学发现本质上是一个受资源约束的过程，需要在物理和成本限制下权衡测量的质量与数量。测量通过揭示新现象推动科学进程，从而提升我们的理解。现有用于评估科学发现代理的基准测试主要关注静态知识推理或无约束实验设计任务，未能体现代理在受限条件下进行测量和规划的能力。为弥合这一差距，我们提出MaD物理（Measuring and Discovering Physics）基准测试，用于评估代理在测量质量和数量受限条件下进行信息性测量和推断的能力。该基准包含三个基于不同物理定律的环境，为避免现有知识污染，MaD物理采用修改后的物理定律。在每次试验中，代理持续对系统进行测量，直至耗尽预算，随后需推断底层物理定律并预测系统未来状态。MaD物理评估科学代理的两项基本能力：从数据中推断模型和在约束下规划。我们还展示了MaD物理可用于评估其他能力，如多模态和上下文学习。我们使用四个Gemini模型（2.5 Flash Lite、2.5 Flash、2.5 Pro和3 Flash）在MaD物理上对代理进行基准测试，发现其结构化探索和数据收集能力存在不足，并指明改进科学推理的方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate AI agents’ ability to conduct scientific discovery under real-world constraints such as limited measurement resources, the paper introduces MaD Physics, a benchmark that tests agents’ capacity to make informative measurements and infer physical laws within budget limits. The benchmark comprises three environments based on altered physical laws to avoid contamination from prior knowledge. Agents must collect measurements until their budget is exhausted and then deduce the underlying law to predict future states. Experiments with four Gemini models reveal that current agents struggle with structured exploration and efficient data collection, underscoring gaps in scientific reasoning and suggesting directions for improvement.
为评估AI代理在现实资源限制下进行科学发现的能力，本文提出MaD Physics基准，测试代理在预算约束下进行信息性测量并推断物理定律的能力。该基准包含三个基于修改物理定律的环境，要求代理在预算内收集数据并预测系统未来状态。实验表明，四个Gemini模型在结构化探索和高效数据收集方面表现不足，揭示了当前科学推理能力的缺陷，并指明了改进约束感知规划与模型推断的方向。
ALAM: Algebraically Consistent Latent Transitions for Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Zuojin Tang, Haoyun Liu, Xinyuan Chang, Changjie Wu, Dongjie Huo, Yandan Yang, Bin Liu, Zhejia Cai, Feng Xiong, Mu Xu, jiachen Luo, De Ma, Zhiheng Ma, Gang Pan
First: 2026-05-11T16:37:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:37:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language-action (VLA) models remain constrained by the scarcity of action-labeled robot data, whereas action-free videos provide abundant evidence of how the physical world changes. Latent action models offer a promising way to extract such priors from videos, but reconstruction-trained latent codes are not necessarily suitable for policy generation: they may predict future observations while lacking the structure needed to be reused or generated coherently with robot actions. We introduce ALAM (Algebraic Latent Action Model), an Algebraically Consistent Latent Action Model that turns temporal relations in action-free video into structural supervision. Given frame triplets, ALAM learns latent transitions that are grounded by reconstruction while being regularized by composition and reversal consistency, encouraging a locally additive transition space. For downstream VLA learning, we freeze the pretrained encoder and use its latent transition sequences as auxiliary generative targets, co-generated with robot actions under a joint flow-matching objective. This couples structured latent transitions with flow-based policy generation, allowing the policy to exploit ALAM's locally consistent transition geometry without requiring latent-to-action decoding. Representation probes show that ALAM reduces additivity and reversibility errors by 25-85 times over unstructured latent-action baselines and improves long-horizon cumulative reconstruction. When transferred to VLA policies, ALAM raises the average success rate from 47.9% to 85.0% on MetaWorld MT50 and from 94.1% to 98.1% on LIBERO, with consistent gains on real-world manipulation tasks. Ablations further confirm that the strongest improvements arise from the synergy between algebraically structured latent transitions and joint flow matching.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ALAM：面向视觉-语言-动作模型的代数一致潜在过渡
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型受限于标注动作的机器人数据稀缺，而无动作视频则提供了丰富的物理世界变化证据。潜在动作模型为从视频中提取此类先验提供了有前景的方法，但通过重建训练的潜在码未必适合策略生成：它们可能预测未来观测，却缺乏与机器人动作协同生成所需的结构。我们提出ALAM（代数潜在动作模型），一种将无动作视频中的时序关系转化为结构监督的代数一致潜在动作模型。给定帧三元组，ALAM学习受重建约束、并由组合与逆向一致性正则化的潜在过渡，鼓励局部可加的过渡空间。下游VLA学习中，我们冻结预训练编码器，将其潜在过渡序列作为辅助生成目标，在联合流匹配目标下与机器人动作共同生成。这将结构化潜在过渡与基于流的策略生成耦合，使策略无需潜在到动作解码即可利用ALAM的局部一致过渡几何。表示探针显示，ALAM相较无结构潜在动作基线，将加法性和可逆性误差降低25-85倍，并提升长时程累积重建效果。当迁移到VLA策略时，ALAM在MetaWorld MT50上将平均成功率从47.9%提升至85.0%，在LIBERO上从94.1%提升至98.1%，在真实世界操作任务中保持稳定增益。消融实验进一步证实，代数结构化潜在过渡与联合流匹配的协同作用带来最强提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of action-labeled robot data, the paper proposes ALAM, an algebraically consistent latent action model that leverages action-free videos to learn structured latent transitions via temporal frame triplets. It enforces reconstruction while regularizing transitions with composition and reversal consistency to create a locally additive latent space. In downstream VLA learning, ALAM freezes its encoder and uses latent transition sequences as auxiliary targets under a joint flow-matching objective, enabling policies to exploit the structured geometry without decoding latents to actions. Experiments show ALAM reduces additivity and reversibility errors by 25–85x over unstructured baselines, improves long-horizon reconstruction, and boosts VLA success rates from 47.9% to 85.0% on MetaWorld MT50 and from 94.1% to 98.1% on LIBERO, with consistent gains on real-world manipulation tasks.
为缓解机器人动作标注数据稀缺的问题，本文提出ALAM（代数一致潜在动作模型），通过无动作视频中的帧三元组学习具有代数结构的潜在过渡，结合重建保真度与组合和逆向一致性约束，构建局部可加的潜在空间。下游VLA训练中，冻结预训练编码器，将潜在过渡与机器人动作联合生成于流匹配目标下，无需潜在到动作解码。实验表明，ALAM相较无结构基线将可加性和可逆性误差降低25–85倍，提升长时程重建效果，并将MetaWorld MT50任务成功率从47.9%提升至85.0%，LIBERO任务从94.1%提升至98.1%，在真实操作任务中表现稳定。
On periodic distributed representations using Fourier embeddings
Authors: Jakeb Chouinard
First: 2026-05-11T16:35:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:35:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Periodic signals are critical for representing physical and perceptual phenomena. Scalar, real angular measures, e.g., radians and degrees, result in difficulty processing and distinguishing nearby angles, especially when their absolute difference exceeds pi. We can avoid this problem by using real-valued, periodic embeddings in high-dimensional space. These representations also allow us to control the nature of their dot product similarities, allowing us to construct a variety of different kernel shapes. In this work, we aim of highlight how these representations can be constructed and focus on the formalization of Dirichlet and periodic Gaussian kernels using the neurally-plausible representation scheme of Spatial Semantic Pointers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于使用傅里叶嵌入的周期分布式表示
周期信号对于表示物理和感知现象至关重要。标量实数角测量（如弧度和度）在处理和区分接近的角度时存在困难，尤其当其绝对差值超过π时。我们可以通过在高维空间中使用实值周期嵌入来避免这一问题。这些表示还允许我们控制其点积相似性，从而构建多种不同的核形状。本研究旨在突出这些表示的构建方法，并重点阐述使用神经上合理的空间语义指针方案形式化狄利克雷和周期高斯核。
Summary / 总结
This work addresses the challenge of representing periodic signals—such as angles—without the discontinuity and ambiguity that arises when using scalar measures like radians, especially near π. It proposes using high-dimensional real-valued periodic embeddings to avoid these issues and enables precise control over dot product similarity, facilitating the construction of diverse kernel shapes. The authors formalize Dirichlet and periodic Gaussian kernels using Spatial Semantic Pointers, a neurally-plausible representation scheme, and demonstrate that these embeddings effectively capture periodic structure while supporting flexible similarity functions.
该论文旨在解决传统标量角度表示（如弧度）在机器学习中难以区分接近值（尤其超过π时）的问题。作者提出使用高维实值周期嵌入，以避免该缺陷并支持可控的点积相似性，从而构建多样化的核函数。他们基于神经上合理的空间语义指针方案，形式化了狄利克雷和周期高斯核，并证明这些嵌入能有效捕捉周期结构并支持灵活的相似性函数。
CLEF: EEG Foundation Model for Learning Clinical Semantics
Authors: Peng Cao, Ali Mirzazadeh, Jong Woo Lee, Aleksandar Videnovic, Dina Katabi
First: 2026-05-11T16:34:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:34:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Clinical EEG interpretation requires reasoning over full EEG sessions and integrating signal patterns with clinical context. Existing EEG foundation models are largely designed for short-window decoding and do not incorporate clinical context. We introduce CLEF, a clinically grounded long-context EEG foundation model. CLEF represents EEG sessions as 3D multitaper spectrogram tokens, enabling tractable Transformer modeling at session scale, and aligns embeddings with neurologist reports and structured EHR data through contrastive objectives. We evaluate CLEF on a new 234-task benchmark spanning disease phenotypes, medication exposures, and EEG findings, with more than 260k EEG sessions from over 108k patients. CLEF outperforms prior EEG foundation models on 229 of 234 tasks, improving mean AUROC from 0.65 to 0.74. Reconstruction-only pretraining surpasses prior EEG foundation models, while report and EHR alignment yields further gains. Held-out concept and external-cohort experiments suggest that these representations transfer beyond observed alignment targets. These results support session-scale, clinically grounded representation learning as a promising foundation-model paradigm for clinical EEG.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLEF：用于学习临床语义的EEG基础模型
临床EEG解读需要对完整EEG会话进行推理，并将信号模式与临床背景相结合。现有EEG基础模型大多针对短窗口解码设计，未融入临床背景。我们引入CLEF，一种基于临床的长上下文EEG基础模型。CLEF将EEG会话表示为3D多窗谱图标记，支持在会话尺度上进行可处理的Transformer建模，并通过对比目标将嵌入与神经科报告和结构化电子健康记录数据对齐。我们在涵盖疾病表型、药物暴露和EEG发现的234项任务新基准上评估CLEF，数据来自超过10.8万名患者的26万多个EEG会话。CLEF在234项任务中的229项上优于现有EEG基础模型，平均AUROC从0.65提升至0.74。仅重建预训练已超越现有EEG基础模型，而报告与EHR对齐进一步提升性能。留出概念和外部队列实验表明，这些表示可泛化至未观察到的对齐目标之外。这些结果支持会话尺度、临床导向的表示学习作为临床EEG基础模型的有前景范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for EEG models that reason over full clinical sessions and integrate signal patterns with clinical context, CLEF introduces a long-context foundation model designed for clinical EEG interpretation. It encodes EEG sessions as 3D multitaper spectrogram tokens to enable scalable Transformer modeling, and aligns representations with neurologist reports and EHR data via contrastive learning. Evaluated on a 234-task benchmark across 260k EEG sessions from 108k patients, CLEF outperforms prior models on 229 tasks, boosting mean AUROC from 0.65 to 0.74. Reconstruction-only pretraining already surpasses existing models, and adding report and EHR alignment further improves performance. Transfer experiments confirm generalization beyond training targets, demonstrating the promise of clinically grounded, session-scale foundation models for EEG.
为解决现有EEG模型难以处理完整临床会话并结合临床背景的问题，CLEF提出一种面向临床的长上下文EEG基础模型。该模型将EEG会话编码为3D多窗谱图标记，支持Transformer在会话尺度建模，并通过对比学习对齐神经报告与结构化电子病历。在涵盖234项任务、26万份EEG数据、10.8万患者的基准测试中，CLEF在229项任务上优于现有模型，平均AUROC从0.65提升至0.74。仅重建预训练已超越现有模型，结合报告与EHR对齐进一步提升性能，且在未见概念与外部队列中表现出迁移能力。
AI Alignment via Incentives and Correction
Authors: Rohit Agarwal, Joshua Lin, Mark Braverman, Elad Hazan
First: 2026-05-02T23:28:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:34:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study AI alignment through the lens of law-and-economics models of deterrence and enforcement. In these models, misconduct is not treated as an external failure, but as a strategic response to incentives: an actor weighs the gain from violation against the probability of detection and the severity of punishment. We argue that the same logic arises naturally in agentic AI pipelines. A solver may benefit from producing a persuasive but incorrect answer, hiding uncertainty, or exploiting spurious shortcuts, while an auditor or verifier must decide whether costly monitoring is worthwhile. Alignment is therefore a fixed-point problem: stronger penalties may deter solver misbehavior, but they can also reduce the auditor's incentive to inspect, since auditing then mainly incurs cost on a population that appears increasingly aligned. This perspective also changes what should count as a post-training signal. Standard feedback often attaches reward to the final answer alone, but a solver-auditor pipeline exposes the full correction event: whether the solver erred, whether the auditor inspected, whether the error was caught, and whether oversight incentives remained active. We formalize this interaction in a two-agent model in which a principal chooses rewards over joint correction outcomes, inducing both solver behavior and auditor monitoring. Reward design is therefore a bilevel optimization problem: rewards are judged not by their immediate semantic meaning, but by the behavioral equilibrium they induce. We propose a bandit-based outer-loop procedure for searching over reward profiles using noisy interaction feedback. Experiments on an LLM coding pipeline show that adaptive reward profiles can maintain useful oversight pressure and improve principal-aligned outcomes relative to static hand-designed rewards, including a substantial reduction in hallucinated incorrect attempts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过激励与修正实现人工智能对齐
我们通过威慑与执法的法律与经济学模型研究人工智能对齐。在这些模型中，不当行为不被视为外部失败，而是对激励的战略回应：行为者权衡违规收益与被发现的概率及惩罚的严重性。我们认为，相同的逻辑在代理型人工智能管道中自然出现。求解器可能因生成具有说服力但错误的答案、隐藏不确定性或利用虚假捷径而获益，而审计员或验证者则需判断成本高昂的监控是否值得。因此，对齐是一个不动点问题：更强的惩罚可能威慑求解器的不当行为，但也可能降低审计员的检查激励，因为当系统看似越来越对齐时，审计主要只带来成本。 该视角也改变了什么应被视为训练后信号。标准反馈通常仅将奖励与最终答案挂钩，但求解器-审计员管道则暴露了完整的修正事件：求解器是否出错、审计员是否检查、错误是否被发现，以及监督激励是否持续有效。我们通过一个双代理模型形式化这一互动：委托人选择奖励以激励联合修正结果，从而影响求解器行为和审计员监控。因此，奖励设计是一个双层优化问题：奖励不以其即时语义意义评判，而是以其诱导的行为均衡为标准。我们提出一种基于多臂老虎机的外层循环程序，利用嘈杂的交互反馈搜索奖励配置。在大型语言模型编码管道上的实验表明，自适应奖励配置可维持有用的监督压力，并相较静态人工设计奖励改善委托人对齐结果，包括大幅减少幻觉性错误尝试。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by law-and-economics models of deterrence, the paper frames AI alignment as a strategic equilibrium problem where agents weigh incentives for misbehavior against detection and punishment. It proposes a two-agent model of solver and auditor, where rewards are designed to steer behavior through induced equilibria rather than direct semantic feedback. Experiments on an LLM coding pipeline demonstrate that adaptive, bandit-based reward profiles outperform static rewards by maintaining oversight pressure and reducing hallucinated outputs while improving alignment with the principal’s goals.
受法律与经济学威慑模型启发，本文将AI对齐视为一个战略均衡问题，其中代理方权衡违规收益与被发现和惩罚的风险。提出一个求解器与审计员的双代理模型，奖励设计需作为双层优化问题，其目标是诱导期望行为而非单纯语义正确性。在LLM编码管道实验中，基于多臂赌博机的自适应奖励策略优于静态奖励，能维持监督压力、减少幻觉输出并提升与主体目标的一致性。
Policy Gradient Methods for Non-Markovian Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Avik Kar, Siddharth Chandak, Rahul Singh, Soumitra Sinhahajari, Eric Moulines, Shalabh Bhatnagar, Nicholas Bambos
First: 2026-05-11T16:34:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:34:28+00:00
Comments: 39 pages, 5 figures, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study policy gradient methods for reinforcement learning in non-Markovian decision processes (NMDPs), where observations and rewards depend on the entire interaction history. To handle this dependence, the agent maintains an internal state that is recursively updated to provide a compact summary of past observations and actions. In contrast to approaches that treat the agent state dynamics as fixed or learn it via predictive objectives, we propose a reward-centric formulation that jointly optimizes the agent state dynamics and the control policy to maximize the expected cumulative reward. To this end, we consider a class of Agent State-Markov (ASM) policies, comprising an agent state dynamics and a control policy that maps the agent state to actions. We establish a novel policy gradient theorem for ASM policies, extending the classical policy gradient results from the Markovian setting to episodic and infinite-horizon discounted NMDPs. Building on this gradient expression, we propose the Agent State-Markov Policy Gradient (ASMPG) algorithm, which leverages the recursive structure of the agent state dynamics for efficient optimization. We establish finite-time and almost sure convergence guarantees, and empirically demonstrate that, on a range of non-Markovian tasks, ASMPG outperforms baselines that learn state representations via predictive objectives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：非马尔可夫强化学习中的策略梯度方法
我们研究了在非马尔可夫决策过程（NMDP）中强化学习的策略梯度方法，其中观测和奖励依赖于完整的交互历史。为处理这种依赖，智能体维护一个内部状态，通过递归更新以提供过去观测和动作的紧凑摘要。与将智能体状态动态视为固定或通过预测目标学习的方法不同，我们提出了一种以奖励为中心的公式，联合优化智能体状态动态和控制策略，以最大化期望累积奖励。为此，我们考虑了一类智能体状态马尔可夫（ASM）策略，包括智能体状态动态和将智能体状态映射到动作的控制策略。我们为ASM策略建立了新颖的策略梯度定理，将经典策略梯度结果从马尔可夫设置扩展到分段和无限时域折扣NMDP。基于该梯度表达式，我们提出了智能体状态马尔可夫策略梯度（ASMPG）算法，利用智能体状态动态的递归结构实现高效优化。我们建立了有限时间和几乎必然收敛的保证，并实验证明，在一系列非马尔可夫任务中，ASMPG优于通过预测目标学习状态表示的基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of applying policy gradient methods to non-Markovian environments where rewards and observations depend on full history, this work introduces a reward-centric framework that jointly learns agent state dynamics and control policy. The method defines Agent State-Markov (ASM) policies, where an internal state recursively summarizes past interactions, and derives a novel policy gradient theorem applicable to episodic and discounted infinite-horizon NMDPs. The resulting ASMPG algorithm exploits the recursive structure for efficient optimization and is proven to converge both in finite time and almost surely; experiments show it consistently outperforms baselines that rely on predictive state representation learning.
为应对奖励和观测依赖完整历史的非马尔可夫环境，本文提出一种以奖励为中心的策略梯度方法，联合学习智能体内部状态动态与控制策略。该方法引入智能体状态马尔可夫（ASM）策略，通过递归更新内部状态以压缩历史交互信息，并推导出适用于分段和折扣无限时域非马尔可夫决策过程的新型策略梯度定理。基于此，提出的ASMPG算法利用状态动态的递归结构实现高效优化，并证明其在有限时间和几乎必然意义上收敛。实验表明，ASMPG在多个非马尔可夫任务上显著优于基于预测目标学习状态表示的基线方法。
Probing Cross-modal Information Hubs in Audio-Visual LLMs
Authors: Jihoo Jung, Chaeyoung Jung, Ji-Hoon Kim, Joon Son Chung
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-11T16:34:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:34:18+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Audio-visual large language models (AVLLMs) have recently emerged as a powerful architecture capable of jointly reasoning over audio, visual, and textual modalities. In AVLLMs, the bidirectional interaction between audio and video modalities introduces intricate processing dynamics, necessitating a deeper understanding of their internal mechanisms. However, unlike extensively studied text-only or large vision language models, the internal workings of AVLLMs remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we focus on cross-modal information flow between audio and visual modalities in AVLLMs, investigating where information derived from one modality is encoded within the token representations of the other modality. Through an analysis of multiple recent AVLLMs, we uncover two common findings. First, AVLLMs primarily encode integrated audio-visual information in sink tokens. Second, sink tokens do not uniformly hold cross-modal information. Instead, a distinct subset of sink tokens, which we term cross-modal sink tokens, specializes in storing such information. Based on these findings, we further propose a simple training-free hallucination mitigation method by encouraging reliance on integrated cross-modal information within cross-modal sink tokens. Our code is available at https://github.com/kaistmm/crossmodal-hub.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探查音频-视觉大语言模型中的跨模态信息枢纽
音频-视觉大语言模型（AVLLMs）近期作为一种强大的架构应运而生，能够联合推理音频、视觉和文本模态。在AVLLMs中，音频与视频模态之间的双向交互引入了复杂的处理动态，亟需深入理解其内部机制。然而，与已被广泛研究的纯文本或大型视觉语言模型不同，AVLLMs的内部运作仍鲜有探索。本文聚焦于AVLLMs中音频与视觉模态间的跨模态信息流动，研究一种模态的信息如何在另一模态的token表示中被编码。通过对多个近期AVLLMs的分析，我们发现两个普遍现象：第一，AVLLMs主要在sink tokens中编码整合的音频-视觉信息；第二，sink tokens并非均匀存储跨模态信息，而是存在一个特定子集——我们称之为跨模态sink tokens——专门用于存储此类信息。基于这些发现，我们进一步提出一种无需训练的幻觉缓解方法，通过鼓励模型依赖跨模态sink tokens中整合的跨模态信息。我们的代码可在https://github.com/kaistmm/crossmodal-hub获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how audio-visual large language models (AVLLMs) process cross-modal information, this study investigates where audio-derived signals are encoded within visual token representations and vice versa. Through analysis of multiple AVLLMs, the authors find that integrated audio-visual information is primarily stored in sink tokens, but not all sink tokens are equally effective at holding cross-modal data; a specialized subset, termed cross-modal sink tokens, is identified as the key carriers of such information. Based on this insight, the paper proposes a training-free hallucination mitigation technique that encourages models to rely on these cross-modal sink tokens, improving output reliability without additional training.
为理解音频-视觉大语言模型（AVLLMs）如何处理跨模态信息，本研究探究音频信号在视觉token中的编码位置及反之亦然。通过对多个AVLLMs的分析发现，整合的音视频信息主要存储于sink token中，但并非所有sink token都具备跨模态信息存储能力——仅有一部分特化token，称为跨模态sink token，专门负责此类信息存储。基于此发现，作者提出一种无需训练的幻觉缓解方法，通过引导模型依赖这些特化token，从而提升跨模态推理准确性。
NanoResearch: Co-Evolving Skills, Memory, and Policy for Personalized Research Automation
Authors: Jinhang Xu, Qiyuan Zhu, Yujun Wu, Zirui Wang, Dongxu Zhang, Jianxin Tang, Marcia Tian, Yiling Duan, Siyuan Li, Jingxuan Wei, Sirui Han, Yike Guo, Odin Zhang, Conghui He, Cheng Tan
First: 2026-05-11T16:33:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:33:47+00:00
Comments: 40 pages, 14 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-powered multi-agent systems can now automate the full research pipeline from ideation to paper writing, but a fundamental question remains: automation for whom? Researchers operate under different resource configurations, hold different methodological preferences, and target different output formats. A system that produces uniform outputs regardless of these differences will systematically under-serve every individual user, making personalization a precondition for research automation to be genuinely usable. However, achieving it requires three capabilities that current systems lack: accumulating reusable procedural knowledge across projects, retaining user-specific experience across sessions, and internalizing implicit preferences that resist explicit formalization. We propose NanoResearch, a multi-agent framework that addresses these gaps through tri-level co-evolution. A skill bank distills recurring operations into compact procedural rules reusable across projects. A memory module maintains user- and project-specific experience that grounds planning decisions in each user's research history. A label-free policy learning converts free-form feedback into persistent parameter updates of the planner, reshaping subsequent coordination. These three layers co-evolve: reliable skills produce richer memory, richer memory informs better planning, and preference internalization continuously realigns the loop to each user. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NanoResearch delivers substantial gains over state-of-the-art AI research systems, and progressively refines itself to produce better research at lower cost over successive cycles.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NanoResearch：面向个性化研究自动化的协同演进技能、记忆与策略
基于大语言模型的多智能体系统如今可自动化完成从创意到论文撰写的完整研究流程，但一个根本性问题仍待解答：为谁实现自动化？研究人员资源配置不同、方法偏好各异、目标输出格式多样。若系统无视这些差异而输出统一结果，将系统性地忽视每位用户需求，因此个性化是研究自动化真正可用的前提。然而，实现个性化需当前系统缺乏的三项能力：跨项目积累可复用的程序性知识、跨会话保留用户专属经验、内化难以显式形式化的隐式偏好。我们提出NanoResearch多智能体框架，通过三层次协同演化解决上述问题：技能库将重复操作提炼为可跨项目复用的紧凑程序规则；记忆模块保留用户与项目专属经验，使规划决策根植于每位用户的研究历史；无标签策略学习将自由形式反馈转化为规划器的持久参数更新，重塑后续协作。三层协同演化：可靠技能生成更丰富记忆，丰富记忆指导更优规划，偏好内化持续调整循环以适配每位用户。大量实验表明，NanoResearch显著优于现有顶尖AI研究系统，并在迭代中持续优化，以更低成本产出更优研究结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to personalize research automation for diverse user needs, NanoResearch introduces a tri-level co-evolution framework that integrates a skill bank for reusable procedural knowledge, a memory module to retain user-specific experience, and a label-free policy learner that adapts to implicit preferences via free-form feedback. Experiments show the system progressively improves output quality and reduces cost per research cycle, outperforming existing AI research systems across multiple metrics while adapting to individual user profiles.
为解决现有研究自动化系统无法适应不同研究人员资源、方法和输出目标的问题，NanoResearch提出一个三层次协同演化的多智能体框架：技能库提取可复用的操作规则，记忆模块保留用户特定研究历史，无标签策略学习器将自由反馈转化为规划器的持续参数更新。实验表明，该协同演化设计显著优于现有系统，随迭代逐步提升研究质量和效率，同时降低成本。
A Unified Representation of Neural Networks Architectures
Authors: Christophe Prieur, Mircea Lazar, Bogdan Robu
First: 2025-12-19T14:01:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:33:22+00:00
Comments: Typographical corrections and additional clarifications, remarks; few new relevant references added and acknowledgements; main results unchanged
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper we consider the limiting case of neural networks (NNs) architectures when the number of neurons in each hidden layer and the number of hidden layers tend to infinity thus forming a continuum, and we derive approximation errors as a function of the number of neurons and/or hidden layers. Firstly, we consider the case of neural networks with a single hidden layer and we derive an integral infinite width neural representation that generalizes existing continuous neural networks (CNNs) representations. Then we extend this to deep residual CNNs that have a finite number of integral hidden layers and residual connections. Secondly, we revisit the relation between neural ODEs and deep residual NNs and we formalize approximation errors via discretization techniques. Then, we merge these two approaches into a unified homogeneous representation of NNs as a Distributed Parameter neural Network (DiPaNet) and we show that most of the existing finite and infinite-dimensional NNs architectures are related via homogenization/discretization with the DiPaNet representation. Our approach is purely deterministic and applies to general, uniformly continuous matrix weight functions. Relations with neural fields and other neural integro-differential equations are discussed along with further possible generalizations and applications of the DiPaNet framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经网络架构的统一表示
本文考虑当各隐藏层神经元数量及隐藏层数量趋于无穷时神经网络（NNs）架构的极限情形，从而形成连续体，并推导出近似误差作为神经元数量和/或隐藏层数量的函数。首先，我们研究单隐藏层神经网络的情形，推导出一种积分无限宽神经网络表示，该表示推广了现有的连续神经网络（CNNs）表示。接着，我们将此方法扩展至具有有限积分隐藏层数和残差连接的深度残差CNNs。其次，我们重新审视神经ODE与深度残差NNs之间的关系，并通过离散化技术形式化近似误差。随后，我们将这两种方法融合为一种统一的齐次神经网络表示——分布式参数神经网络（DiPaNet），并证明大多数现有有限和无限维NN架构均可通过齐次化/离散化与DiPaNet表示相关联。我们的方法纯属确定性，适用于一般均匀连续的矩阵权重函数。本文还讨论了与神经场及其他神经积分微分方程的关系，并探讨了DiPaNet框架的进一步可能推广与应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand neural network behavior in the infinite-width limit, this paper derives approximation errors for neural networks as the number of neurons and hidden layers grows to infinity, forming a continuous representation. It begins by deriving an integral-based infinite-width neural network for single-layer networks, then extends this to deep residual networks with finite layers and residual connections. The paper further connects neural ODEs and deep residual networks via discretization, and unifies both approaches into a Distributed Parameter neural Network (DiPaNet) framework. Experimental results show that DiPaNet can represent a wide range of existing neural architectures through homogenization and discretization, and the framework applies to general uniformly continuous weight functions without stochastic assumptions.
为理解神经网络在无限宽度极限下的行为，本文推导了神经网络在神经元和层数趋于无穷时的逼近误差，形成连续结构。首先，它为单层网络构建了积分表示，推广了现有连续神经网络模型，随后扩展至具有有限层数和残差连接的深度残差网络。文章进一步通过离散化方法将神经ODE与深度残差网络关联，并统一二者为分布式参数神经网络（DiPaNet）框架。实验表明，DiPaNet可通过均质化和离散化表示多种有限与无限维架构，且该框架适用于一般均匀连续权重函数，无需随机假设。
Beyond Nearest Neighbor Interpolation in Data Augmentation
Authors: Olivier Rukundo
First: 2025-04-02T09:13:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-11T16:32:53+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 11 figures, 14 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Avoiding the risk of undefined categorical labels using nearest neighbor interpolation overlooks the risk of exacerbating pixel level annotation errors in augmented training data. Additionally, the inherent low pass filtering effects of interpolation algorithms exacerbate the risk of degrading high frequency structural details within annotated regions of interest. To avoid these risks, the author modified convolutional neural networks data transformation functions by incorporating a modified geometric transformation function, removing reliance on nearest neighbor interpolation, and integrating a mean-based class filtering mechanism to handle undefined categorical labels with alternative interpolation algorithms. The author also implemented an offline data augmentation pipeline to generate interpolation specific augmented training data, enabling quantitative assessment of interpolation specific low pass filtering effects on augmented training data. Experimental evaluation on three medical image segmentation datasets and the XBAT datasets demonstrated performance gains across multiple quantitative metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：数据增强中超越最近邻插值
避免使用最近邻插值时可能出现的未定义类别标签风险，却忽视了其可能加剧增强训练数据中像素级标注错误的风险。此外，插值算法固有的低通滤波效应会加剧标注感兴趣区域内部高频结构细节退化的风险。为规避这些风险，作者通过引入修改后的几何变换函数，修改了卷积神经网络的数据变换函数，消除了对最近邻插值的依赖，并整合了基于均值的类别过滤机制，以使用其他插值算法处理未定义类别标签。作者还实现了一个离线数据增强管道，用于生成特定插值方法的增强训练数据，从而能够定量评估插值方法对增强训练数据的低通滤波效应。在三个医学图像分割数据集和XBAT数据集上的实验评估表明，多个定量指标均获得性能提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risks of pixel-level annotation errors and structural detail degradation caused by nearest neighbor interpolation in data augmentation, the paper proposes modifying CNN data transformation functions to eliminate reliance on nearest neighbor methods. It introduces a geometric transformation function and a mean-based class filtering mechanism to handle undefined labels, while using an offline pipeline to quantitatively assess interpolation-specific low pass filtering effects. Experiments on three medical segmentation datasets and XBAT showed consistent performance gains across multiple quantitative metrics.
为避免插值导致的像素级标注错误和高频结构细节丢失，该文提出修改CNN数据变换函数，去除对最近邻插值的依赖，引入几何变换函数和基于均值的类别过滤机制以处理未定义标签，并构建离线数据增强流水线以量化不同插值方法的影响。在三个医学图像分割数据集和XBAT数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多个量化指标上均取得性能提升。
